--=====================================================================
--  Kira Hub :: Steal an Egg
--  Decoded — Part 1/9
--  Header + Metadata + Themes + UI Core
--=====================================================================

if not game:IsLoaded() then
    game.Loaded:Wait()
end

--=====================================================================
--  [1] Slot management (multi-instance safe unload)
--=====================================================================
do
    local str
    do
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not LocalPlayer then
            pcall(function() Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Wait() end)
            LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        end
        str = tostring(LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.UserId or 0)
    end

    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    local KiraHub = env.KiraHub
    if type(KiraHub) ~= "table" then
        KiraHub = { slots = {} }
        env.KiraHub = KiraHub
    end
    if type(KiraHub.slots) ~= "table" then
        KiraHub.slots = {}
    end

    local unload
    do
        local slot = KiraHub.slots[str]
        if type(slot) ~= "table" then
            slot = {}
            KiraHub.slots[str] = slot
        end

        slot.gen = (tonumber(slot.gen) or 0) + 1

        -- If no other alive slot → bump config generation
        local otherAlive = false
        for k, v in pairs(KiraHub.slots) do
            if str ~= tostring(k) and type(v) == "table" and v.alive == true then
                otherAlive = true
                break
            end
        end
        if not otherAlive then
            env.KiraCfgGen = (tonumber(env.KiraCfgGen) or 0) + 1
        end

        unload = slot.unload
        slot.unload = nil
        slot.alive = false
    end

    -- Call previous unload (either slot-specific or global)
    if type(unload) == "function" then
        pcall(unload)
    elseif type(env.KiraUnload) == "function" then
        local KiraUnloadUid = env.KiraUnloadUid
        local shouldRun = KiraUnloadUid == nil or str == tostring(KiraUnloadUid)

        if KiraUnloadUid == nil then
            for k, v in pairs(KiraHub.slots) do
                if str ~= tostring(k) and type(v) == "table" and type(v.unload) == "function" then
                    shouldRun = false
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        if shouldRun then
            local KiraUnload = env.KiraUnload
            if str == tostring(KiraUnloadUid or str) then
                env.KiraUnload = nil
                env.KiraUnloadUid = nil
            end
            pcall(KiraUnload)
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [2] Wait for local player's character to spawn
--=====================================================================
do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not LocalPlayer then
        pcall(function() Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Wait() end)
        LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    end

    local deadline = os.clock() + 60
    while LocalPlayer and deadline > os.clock() do
        local humanoid, root
        do
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            root     = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end

        if humanoid and root and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            task.wait(0.45)
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local h = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local r = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not h or not r or not (h.Health > 0) then
                continue
            end
            break
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end

local function sanitizeName(s)
    local out = tostring(s or "Game")
        :gsub("[<>:\"/\\|?*]", "_")
        :gsub("%s+", "_")
        :gsub("_+", "_")
        :match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    if not out or out == "" or out == "_" then out = "Game" end
    return out
end

META.LogoFile      = "Kira/logo.png"
META.LogoFileLight = "Kira/logo-light.png"

--=====================================================================
--  [5] Device detection (mobile / console)
--=====================================================================
local isMobile, phoneLocked, phoneRelayout
isMobile = false
phoneLocked = false      -- if user manually forced PhoneUI
phoneRelayout = nil      -- set later, used to reflow layout

local function detectMobile()
    if UserInputService.VREnabled then return false end

    local tenFoot = false
    pcall(function() tenFoot = GuiService:IsTenFootInterface() end)
    if tenFoot then return false end

    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then return true end
    if UserInputService.MouseEnabled == false then return true end

    local gyro, accel = false, false
    pcall(function() gyro  = UserInputService.GyroscopeEnabled == true end)
    pcall(function() accel = UserInputService.AccelerometerEnabled == true end)
    if gyro or accel then return true end

    local preferred, lastInput
    pcall(function() preferred = UserInputService.PreferredInput end)
    pcall(function() lastInput = UserInputService:GetLastInputType() end)
    if preferred == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch or lastInput == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        return true
    end

    local pg = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg and (
        pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui", true) or
        pg:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame", true) or
        pg:FindFirstChild("JumpButton", true) or
        pg:FindFirstChild("DynamicThumbstickFrame", true)
    ) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function computeWinSize()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vp  = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)

    local w = math.floor(math.clamp(vp.X * 0.7, 440, 560))
    local h = math.floor(math.clamp(vp.Y * 0.74, 340, 410))

    if vp.X > 80 then w = math.min(w, vp.X - 36) end
    if vp.Y > 80 then h = math.min(h, vp.Y - 36) end

    return math.max(400, w), math.max(320, h)
end

isMobile = detectMobile()
if isMobile then
    local w, h = computeWinSize()
    WIN_W, WIN_H = w, h
    RAIL_W = 128
end

--=====================================================================
--  [6] Environment helpers
--=====================================================================
local function getEnv()
    return (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
end

local function getSlot()
    local env = getEnv()
    local KiraHub = env.KiraHub
    if type(KiraHub) ~= "table" then KiraHub = { slots = {} }; env.KiraHub = KiraHub end
    if type(KiraHub.slots) ~= "table" then KiraHub.slots = {} end

    local slot = KiraHub.slots[UserIdStr]
    if type(slot) ~= "table" then slot = {}; KiraHub.slots[UserIdStr] = slot end
    return slot
end

META.ConfigFile = ("Kira/%s/cache/%s-config.json"):format(
    sanitizeName(META.Game),
    sanitizeName(LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name or "Player")
)

local selfTweens  = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

--=====================================================================
--  [9] Low-level UI helpers
--=====================================================================
local function tween(inst, dur, props, style)
    local existing = selfTweens[inst]
    if existing then pcall(function() existing:Cancel() end) end

    local t = TweenService:Create(
        inst,
        TweenInfo.new(dur, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        props
    )
    selfTweens[inst] = t
    t:Play()
    t.Completed:Connect(function()
        if selfTweens[inst] == t then selfTweens[inst] = nil end
    end)
end

local function new(className, props, parent)
    local inst = Instance.new(className)
    if props then
        for k, v in pairs(props) do inst[k] = v end
    end
    if parent then inst.Parent = parent end
    return inst
end

local function addStroke(parent, themeKey, thickness)
    local key   = type(themeKey) == "string" and themeKey or nil
    local color = key and (ActiveTheme[key] or ActiveTheme.line) or (themeKey or ActiveTheme.line)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color           = color
    stroke.Thickness       = thickness or 1
    stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    stroke.Parent          = parent
    if key then stroke:SetAttribute("th_stroke", key) end
    return stroke
end

local function addPadding(parent, l, t, r, b)
    if t == nil then t, r, b = l, l, l end
    local p = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    p.PaddingLeft   = UDim.new(0, l)
    p.PaddingTop    = UDim.new(0, t)
    p.PaddingRight  = UDim.new(0, r or l)
    p.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or t)
    p.Parent = parent
    return p
end

local function applyHover(frame, bgKey, hoverKey)
    if type(bgKey) == "string" then
        frame:SetAttribute("th_bg", bgKey)
        local c = ActiveTheme[bgKey]
        if c and frame:IsA("GuiObject") then frame.BackgroundColor3 = c end
    end
    frame:SetAttribute("th_hover", hoverKey)

    frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if frame:GetAttribute("locked") then return end
        frame:SetAttribute("th_over", true)
        local k = frame:GetAttribute("th_hover") or hoverKey
        local c = type(k) == "string" and ActiveTheme[k] or k
        if c then tween(frame, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = c }) end
    end)
    frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if frame:GetAttribute("locked") then return end
        frame:SetAttribute("th_over", false)
        local k = frame:GetAttribute("th_bg") or bgKey
        local c = type(k) == "string" and ActiveTheme[k] or k
        if c then tween(frame, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = c }) end
    end)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [10] Icon factory (vector-drawn icons)
--=====================================================================
local function makeIcon(parent, kind, color, zIndex)
    local root = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size  = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
        ZIndex = zIndex,
    }, parent)

    local function filled(x, y, w, h, radius)
        local f = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundColor3 = color,
            BorderSizePixel  = 0,
            Position         = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y),
            Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h),
            ZIndex           = zIndex + 1,
        }, root)
        if radius then
            new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius) }, f)
        end
        return f
    end

    local function outlined(x, y, w, h, radius)
        local f = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y),
            Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h),
            ZIndex   = zIndex + 1,
        }, root)
        new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 8) }, f)
        addStroke(f, color, 1.2)
        return f
    end

    if kind == "egg" then
        filled(4, 2, 8, 12, 5)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "aim" then
        outlined(2, 2, 12, 12, 6)
        filled(7, 7, 2, 2, 1)
        filled(7, 0, 2, 3, 0)
        filled(7, 13, 2, 3, 0)
        filled(0, 7, 3, 2, 0)
        filled(13, 7, 3, 2, 0)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "spark" then
        filled(7, 1, 2, 14, 1)
        filled(1, 7, 14, 2, 1)
        filled(4, 4, 2, 2, 1)
        filled(10, 10, 2, 2, 1)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "grid" then
        filled(1, 1, 6, 6, 2)
        filled(9, 1, 6, 6, 2)
        filled(1, 9, 6, 6, 2)
        filled(9, 9, 6, 6, 2)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "layers" then
        filled(2, 3,  12, 2, 1)
        filled(2, 7,  12, 2, 1)
        filled(2, 11, 12, 2, 1)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "out" then
        outlined(1, 3, 10, 10, 3)
        filled(8, 2, 6, 2, 1)
        filled(12, 2, 2, 6, 1)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "cog" then
        outlined(3, 3, 10, 10, 5)
        filled(7, 1, 2, 3, 1)
        filled(7, 12, 2, 3, 1)
        filled(1, 7, 3, 2, 1)
        filled(12, 7, 3, 2, 1)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "rocket" then
        filled(6, 1, 4, 9, 2)
        filled(7, 0, 2, 3, 1)
        filled(4, 8, 3, 4, 1)
        filled(9, 8, 3, 4, 1)
        filled(7, 11, 2, 4, 1)
        return root
    end

    if kind == "search" then
        outlined(1, 1, 10, 10, 5)
        filled(9, 10, 5, 2, 1).Rotation = 40
        return root
    end

    if kind == "info" then
        outlined(2, 2, 12, 12, 6)
        filled(7, 4, 2, 2, 1)
        filled(7, 7, 2, 5, 1)
    end

    return root
end

--=====================================================================
--  [11] Logo (embedded PNG, written to disk on first run)
--=====================================================================
local logoDarkAsset, logoLightAsset

local function decodeBase64(s)
    if type(s) ~= "string" or s == "" then return nil end
    local decoders = {}
    if crypt then
        decoders[#decoders+1] = crypt.base64decode
        decoders[#decoders+1] = crypt.base64_decode
    end
    if syn and syn.crypt and syn.crypt.base64 and syn.crypt.base64.decode then
        decoders[#decoders+1] = syn.crypt.base64.decode
    end
    if base64 and base64.decode then
        decoders[#decoders+1] = base64.decode
    end
    if base64_decode then
        decoders[#decoders+1] = base64_decode
    end

    for i = 1, #decoders do
        local ok, res = pcall(decoders[i], s)
        if ok and type(res) == "string" and #res > 64 then
            return res
        end
    end
end

local function ensureLogo(light)
    if light then
        if logoLightAsset then return logoLightAsset end
    else
        if logoDarkAsset then return logoDarkAsset end
    end

    local decoded = decodeBase64(LOGO_B64)
    if not decoded then return nil end

    local path = light and META.LogoFileLight or META.LogoFile

    if type(makefolder) == "function" then
        pcall(makefolder, "Kira")
    end
    if writefile then
        pcall(writefile, path, decoded)
    end

    if getcustomasset then
        local ok, res = pcall(getcustomasset, path)
        if ok and type(res) == "string" and res ~= "" then
            if light then logoLightAsset = res else logoDarkAsset = res end
            return res
        end
    end
end

local function refreshLogoTheme()
    local asset = ensureLogo(Flags.Theme == "Light")
    if not asset then return end
    for _, frame in ipairs(LogoFrames) do
        local mark = frame:FindFirstChild("Mark")
        if mark and mark:IsA("ImageLabel") then
            mark.Image = asset
            mark.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        end
    end
end

local function makeLogo(parent, zIndex, size)
    local sz = size or 18
    local frame = new("Frame", {
        Name                 = "Kira Hub",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size                 = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz),
        ZIndex               = zIndex,
    }, parent)

    local asset = ensureLogo(false)
    local img = new("ImageLabel", {
        Name                 = "Mark",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size                 = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        Image                = asset or "",
        ImageColor3          = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        ScaleType            = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        ZIndex               = zIndex + 1,
    }, frame)

    LogoFrames[#LogoFrames + 1] = frame
    return frame
end

--=====================================================================
--  [12] Get/create ScreenGui
--=====================================================================
local function getGuiParent()
    if gethui then
        local ok, res = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and res then return res end
    end

    local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    if pcall(function() return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("PH_UI") end) then
        return CoreGui
    end
    return LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end

local guiParent = getGuiParent()

-- Destroy any previous instance for this user
do
    local old = guiParent:FindFirstChild(SCREEN_NAME)
    if old then old:Destroy() end

    local PH = guiParent:FindFirstChild("PH_UI")
    if PH then
        local uid = (getgenv and getgenv() or _G).KiraUnloadUid
        if uid == nil or UserIdStr == tostring(uid) then
            PH:Destroy()
        end
    end
end

local ScreenGui = new("ScreenGui", {
    Name            = SCREEN_NAME,
    ResetOnSpawn    = false,
    IgnoreGuiInset  = true,
    ZIndexBehavior  = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    DisplayOrder    = 1200,
}, guiParent)

pcall(function()
    if syn and syn.protect_gui then
        syn.protect_gui(ScreenGui)
    end
end)

    computeWinSize   = computeWinSize,
}

assert(P1, "Part 1 must be loaded first")

--=====================================================================
--  [1] Config change debouncer (used by widgets)
--=====================================================================
local configSaveBusy = false
local configSaveQueued = false
local currentGen = P1.getSlot().gen or 1

-- Function set in Part 3. Placeholder for widgets.
local function scheduleConfigSave()
    if configSaveBusy or configSaveQueued then return end
    configSaveQueued = true
    local gen = currentGen
    task.delay(0.35, function()
        configSaveQueued = false
        if gen ~= (P1.getSlot().gen or 0) then return end
        if type(P1.saveConfig) == "function" then
            pcall(P1.saveConfig)
        end
    end)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [2] Dropdown popup support
--=====================================================================
local DropContainer = new("Frame", {
    Name             = "Drops",
    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
    Size             = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
    Visible          = false,
    ZIndex           = 90,
}, ScreenGui)

local function closeDrops()
    DropContainer:ClearAllChildren()
    DropContainer.Visible = false
end

Overlay.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    closeDrops()
    Overlay.Visible = false
end)

-- Generic popup renderer (single-select or multi-select)
local function openDropdown(anchor, flag, options, multi)
    closeDrops()
    Overlay.Visible = true
    Overlay.ZIndex  = 85
    DropContainer.Visible = true

    local absPos  = anchor.AbsolutePosition
    local absSize = anchor.AbsoluteSize

    local visibleCount = math.min(7, #options)
    local popH = visibleCount * 28 + 10
    local popW = math.max(absSize.X, multi and 180 or 120)

    local panel = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(absPos.X, absPos.Y + absSize.Y + 6),
        Size     = UDim2.fromOffset(popW, popH),
        ZIndex   = 95,
    }, DropContainer)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10) }, panel)
    addStroke(panel, "accentDeep", 1)
    panel:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")

    local scroll = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #options * 28),
        ScrollBarThickness = 3,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 96,
    }, panel)
    addPadding(scroll, 5, 5, 5, 5)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 2),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, scroll)

    -- Track selected set for multi
    local selectedSet = {}
    if multi and type(Flags[flag]) == "table" then
        for _, v in ipairs(Flags[flag]) do selectedSet[v] = true end
    end

    for i, opt in ipairs(options) do
        local isOn = multi and selectedSet[opt] or (not multi and opt == Flags[flag])

        local btn = new("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3 = isOn and ActiveTheme.lift or ActiveTheme.card,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
            Font = FONTS.body,
            Text = "   " .. tostring(opt),
            TextColor3 = isOn and ActiveTheme.accent or ActiveTheme.text,
            TextSize = 12,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            LayoutOrder = i,
            ZIndex = 97,
        }, scroll)
        new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, btn)
        applyHover(btn, isOn and "lift" or "card", "fill")

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if multi then
                local set = {}
                for _, v in ipairs(Flags[flag]) do set[v] = true end
                set[opt] = not set[opt]

                local list = {}
                for _, v in ipairs(options) do
                    if set[v] then list[#list + 1] = v end
                end
                Flags[flag] = list

                local w = Widgets[flag]
                if w and w.on then pcall(w.on, list) end
                if w and w.refresh then pcall(w.refresh) end

                scheduleConfigSave()
                -- re-render for immediate feedback
                openDropdown(anchor, flag, options, multi)
            else
                Flags[flag] = opt
                local w = Widgets[flag]
                if w and w.set then pcall(w.set, opt) end
                if w and w.on then pcall(w.on, opt) end
                closeDrops()
                Overlay.Visible = false
                scheduleConfigSave()
            end
        end)
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [3] Page creation
--=====================================================================
local function createPage(name, subtitle)
    local scroll = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        Name = name,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ScrollBarThickness = (not isMobile) and 4 or 8,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = ActiveTheme.line,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 13,
    }, P1.PageBody or (function()
        -- PageBody is created in the same call sequence; lazily create here
        return nil
    end)())
    -- NOTE: PageBody frame is created below on first call
    scroll:SetAttribute("th_scroll", "line")
    addPadding(scroll, 12, 4, 12, 14)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 8),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, scroll)

    local page = {
        name = name,
        subtitle = subtitle,
        scroll = scroll,
        items = {},
        n = 0,
        card = nil,
        lastRule = nil,
    }

    Pages[name] = page
    PageOrder[#PageOrder + 1] = name
    return page
end

-- Build the PageBody frame once
if not P1.PageBody then
    local bodyTop = (not isMobile) and 80 or 88
    P1.PageBody = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, bodyTop),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -bodyTop),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        ZIndex = 12,
    }, Main)
end

-- Re-run createPage definition now that PageBody exists
local function createPageFinal(name, subtitle)
    local scroll = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        Name = name,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ScrollBarThickness = (not isMobile) and 4 or 8,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = ActiveTheme.line,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 13,
    }, P1.PageBody)
    scroll:SetAttribute("th_scroll", "line")
    addPadding(scroll, 12, 4, 12, 14)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 8),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, scroll)

    local page = {
        name = name,
        subtitle = subtitle,
        scroll = scroll,
        items = {},
        n = 0,
        card = nil,
        lastRule = nil,
    }
    Pages[name] = page
    PageOrder[#PageOrder + 1] = name
    return page
end

P1.createPage = createPageFinal

--=====================================================================
--  [4] Sidebar (page list on rail) + Page switching
--=====================================================================
local navItems = {}

local function paintIconTree(root, color)
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("ImageLabel") then
            d.ImageColor3 = color
        elseif d:IsA("Frame") and d.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then
            d.BackgroundColor3 = color
        elseif d:IsA("UIStroke") then
            d.Color = color
        end
    end
end

local currentPage
local searchBox = nil   -- set below

local function filterItems(page, query)
    local q = string.lower(query or "")
    local any = false
    local sectionInst
    for _, item in ipairs(page.items) do
        if item.kind == "section" then
            if sectionInst then
                sectionInst.Visible = (q == "") or any
            end
            sectionInst = item.inst
            any = false
        else
            local visible = (q == "") or (string.find(item.q, q, 1, true) ~= nil)
            if item.visibleIf and not item.visibleIf() then visible = false end
            item.inst.Visible = visible
            if visible then any = true end
        end
    end
    if sectionInst then
        sectionInst.Visible = (q == "") or any
    end
end

local function setPage(name)
    local page = Pages[name]
    if not page then return end

    currentPage = page

    for k, v in pairs(navItems) do
        local on = (k == name)
        v.on = on
        v.lab.TextColor3 = on and ActiveTheme.text or ActiveTheme.dim
        v.lab.Font = on and FONTS.mid or FONTS.body
        v.btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and ActiveTheme.card or ActiveTheme.rail
        v.btn.BackgroundTransparency = (not on) and 1 or 0
        paintIconTree(v.ico, on and ActiveTheme.accent or ActiveTheme.mute)
    end

    for _, p in pairs(Pages) do
        p.scroll.Visible = (p == page)
        if p == page then p.scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero end
    end

    PageTitle.Text = name
    PageSubtitle.Text = page.subtitle or ""
    if searchBox then filterItems(page, searchBox.Text) end
end

P1.setPage = setPage
P1.currentPage = function() return currentPage end

-- Sidebar section builder
local function addNavSection(label, pageNames, order)
    local header = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
        Font = FONTS.mono,
        Text = label,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.mute,
        TextSize = 9,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        LayoutOrder = order,
        ZIndex = 12,
    }, PageList)
    header:SetAttribute("th_text", "mute")

    for i, name in ipairs(pageNames) do
        local btn = new("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.rail,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, (not isMobile) and 26 or 34),
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            LayoutOrder = order + i,
            ZIndex = 12,
        }, PageList)
        new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, btn)

        local ico = makeIcon(btn, PAGE_ICONS[name] or "layers", ActiveTheme.mute, 13)
        ico.Name = "Ico"
        ico.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 5)

        local lab = new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0),
            Font = FONTS.body,
            Text = name,
            TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
            TextSize = 12,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ZIndex = 13,
        }, btn)
        lab:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")

        navItems[name] = { btn = btn, lab = lab, ico = ico, on = false }

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if navItems[name].on then return end
            tween(btn, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.lift })
        end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if navItems[name].on then return end
            tween(btn, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
        end)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            setPage(name)
        end)
    end
end

P1.addNavSection = addNavSection

do

        ZIndex = 12,

    searchFrame:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")

    local sIcon = makeIcon(searchFrame, "search", ActiveTheme.mute, 13)
    sIcon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 6)

    local box = new("TextBox", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 0),
        Font = FONTS.body,
        PlaceholderText = "Filter this page",
        PlaceholderColor3 = ActiveTheme.mute,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = (not isMobile) and 12 or 14,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        ZIndex = 13,
    }, searchFrame)
    box:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")
    box:SetAttribute("th_placeholder", "mute")

    box.Focused:Connect(function()
        tween(outline, 0.12, { Color = ActiveTheme.accent })
    end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        tween(outline, 0.12, { Color = ActiveTheme.line })
    end)

    searchBox = box
    P1.searchBox = box

    box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if currentPage then
            filterItems(currentPage, box.Text)
        end
    end)

--=====================================================================
--  [6] Section (card with header)
--=====================================================================
local function createSection(page, label)
    page.lastRule = nil

    page.n = page.n + 1

    local card = new("Frame", {
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        LayoutOrder = page.n,
        ZIndex = 13,
    }, page.scroll)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, card)
    addStroke(card, "line", 1)
    card:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")
    new("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 0),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, card)

    -- Header
    local head = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
        LayoutOrder = 0,
        ZIndex = 14,
    }, card)

    local tick = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.accent,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 2),
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, head)
    tick:SetAttribute("th_bg", "accent")

    local hLabel = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0),
        Font = FONTS.mono,
        Text = label,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
        TextSize = 10,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, head)
    hLabel:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")

    page.card = card
    page.items[#page.items + 1] = {
        kind = "section",
        inst = card,
        q = string.lower(label),
    }

    return card
end

P1.createSection = createSection

--=====================================================================
--  [7] Row (base for every widget)
--=====================================================================
local function createRow(page, title, desc, customHeight)
    local rowH = customHeight or (not isMobile and 36 or 44)
    if desc and not customHeight then
        rowH = (not isMobile) and 46 or 54
    end

    page.n = page.n + 1

    local row = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rowH),
        LayoutOrder = page.n,
        ZIndex = 14,
    }, page.card or page.scroll)

    -- Divider on top
    local rule = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.line,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1),
        Visible = page.lastRule ~= nil,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, row)
    rule:SetAttribute("th_bg", "line")
    page.lastRule = rule

    row:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")
    row:SetAttribute("th_hover", "lift")
    row:SetAttribute("th_row", true)

    row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        row:SetAttribute("th_over", true)
        tween(row, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.lift })
    end)
    row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        row:SetAttribute("th_over", false)
        tween(row, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card })
    end)

    -- Title
    local titleLbl = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, desc and 6 or 10),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -(140 + 20), 0, 14),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = title,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 12,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, row)
    titleLbl:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

    if desc then
        local descLbl = new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 22),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -(140 + 20), 0, 20),
            Font = FONTS.body,
            Text = desc,
            TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
            TextWrapped = true,
            ZIndex = 15,
        }, row)
        descLbl:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")
    end

    -- Right-side container for control
    local ctrl = new("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(140, (not isMobile) and 26 or 32),
        ZIndex = 16,
    }, row)

    page.items[#page.items + 1] = {
        kind = "row",
        inst = row,
        q = string.lower(title .. " " .. (desc or "")),
    }

    return row, ctrl
end

P1.createRow = createRow

--=====================================================================
--  [8] Toggle widget
--=====================================================================
local function addToggle(page, flag, title, desc, default, opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    Flags[flag] = not not default

    local _, ctrl = createRow(page, title, desc)

    -- Optional choice chip (right of the toggle)
    local choiceChip
    if opts.options then
        Flags[opts.flag] = Flags[opts.flag] or opts.options[1]

        choiceChip = new("TextButton", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 22),
            Font = FONTS.mid,
            Text = tostring(Flags[opts.flag]) .. " ▾",
            TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
            TextSize = 10,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            ZIndex = 17,
        }, ctrl)
        new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, choiceChip)
        addStroke(choiceChip, "line", 1)
        choiceChip:SetAttribute("th_bg", "fill")
        choiceChip:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

        choiceChip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            openDropdown(choiceChip, opts.flag, opts.options, false)
        end)

        Widgets[opts.flag] = {
            kind = "choice",
            chip = choiceChip,
            set = function(v)
                Flags[opts.flag] = v
                choiceChip.Text = tostring(v) .. " ▾"
                if not P1.applyingConfig then
                    scheduleConfigSave()
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Toggle knob
    local switch = new("TextButton", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = Flags[flag] and ActiveTheme.accent or ActiveTheme.fill,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset((not isMobile) and 34 or 42, (not isMobile) and 18 or 24),
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 17,
    }, ctrl)
    local radius = (not isMobile) and 9 or 12
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius) }, switch)

    local switchStroke = addStroke(switch, Flags[flag] and "accent" or "line", 1)

    local knobOff = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2)
    local knobOn  = (isMobile and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -6))
    local knobSize = (not isMobile) and 14 or 16

    local knob = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Flags[flag] and ActiveTheme.ink or ActiveTheme.text,
        Position = Flags[flag] and knobOn or knobOff,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(knobSize, knobSize),
        ZIndex = 18,
    }, switch)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, (not isMobile) and 7 or 8) }, knob)

    local function paint(on)
        tween(switch, 0.16, { BackgroundColor3 = on and ActiveTheme.accent or ActiveTheme.fill })
        tween(knob, 0.16, {
            Position = on and knobOn or knobOff,
            BackgroundColor3 = on and ActiveTheme.ink or ActiveTheme.text,
        })
        tween(switchStroke, 0.16, { Color = on and ActiveTheme.accent or ActiveTheme.line })
    end

    switch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local newVal = not Flags[flag]
        Flags[flag] = newVal
        paint(newVal)

        if not P1.applyingConfig then
            scheduleConfigSave()
        end

        local w = Widgets[flag]
        task.defer(function()
            if w and w.on then pcall(w.on, newVal) end
        end)
    end)

    Widgets[flag] = Widgets[flag] or {}
    Widgets[flag].kind = "toggle"
    Widgets[flag].status = nil
    Widgets[flag].set = function(v)
        Flags[flag] = not not v
        paint(Flags[flag])
    end
    return Widgets[flag]
end

P1.addToggle = addToggle

--=====================================================================
--  [9] Slider widget
--=====================================================================
local function addSlider(page, flag, title, desc, minV, maxV, default, suffix)
    Flags[flag] = default

    local row, ctrl = createRow(page, title, desc)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 72)
    ctrl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(148, 48)

    local readout = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20),
        Font = FONTS.mono,
        Text = tostring(default) .. (suffix or ""),
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.accent,
        TextSize = 12,
        ZIndex = 17,
    }, ctrl)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, readout)
    readout:SetAttribute("th_bg", "fill")
    readout:SetAttribute("th_text", "accent")

    local track = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 32),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10),
        ZIndex = 17,
        Active = true,
    }, ctrl)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, track)

    local fillBar = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.accent,
        Size = UDim2.new((default - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 1, 0),
        ZIndex = 18,
    }, track)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, fillBar)

    local thumb = new("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        Position = UDim2.new((default - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
        ZIndex = 19,
    }, track)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9) }, thumb)
    addStroke(thumb, "accentDeep", 1)

    local dragHit = new("TextButton", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = "",
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        Active = true,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32),
        ZIndex = 21,
    }, ctrl)

    local dragging = false

    local function commit(v)
        local num = tonumber(v)
        if not num then return end
        num = math.clamp(math.floor(num + 0.5), minV, maxV)
        Flags[flag] = num

        local t = (num - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1)
        fillBar.Size = UDim2.new(t, 0, 1, 0)
        thumb.Position = UDim2.new(t, 0, 0.5, 0)
        readout.Text = tostring(num) .. (suffix or "")

        if P1.applyingConfig then return end

        local w = Widgets[flag]
        if w and w.on then pcall(w.on, num) end
        if not P1.applyingConfig then scheduleConfigSave() end
    end

    dragHit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or tp == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            if page.scroll then page.scroll.ScrollingEnabled = false end
            if PageList then PageList.ScrollingEnabled = false end
            local relX = math.clamp(
                (input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1),
                0, 1
            )
            commit(minV + relX * (maxV - minV))
        end
    end)

    local connMove = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or tp == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local relX = math.clamp(
                (input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1),
                0, 1
            )
            commit(minV + relX * (maxV - minV))
        end
    end)
    CleanupList[#CleanupList + 1] = connMove

    local connEnd = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or tp == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
            if page.scroll then page.scroll.ScrollingEnabled = true end
            if PageList then PageList.ScrollingEnabled = true end
        end
    end)
    CleanupList[#CleanupList + 1] = connEnd

    Widgets[flag] = {
        kind = "slider",
        status = nil,
        set = commit,
    }
    return Widgets[flag]
end

P1.addSlider = addSlider

--=====================================================================
--  [10] Dropdown widget
--=====================================================================
local function multiSummary(list, options)
    if type(list) ~= "table" then return "none" end
    local set = {}
    for _, v in ipairs(list) do set[v] = true end
    local count, total = 0, #options
    for _, v in ipairs(options) do if set[v] then count = count + 1 end end
    if count == 0 then return "none" end
    if count == total then return "all · " .. total end
    return count .. " / " .. total
end

local function addDropdown(page, flag, title, desc, options, default, multi)
    if multi then
        Flags[flag] = default or { unpack(options) }
    else
        Flags[flag] = default or options[1]
    end

    local _, ctrl = createRow(page, title, desc)
    local chip = new("TextButton", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = (multi and multiSummary(Flags[flag], options) or tostring(Flags[flag])) .. "   ",
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 11,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 17,
    }, ctrl)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, chip)
    addStroke(chip, "line", 1)
    applyHover(chip, "fill", "lift")
    chip:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

    local arrow = new("TextLabel", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = "▾",
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.mute,
        TextSize = 11,
        ZIndex = 18,
    }, chip)

    local function refresh()
        chip.Text = (multi and multiSummary(Flags[flag], options) or tostring(Flags[flag])) .. "   "
    end

    chip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        openDropdown(chip, flag, options, multi)
    end)

    Widgets[flag] = {
        kind = "dropdown",
        options = options,
        refresh = refresh,
        set = function(v)
            Flags[flag] = v
            refresh()
        end,
    }
    return Widgets[flag]
end

P1.addDropdown = addDropdown

--=====================================================================
--  [11] Input widget (textbox)
--=====================================================================
local function addInput(page, flag, title, desc, placeholder, default, extra)
    Flags[flag] = default or ""

    local _, ctrl = createRow(page, title, desc)

    local box = new("TextBox", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
        Font = FONTS.mono,
        Text = Flags[flag],
        PlaceholderText = placeholder or "",
        PlaceholderColor3 = ActiveTheme.mute,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 11,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ClearTextOnFocus = false,
        ZIndex = 17,
    }, ctrl)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, box)
    box:SetAttribute("th_bg", "fill")
    box:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")
    box:SetAttribute("th_placeholder", "mute")

    local outline = addStroke(box, "line", 1)
    addPadding(box, 8, 0, 8, 0)

    box.Focused:Connect(function()
        tween(outline, 0.12, { Color = ActiveTheme.accent })
    end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        tween(outline, 0.12, { Color = ActiveTheme.line })
        Flags[flag] = box.Text
        local w = Widgets[flag]
        if w and w.on then pcall(w.on, box.Text) end
        if not P1.applyingConfig then scheduleConfigSave() end
    end)
    box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        Flags[flag] = box.Text
        if P1.applyingConfig then return end
        if not P1.applyingConfig then scheduleConfigSave() end
    end)

    Widgets[flag] = {
        kind = "input",
        box = box,
        set = function(v)
            Flags[flag] = v
            box.Text = tostring(v)
        end,
    }

    -- Register visibleIf on the item
    local item = page.items[#page.items]
    if extra and extra.visibleIf and item then
        item.visibleIf = extra.visibleIf
    end

    return Widgets[flag]
end

P1.addInput = addInput

--=====================================================================
--  [12] Button widget
--=====================================================================
local function addButton(page, flag, title, desc, label, callback, accent)
    local _, ctrl = createRow(page, title, desc)

    local btn = new("TextButton", {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = label,
        TextSize = 11,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 17,
    }, ctrl)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, btn)

    if not accent then
        addStroke(btn, "line", 1)
    end

    applyHover(btn, accent and "accent" or "fill", accent and "accentHover" or "lift")

    local textKey = accent and "ink" or "text"
    btn:SetAttribute("th_text", textKey)
    btn.TextColor3 = ActiveTheme[textKey]

    Widgets[flag] = {
        kind = "button",
        btn = btn,
    }

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if callback then pcall(callback) end
        local w = Widgets[flag]
        if w and w.on then pcall(w.on) end
    end)

    return Widgets[flag]
end

P1.addButton = addButton

--=====================================================================
--  [13] Keybind widget
--=====================================================================
local function addKeybind(page, flag, title, desc, default)
    Flags[flag] = default

    local _, ctrl = createRow(page, title, desc)
    local listening = false

    local btn = new("TextButton", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24),
        Font = FONTS.mono,
        Text = default.Name,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 11,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 17,
    }, ctrl)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, btn)
    addStroke(btn, "line", 1)
    applyHover(btn, "fill", "lift")
    btn:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        listening = true
        btn.Text = "press"
        btn.TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.accent
    end)

    local conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        if not listening then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
        if gameProcessed then return end

        listening = false
        Flags[flag] = input.KeyCode
        btn.Text = input.KeyCode.Name
        btn.TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text

        local w = Widgets[flag]
        if w and w.on then pcall(w.on, input.KeyCode) end
        if not P1.applyingConfig then scheduleConfigSave() end
    end)
    CleanupList[#CleanupList + 1] = conn

    Widgets[flag] = {
        kind = "keybind",
        status = nil,
        set = function(v)
            if type(v) == "string" then
                local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[v] end)
                if ok then v = kc end
            end
            Flags[flag] = v
            local ok, name = pcall(function() return v.Name end)
            btn.Text = ok and name or tostring(v)
        end,
    }
    return Widgets[flag]
end

P1.addKeybind = addKeybind

--=====================================================================
--  [14] Info row (no control)
--=====================================================================
local function addInfo(page, title, desc)
    local row = new("Frame", {
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        LayoutOrder = 0,
        ZIndex = 14,
    }, page.card or page.scroll)

    page.n = page.n + 1
    row.LayoutOrder = page.n

    local rule = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.line,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1),
        Visible = page.lastRule ~= nil,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, row)
    rule:SetAttribute("th_bg", "line")
    page.lastRule = rule

    row:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")
    row:SetAttribute("th_hover", "lift")
    row:SetAttribute("th_row", true)

    row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        row:SetAttribute("th_over", true)
        tween(row, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.lift })
    end)
    row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        row:SetAttribute("th_over", false)
        tween(row, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card })
    end)

    local head = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 8),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 14),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = title,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 12,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, row)
    head:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

    local body = new("TextLabel", {
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 24),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0),
        Font = FONTS.body,
        Text = desc,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
        TextSize = 11,
        TextWrapped = true,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, row)
    body:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")

    addPadding(row, 0, 0, 0, 10)

    page.items[#page.items + 1] = {
        kind = "row",
        inst = row,
        q = string.lower(title .. " " .. desc),
    }
    return row
end

P1.addInfo = addInfo

--=====================================================================
P1.openDropdown   = openDropdown
P1.closeDrops     = closeDrops
P1.scheduleConfigSave = scheduleConfigSave
P1.configSaveBusy = function() return configSaveBusy end

--=====================================================================
--  [16] Status line update helper (used by widget on())
--=====================================================================
local function setStatus(flag, text)
    local w = Widgets[flag]
    if w and w.status then
        w.status.Text = text
    end
end

P1.setStatus = setStatus

-- About: Links section (only if any link is present)
do
    local function cleanString(s)
        return type(s) == "string" and s:match("%S") ~= nil
    end

    local function normalizeDiscord(s)
        if type(s) ~= "string" or s:match("%S") == nil then return nil end
        if s:find("discord%.", 1) or s:find("http", 1, true) then return s end
        return "discord.gg/" .. s
    end

    local discordLink = normalizeDiscord(META.Discord)
    local hasSite     = cleanString(META.Website)
    local hasChange   = cleanString(META.Changelog)

    if discordLink or hasSite or hasChange then
        createSection(pageAbout, "Links")

        if discordLink then
            addButton(pageAbout, "CopyDiscord", "Discord", discordLink, "Copy", function()
                if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, discordLink) end
            end, true)
        end
        if hasSite then
            addButton(pageAbout, "CopySite", "Website", META.Website, "Copy", function()
                if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, META.Website) end
            end, false)
        end
        if hasChange then
            addInfo(pageAbout, "What's new", META.Changelog)
        end
    end
end

-- About: Credits

addInfo(pageAbout, "Made by", cleanString and nil or (type(META.Author) == "string" and META.Author:match("%S") and META.Author or META.Title) or META.Title)

do
    if type(META.Credits) == "string" and META.Credits:match("%S") then
        addInfo(pageAbout, "With", META.Credits)
    end

    if type(META.Support) == "string" and META.Support:match("%S") then
        local normalized = nil
        local d = META.Discord
        if type(d) == "string" and d:match("%S") then
            normalized = (d:find("discord%.", 1) or d:find("http", 1, true)) and d or ("discord.gg/" .. d)
        end

        if not normalized or not string.find(META.Support, normalized, 1, true) then
            addInfo(pageAbout, "Support", META.Support)
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [3] ABOUT PAGE — hero card
--=====================================================================
(function(page)
    local hero = new("Frame", {
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ZIndex = 13,
    }, page.scroll)
    page.n = page.n + 1
    hero.LayoutOrder = page.n

    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, hero)
    addStroke(hero, "line", 1)
    hero:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")

    addPadding(hero, 14, 14, 14, 14)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 10),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, hero)

    -- Row: logo + title + chips
    local headRow = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48),
        LayoutOrder = 1,
        ZIndex = 14,
    }, hero)

    makeLogo(headRow, 15, 44).Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 2)

    local title = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 2),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 20),
        Font = FONTS.title,
        Text = META.Title .. " " .. META.Product,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 18,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, headRow)
    title:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

    local chipRow = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 26),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 20),
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, headRow)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
    }, chipRow)

    local function makeChip(text, order, colorKey)
        local chip = new("TextLabel", {
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18),
            Font = FONTS.mono,
            Text = "  " .. text .. "  ",
            TextColor3 = ActiveTheme[colorKey] or ActiveTheme.dim,
            TextSize = 10,
            LayoutOrder = order,
            ZIndex = 16,
        }, chipRow)
        new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, chip)
        chip:SetAttribute("th_bg", "fill")
        chip:SetAttribute("th_text", colorKey or "dim")
        return chip
    end

    makeChip("v" .. tostring(META.Version), 1, "accent")
    if type(META.Status) == "string" and META.Status:match("%S") then
        makeChip(META.Status, 2, "dim")
    end
    if type(META.Game) == "string" and META.Game:match("%S") then
        makeChip(META.Game, 3, "dim")
    end

    -- Tagline
    local tagline = (type(META.Tagline) == "string" and META.Tagline:match("%S"))
        and META.Tagline
        or "Autofarm, hatch eggs and other stuff"

    local desc = new("TextLabel", {
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        Font = FONTS.body,
        Text = tagline,
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
        TextSize = 12,
        TextWrapped = true,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        LayoutOrder = 2,
        ZIndex = 15,
    }, hero)
    desc:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")

    page.items[#page.items + 1] = {
        kind = "section",
        inst = hero,
        q = string.lower(title.Text .. " " .. tagline),
    }

    page.card = nil
    page.lastRule = nil
end)(pageAbout)

-- About: Links section (only if any link is present)
do
    local function cleanString(s)
        return type(s) == "string" and s:match("%S") ~= nil
    end

    local function normalizeDiscord(s)
        if type(s) ~= "string" or s:match("%S") == nil then return nil end
        if s:find("discord%.", 1) or s:find("http", 1, true) then return s end
        return "discord.gg/" .. s
    end

    local discordLink = normalizeDiscord(META.Discord)
    local hasSite     = cleanString(META.Website)
    local hasChange   = cleanString(META.Changelog)

    if discordLink or hasSite or hasChange then
        createSection(pageAbout, "Links")

        if discordLink then
            addButton(pageAbout, "CopyDiscord", "Discord", discordLink, "Copy", function()
                if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, discordLink) end
            end, true)
        end
        if hasSite then
            addButton(pageAbout, "CopySite", "Website", META.Website, "Copy", function()
                if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, META.Website) end
            end, false)
        end
        if hasChange then
            addInfo(pageAbout, "What's new", META.Changelog)
        end
    end
end

-- About: Credits
createSection(pageAbout, "Credits")
addInfo(pageAbout, "Made by", cleanString and nil or (type(META.Author) == "string" and META.Author:match("%S") and META.Author or META.Title) or META.Title)

do
    if type(META.Credits) == "string" and META.Credits:match("%S") then
        addInfo(pageAbout, "With", META.Credits)
    end

    if type(META.Support) == "string" and META.Support:match("%S") then
        local normalized = nil
        local d = META.Discord
        if type(d) == "string" and d:match("%S") then
            normalized = (d:find("discord%.", 1) or d:find("http", 1, true)) and d or ("discord.gg/" .. d)
        end

        if not normalized or not string.find(META.Support, normalized, 1, true) then
            addInfo(pageAbout, "Support", META.Support)
        end
    end
end

createSection(pageAbout, "Disclaimer")
addInfo(pageAbout, "Not official",
    "Not affiliated with " .. (META.Game or "this game") ..
    ". I'm not responsible for any bans, use at your own risk !")

    "Copy", function()
        if P1.saveConfig then pcall(P1.saveConfig, true) end
        local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
            if P1.collectFlags then
                return HttpService:JSONEncode(P1.collectFlags())
            end
        end)
        if ok and encoded and setclipboard then
            pcall(setclipboard, encoded)
        end
    end, true)

addInput(pageSettings, "ImportPaste", "Import settings",
    "load config from clipboard",
    "paste, then press Import", "")

addButton(pageSettings, "ImportCfg", "Import", nil,
    "Import", function()
        local pasted = Flags.ImportPaste
        if type(pasted) ~= "string" or pasted == "" then return end

        local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(pasted)
        end)
        if not ok or type(decoded) ~= "table" then return end

        if P1.applyConfig then P1.applyConfig(decoded, true) end
        if P1.setTheme then pcall(P1.setTheme, Flags.Theme or "Dark") end
        if P1.saveConfig then pcall(P1.saveConfig, true) end
    end, false)

createSection(pageSettings, "Window")

addButton(pageSettings, "ResetWin", "Reset position & size",
    "window and orb back to the middle at default size",
    "Reset", function()
        Window.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
        Window.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(P1.WIN_W, P1.WIN_H)
        UIScale.Scale = 1

        if Widgets.UIScale and Widgets.UIScale.set then
            Widgets.UIScale.set(100)
        end

        if P1.Orb and P1.Orb.Position then
            if isMobile then
                P1.Orb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 36, 1, -88)
            else
                P1.Orb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0.5, 0)
            end
        end

        if P1.relayout then pcall(P1.relayout) end
    end, false)

--=====================================================================
--  [6] Widget handlers (on())
--=====================================================================
local function bindOn(flag, fn)
    local w = Widgets[flag]
    if w then w.on = fn end
end

bindOn("UIScale", function(v)
    UIScale.Scale = v / 100
end)

bindOn("PhoneUI", function(v)
    if P1.phoneLayoutLocked ~= nil then
        P1.phoneLayoutLocked = true
        P1.isMobile = not not v
        if P1.relayout then pcall(P1.relayout) end
    end
end)

bindOn("Theme", function(v)
    if P1.setTheme then pcall(P1.setTheme, v) end
end)

bindOn("FlightBind2", function(kc)
    Flags.FlightBind = kc
end)

bindOn("CfgPreset", function(name)
    if P1.loadNamed then
        pcall(P1.loadNamed, name, true)
    end
    if P1.refreshCfgList then pcall(P1.refreshCfgList) end
end)

-- Auto Steal callbacks are wired in Part 8 (game logic)
-- Webhook test / Preview / etc will be wired there too

--=====================================================================
--  [7] CONFIG SYSTEM
--=====================================================================
local CONFIG_DIR     = "Kira"
local CONFIG_GAME    = "Kira/" .. P1.sanitizeName(META.Game)
local CONFIG_CACHE   = CONFIG_GAME .. "/cache"
local CONFIG_NAMED   = CONFIG_GAME .. "/configs"

local HIDDEN_FLAGS = {
    ImportPaste  = true,
    Flight       = true,
    HopNearNight = true,
    CfgSaveName  = true,
    PhoneUI      = true,
}

-- Ensure directories exist
local function ensureDirs()
    if type(makefolder) == "function" then
        pcall(makefolder, "Kira")
        pcall(makefolder, CONFIG_GAME)
        pcall(makefolder, CONFIG_CACHE)
        pcall(makefolder, CONFIG_NAMED)
    end
end

local function safeWrite(path, data)
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
    ensureDirs()
    local folder = path:match("^(.*)/[^/]+$")
    if folder and type(makefolder) == "function" then
        pcall(makefolder, folder)
    end
    local ok = pcall(writefile, path, data)
    if not ok then
        warn("[Kira/cfg] write fail:", path)
    end
    return ok
end

local function safeRead(path)
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
    local ok, data = pcall(readfile, path)
    if ok and type(data) == "string" and data ~= "" then
        return data
    end
end

local function safeDecode(str)
    local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(str)
    end)
    if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then
        return decoded
    end
end

-- Collect flags into a plain serializable table
local function collectFlags()
    local out = {}
    for k, v in pairs(Flags) do
        if not HIDDEN_FLAGS[k] and (k ~= "HookUrl" or Flags.ExportUrl) then
            local tp = type(v)
            if tp == "boolean" or tp == "number" or tp == "string" then
                out[k] = v
            elseif tp == "table" then
                out[k] = v
            else
                local ok, name = pcall(function() return v.Name end)
                if ok and type(name) == "string" then
                    out[k] = name
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end

-- Save current cache
local function saveConfig(silent)
    if P1.applyingConfig and not silent then return end
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
        warn("[Kira/cfg] writefile missing")
        return
    end

    local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONEncode(collectFlags())
    end)
    if not ok or type(encoded) ~= "string" then
        warn("[Kira/cfg] encode fail")
        return
    end

    local playerName = P1.sanitizeName(LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name or "Player")
    local primary = CONFIG_CACHE .. "/" .. playerName .. "-config.json"
    if not safeWrite(primary, encoded) then
        safeWrite(CONFIG_CACHE .. "/" .. P1.UserIdStr .. "-config.json", encoded)
    end
end

-- Save as named preset
local function saveNamed(name)
    local cleaned = tostring(name or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    local safe    = cleaned ~= "" and P1.sanitizeName(cleaned) or "default"

    local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONEncode(collectFlags())
    end)
    if not ok or type(encoded) ~= "string" then
        warn("[Kira/cfg] named encode fail")
        return false
    end

    if not safeWrite(CONFIG_NAMED .. "/" .. safe .. ".json", encoded) then
        return false
    end

    Flags.CfgPreset = safe
    if P1.refreshCfgList then pcall(P1.refreshCfgList) end
    print("[Kira/cfg] saved named:", safe)
    return true
end

-- Translate legacy flag names and enum strings
local function normalizeValue(flag, value)
    local w = Widgets[flag]

    if w and w.kind == "keybind" and type(value) == "string" then
        local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[value] end)
        if ok and kc then return kc end
    end

    if flag == "Theme" and (value == "Dusk" or value == "dusk") then
        return "Dark"
    end

    return value
end

-- Apply a decoded config table to flags/widgets
local function applyConfig(data, runOn)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return end

    P1.applyingConfig = true

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        if data.NeverTraps ~= nil then
            data.AntiTrap = data.AntiTrap or true
        end

        data.NeverTraps = nil
        data.RarityZones = nil
        data.HopMinPlayers = nil
        data.HopMaxPlayers = nil
        data.AntiDie = nil

        if data.StealMode == "Rarity snipe" then
            data.StealMode = "Egg type filter"
        end

        for k, v in pairs(data) do
            if k ~= "ImportPaste" then
                local value = normalizeValue(k, v)
                local w = Widgets[k]

                if w and w.set then
                    pcall(w.set, value)
                    if runOn and w.on and w.kind ~= "slider"
                        and k ~= "Flight" and k ~= "CfgPreset" then
                        pcall(w.on, Flags[k])
                    end
                else
                    Flags[k] = value
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    P1.applyingConfig = false

    if not ok then
        warn("[Kira/cfg] apply fail:", tostring(err))
    end
end

-- Load from file
local function loadFromFile(path)
    local str = safeRead(path)
    if not str then return nil end
    local data = safeDecode(str)
    if data then return data, path end
end

-- List all saved presets
local function listPresets()
    local list = { "default" }
    local seen = { default = true }

    if type(listfiles) == "function" then
        local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, CONFIG_NAMED)
        if ok and type(files) == "table" then
            for _, f in ipairs(files) do
                local name = tostring(f or ""):gsub("\\", "/"):match("([^/]+)%.json$")
                if name and not seen[name] then
                    seen[name] = true
                    list[#list + 1] = name
                end
            end
        end
    elseif type(isfile) == "function" then
        local typed = tostring(Flags.CfgPreset or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        local safe  = typed ~= "" and P1.sanitizeName(typed) or "default"
        if safe ~= "default" and not seen[safe] then
            list[#list + 1] = safe
        end
    end

    table.sort(list, function(a, b)
        if a == "default" then return true end
        if b == "default" then return false end
        return a < b
    end)

    return list
end

-- Refresh CfgPreset dropdown options
local function refreshCfgList()
    local widget = Widgets.CfgPreset
    if not widget or not widget.options then return end

    local list = listPresets()

    for i = #widget.options, 1, -1 do
        widget.options[i] = nil
    end
    for i = 1, #list do
        widget.options[i] = list[i]
    end

    local typed = tostring(Flags.CfgPreset or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    local safe  = typed ~= "" and P1.sanitizeName(typed) or "default"
    local found = false
    for _, v in ipairs(list) do
        if v == safe then found = true; break end
    end
    if not found then
        safe = "default"
        Flags.CfgPreset = safe
    end

    if widget.set then
        pcall(widget.set, safe)
    elseif widget.refresh then
        pcall(widget.refresh)
    end
end

-- Load named preset
local function loadNamed(name, runOn)
    local typed = tostring(name or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    local safe  = typed ~= "" and P1.sanitizeName(typed) or "default"

    local data = loadFromFile(CONFIG_NAMED .. "/" .. safe .. ".json")
    if not data then
        warn("[Kira/cfg] no named config:", safe)
        return false
    end

    applyConfig(data, runOn)

    Flags.Flight = false
    if Widgets.Flight and Widgets.Flight.set then
        pcall(Widgets.Flight.set, false)
    end

    Flags.CfgPreset = safe

    if P1.setTheme then pcall(P1.setTheme, Flags.Theme or "Dark") end

    local scale = tonumber(Flags.UIScale)
    if scale then UIScale.Scale = scale / 100 end

    if P1.saveConfig then pcall(P1.saveConfig, true) end
    print("[Kira/cfg] loaded named:", safe)
    return true
end

-- Auto-load on startup
local function autoLoad(runOn)
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" then
        warn("[Kira/cfg] readfile missing")
        return false
    end

    local playerName = P1.sanitizeName(LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name or "Player")

    -- 1. Try cache by name
    local data, path = loadFromFile(CONFIG_CACHE .. "/" .. playerName .. "-config.json")

    -- 2. Try cache by uid
    if not data then
        data, path = loadFromFile(CONFIG_CACHE .. "/" .. P1.UserIdStr .. "-config.json")
    end

    -- 3. Try named "default"
    if not data then
        data, path = loadFromFile(CONFIG_NAMED .. "/default.json")
    end

    -- 4. Legacy paths
    if not data then
        local legacy = {
            CONFIG_GAME .. "/config.json",
            "Kira/config.json",
            "Kira/" .. META.Game .. "/config.json",
            CONFIG_GAME .. "/Kira.json",
            "Kira/Kira.json",
            "Kira.json",
        }
        for _, p in ipairs(legacy) do
            data, path = loadFromFile(p)
            if data then break end
        end
    end

    if not data then
        refreshCfgList()
        return false
    end

    applyConfig(data, runOn)

    Flags.Flight = false
    if Widgets.Flight and Widgets.Flight.set then
        pcall(Widgets.Flight.set, false)
    end

    if P1.setTheme then pcall(P1.setTheme, Flags.Theme or "Dark") end

    local scale = tonumber(Flags.UIScale)
    if scale then UIScale.Scale = scale / 100 end

    if Flags.StartMin and P1.setVisible then
        pcall(P1.setVisible, false)
    end

    -- Re-apply every widget set() to be safe
    for k, w in pairs(Widgets) do
        if w and w.set and Flags[k] ~= nil then
            if w.kind == "toggle" then
                pcall(w.set, Flags[k] == true)
            elseif w.kind == "choice" or w.kind == "dropdown"
                or w.kind == "input" or w.kind == "slider" then
                pcall(w.set, Flags[k])
            end
        end
    end

    if P1.currentPage and P1.setPage then
        local cp = P1.currentPage()
        if cp then P1.setPage(cp.name) end
    end

    if P1.refreshCfgList then pcall(P1.refreshCfgList) end

    if path ~= (CONFIG_CACHE .. "/" .. playerName .. "-config.json") then
        saveConfig(true)
    end

    print("[Kira/cfg] loaded:", path)
    return true
end

-- Expose to P1
P1.collectFlags    = collectFlags
P1.saveConfig      = saveConfig
P1.saveNamed       = saveNamed
P1.loadNamed       = loadNamed
P1.autoLoad        = autoLoad
P1.applyConfig     = applyConfig
P1.refreshCfgList  = refreshCfgList
P1.listPresets     = listPresets
P1.ensureDirs      = ensureDirs
