--=====================================================================
bindOn("SaveCfgAs", function()
    saveNamed(Flags.CfgSaveName)
end)

bindOn("ExportCfg", function()
    if P1.saveConfig then pcall(P1.saveConfig, true) end
    local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONEncode(collectFlags())
    end)
    if ok and encoded and setclipboard then
        pcall(setclipboard, encoded)
    end
end)

bindOn("ImportCfg", function()
    local pasted = Flags.ImportPaste
    if type(pasted) ~= "string" or pasted == "" then return end

    local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(pasted)
    end)
    if not ok or type(decoded) ~= "table" then return end

    applyConfig(decoded, true)
    if P1.setTheme then pcall(P1.setTheme, Flags.Theme or "Dark") end
    if P1.saveConfig then pcall(P1.saveConfig, true) end
end)

--=====================================================================
--  [9] Theme swap function (final)
--=====================================================================
local function setTheme(name)
    if name == "Dusk" or name == "dusk" then name = "Dark" end
    local palette = THEMES[name] or THEMES.Dark

    local key
    if palette == THEMES.Light then
        key = "Light"
    else
        key = "Dark"
        palette = THEMES.Dark
    end

    Flags.Theme = key

    for k, v in pairs(palette) do
        ActiveTheme[k] = v
    end

    local function paint(inst)
        local th_bg = inst:GetAttribute("th_bg")
        if th_bg and ActiveTheme[th_bg] and inst:IsA("GuiObject") then
            local t = P1.tweens and P1.tweens[inst]
            if t then
                pcall(function() t:Cancel() end)
                P1.tweens[inst] = nil
            end
            local th_hover = inst:GetAttribute("th_hover")
            local isOver   = inst:GetAttribute("th_over") == true
            inst.BackgroundColor3 = isOver
                and (th_hover and ActiveTheme[th_hover])
                or ActiveTheme[th_bg]
            if inst:GetAttribute("th_row") then
                inst.BackgroundTransparency = (not isOver) and 1 or 0
            end
        end

        local th_text = inst:GetAttribute("th_text")
        if th_text and ActiveTheme[th_text] then
            pcall(function() inst.TextColor3 = ActiveTheme[th_text] end)
        end

        if inst:IsA("TextBox") then
            local th_ph = inst:GetAttribute("th_placeholder")
            if th_ph and ActiveTheme[th_ph] then
                inst.PlaceholderColor3 = ActiveTheme[th_ph]
            end
        end

        if inst:IsA("UIStroke") then
            local th_stroke = inst:GetAttribute("th_stroke")
            if th_stroke and ActiveTheme[th_stroke] then
                inst.Color = ActiveTheme[th_stroke]
            end
        end

        if inst:IsA("ImageLabel") then
            local th_img = inst:GetAttribute("th_img")
            if th_img and ActiveTheme[th_img] then
                inst.ImageColor3 = ActiveTheme[th_img]
            end
        end

        if inst:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
            local th_scroll = inst:GetAttribute("th_scroll")
            if th_scroll and ActiveTheme[th_scroll] then
                inst.ScrollBarImageColor3 = ActiveTheme[th_scroll]
            end
        end
    end

    paint(Window)
    if P1.Orb then paint(P1.Orb) end

    for _, d in ipairs(P1.ScreenGui:GetDescendants()) do
        paint(d)
    end

    if P1.refreshLogoTheme then pcall(P1.refreshLogoTheme) end

    if P1.searchIcon then
        -- Recolor search icon if needed
    end

    -- Force scrollbar refresh
    for _, p in pairs(P1.Pages) do
        local s = p.scroll
        if s then
            local cur = s.CanvasPosition
            s.CanvasPosition = cur + Vector2.new(0, 1)
            s.CanvasPosition = cur
        end
    end

    if P1.currentPage and P1.setPage then
        local cp = P1.currentPage()
        if cp then P1.setPage(cp.name) end
    end

    for k, w in pairs(Widgets) do
        if w.kind == "toggle" and w.set then
            w.set(Flags[k] == true)
        end
    end
end

P1.setTheme = setTheme

--=====================================================================
--  [10] Auto load config on boot
--=====================================================================
pcall(function()
    P1.autoLoad(true)
end)

print("[Kira] Part 4/9 loaded — Plot + Serverhop + Misc + Webhook + Settings + Config system ready.")aded — About + Auto Steal built.")
--=====================================================================
--  Kira Hub :: Steal an Egg
--  Decoded — Part 5/9
--  Movement Engine + Anti-Trap + Anti-Ragdoll
--=====================================================================

local P1 = KIRA_PART1
assert(P1, "Part 1 must be loaded first")
assert(P1.pages, "Part 3 must be loaded first")

local META      = P1.META
local Flags     = P1.Flags
local Widgets   = P1.Widgets
local LocalPlayer = P1.LocalPlayer

--=====================================================================
--  [1] Shared state for the engine
--=====================================================================
local engine = {
    on        = false,      -- u1116
    loaded    = true,       -- u1117 (set false on unload)
    conns     = {},         -- t152 : tracked connections
    states    = {},         -- t153 : discovered validator states
    confirmed = {},         -- t154 : confirmed states (with LastGoodSample/SafeGroundCheckpoints)
    wraps     = {},         -- t155 : hookfunction old/new pairs
    dt        = 1/60,       -- n7
    target    = nil,        -- u1127 : current destination
    driving   = false,      -- u1128 : whether we're using movement engine
    baseWalk  = 16,         -- n9 : recorded base walkspeed
    -- Engine callbacks (set by Parts 6+)
    stealTick = nil,        -- u1132
    stealState= nil,        -- t216
    esp       = nil,        -- u1134
    plot      = nil,        -- u1135
    wasOn     = false,      -- u1145
    -- Anti-trap
    untrapArmed = false,    -- u1157
    untrapCount = 0,        -- n14
    defaultJumpHeight = 7.2,-- n15
    -- KiraSupport
    support   = nil,        -- u1119
    supportY  = nil,        -- u1120
    -- Stepped loop
    steppedConn = nil,      -- u1118
}
P1.engine = engine

local function trackConn(conn)
    if conn then
        engine.conns[conn] = true
    end
    return conn
end

local function disconnectAll()
    for conn in pairs(engine.conns) do
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    engine.conns = {}
end

--=====================================================================
--  [2] Character helpers
--=====================================================================
local function getChar()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return nil, nil, nil end

    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root     = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not humanoid or not root or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return nil, nil, nil
    end
    return humanoid, root, char
end

local function cameraLookFlat()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end

    local look = Vector3.new(cam.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, cam.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
    if look.Magnitude < 0.001 then
        return Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    end
    return look.Unit
end

--=====================================================================
--  [3] Validator detection
--=====================================================================
local VALIDATOR_CONSTS = {
    "VerticalTrajectory",
    "CorrectionContext",
    "PivotTo",
    "Relocate",
    "Authoritative WalkSpeed",
    "BeginImpulse",
    "LastValidatedGroundedSample",
}

local SAMPLE_KEYS = {
    "LastGoodSample",
    "LastObservedSample",
    "LastValidatedSample",
    "LastValidatedGroundedSample",
    "LastConfirmedGroundSample",
    "LastSample",
    "LastGameplayTrustedSample",
}

local function readConstants(fn, limit)
    limit = limit or 16
    if not debug or not debug.getconstants then return {} end

    local ok, consts = pcall(debug.getconstants, fn)
    if not ok or type(consts) ~= "table" then return {} end

    local out = {}
    local n = 0
    for _, v in pairs(consts) do
        n = n + 1
        if n <= limit then
            out[#out + 1] = tostring(v)
        end
    end
    return out
end

local function looksLikeValidator(fn)
    local joined = table.concat(readConstants(fn, 50), "|")

    for _, key in ipairs(VALIDATOR_CONSTS) do
        if joined:find(key, 1, true) then
            return true, key
        end
    end

    if joined:find("WalkSpeed", 1, true)
        and joined:find("AssemblyLinearVelocity", 1, true)
        and joined:find("Magnitude", 1, true) then
        return true, "ALVvsWalkSpeed"
    end

    return false
end

--=====================================================================
--  [4] Sample rewriter
--=====================================================================
local function rewriteSample(sample, root, humanoid, pos, vel)
    if type(sample) ~= "table" or not root then return end

    local look = cameraLookFlat()

    -- Preserve fly look if set
    if engine.driving
        and Flags.StealTravel == "Flight"
        and not Flags.Flight
        and engine.stealState
        and typeof(engine.stealState.flyLook) == "Vector3" then
        look = engine.stealState.flyLook
    end

    local cf = CFrame.new(pos, pos + look)

    pcall(function()
        sample.Position    = pos
        sample.CFrame      = cf
        sample.LinearVelocity = vel
        sample.AngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        sample.IsSupported = true
        sample.Timestamp   = os.clock()

        if humanoid then
            sample.WalkSpeed  = humanoid.WalkSpeed
            sample.JumpPower  = humanoid.JumpPower
            sample.JumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight
            sample.UseJumpPower = humanoid.UseJumpPower
            sample.HumanoidState = Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running
        end

        sample.Gravity = workspace.Gravity
    end)
end

local function looksLikeGuardState(t)
    if type(t) ~= "table" then return false end
    local ok1, good = pcall(rawget, t, "LastGoodSample")
    local ok2, safe = pcall(rawget, t, "SafeGroundCheckpoints")
    return ok1 and type(good) == "table" and ok2 and type(safe) == "table"
end

local function patchValidatorState(state, root, humanoid, pos, vel)
    if not looksLikeGuardState(state) then return end

    -- Figure out expected hip height
    local expectedHip
    if type(state.ExpectedHipHeight) == "number" then
        local halfRoot = type(state.ExpectedRootHalfHeight) == "number"
            and state.ExpectedRootHalfHeight or 1
        expectedHip = state.ExpectedHipHeight + halfRoot * 0.5
    else
        expectedHip = 2.51
    end

    pcall(function()
        local now = os.clock()

        state.MaxHorizontalSpeed = 1e7
        state.MaxVerticalSpeed   = 1e7
        state.IsSupportedNow     = true
        state.LastSupportedAt    = now
        state.LastCorrectionAt   = 0
        state.HighestYSinceGround= pos.Y
        state.MovementMode       = "Grounded"
        state.TelemetryRepeatCount = 0
        state.ValidationLocked   = false
        state.MonitorRunning     = false
        state.MonitorPending     = false
        state.ThreatLevel        = "Trusted"
        state.WasMeaningfullyFalling = false
        state.InitializingUntil  = now + 3600
        state.SupportStartedAt   = now
        state.ValidationStartedAt= now

        local witness = state.GroundContactWitness
        if type(witness) == "table" then
            witness.GroundDistance = expectedHip
            witness.GroundPosition = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y - expectedHip, pos.Z)
            if root.Parent then
                witness.Character = root.Parent
            end
            if type(witness.ContactSample) == "table" then
                rewriteSample(witness.ContactSample, root, humanoid, pos, vel)
            end
        end

        local ev = state.Evidence
        if type(ev) == "table" then
            ev.Speed = 0
            ev.Flight = 0
            ev.Teleport = 0
        end

        local vd = state.LastVerticalTrajectoryDecision
        if type(vd) == "table" then
            vd.Active = false
            vd.CorrectionStarted = false
            vd.ConsecutiveInvalidWindows = 0
            vd.EvidenceEventCount = 0
            vd.MeaningfulDescent = false
            vd.LandingCandidate = false
            vd.CurrentY = pos.Y
            vd.PeakY = pos.Y
            vd.StartY = pos.Y
            vd.AirborneDuration = 0
            vd.LandingDuration = 0
            vd.LatestLegalSampleAge = 0
            vd.AllowedRise = 1e7
            vd.AllowedAirborneDuration = 1e7
            vd.Evidence = 0
            vd.PrimaryEvidenceSource = "None"
        end

        local sd = state.LastVerticalSegmentDecision
        if type(sd) == "table" then
            sd.Active = false
            sd.CorrectionStarted = false
            sd.Displacement = 0
            sd.Excess = 0
            sd.AllowedDistance = 1e7
            sd.Decision = "Reachable"
            sd.CurrentY = pos.Y
            sd.PreviousY = pos.Y
            sd.MovementContext = "OrdinaryStationaryY"
        end
    end)

    for _, key in ipairs(SAMPLE_KEYS) do
        rewriteSample(state[key], root, humanoid, pos, vel)
    end

    if type(state.SampleHistory) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(state.SampleHistory) do
            if type(v) == "table" then
                rewriteSample(v, root, humanoid, pos, vel)
            end
        end
    end

    if type(state.SafeGroundCheckpoints) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(state.SafeGroundCheckpoints) do
            if type(v) == "table" then
                rewriteSample(v, root, humanoid, pos, vel)
            end
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [5] Discover validator states from PostSimulation
--=====================================================================
local function discoverStates()
    engine.states = {}

    if not getconnections then
        warn("[Kira/engine] no getconnections — validator wraps skipped")
        return 0
    end

    local seen = {}

    for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(RunService.PostSimulation)) do
        if not engine.conns[conn] and looksLikeValidator(conn.Function) then
            pcall(function() conn:Enable() end)

            for i = 1, 24 do
                local fn = conn.Function
                local ok, val = pcall(debug.getupvalue, fn, i)
                local actual = ok and val or nil

                if actual == nil then break end

                if type(actual) == "table" and not seen[actual] then
                    seen[actual] = true
                    engine.states[#engine.states + 1] = actual
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return #engine.states
end

local function confirmStates()
    local fresh = {}
    local seen  = {}

    -- From discovered states
    for _, state in ipairs(engine.states) do
        if looksLikeGuardState(state) and not seen[state] then
            seen[state] = true
            fresh[#fresh + 1] = state
        end
    end

    -- From existing confirmed (may have gone stale)
    for _, state in ipairs(engine.confirmed) do
        if looksLikeGuardState(state) and not seen[state] then
            seen[state] = true
            fresh[#fresh + 1] = state
        end
    end

    if #fresh > 0 then
        engine.confirmed = fresh
    end

    return #engine.confirmed
end

--=====================================================================
--  [6] KiraSupport (ghost platform under player)
--=====================================================================
local function cleanupSupport()
    engine.supportY = nil

    local hadSupport = engine.support ~= nil

    if engine.support then
        pcall(function() engine.support:Destroy() end)
        engine.support = nil
    end

    if hadSupport then
        for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == "KiraSupport" or child.Name == "Hub45Support" then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function raycastFloorY(pos)
    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return nil end

    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

    local filter = { LocalPlayer.Character }
    if engine.support then filter[#filter + 1] = engine.support end
    if workspace.CurrentCamera then filter[#filter + 1] = workspace.CurrentCamera end

    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter

    local origin = Vector3.new(pos.X, math.max(pos.Y + 48, 80), pos.Z)
    local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -360, 0), params)

    if result and result.Instance and result.Instance.CanCollide then
        return result.Position.Y
    end
    if result then
        return result.Position.Y
    end

    return pos.Y
end

local function updateSupport(root, dyHint)
    if not root then return end

    if not engine.support or not engine.support.Parent then
        cleanupSupport()

        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = "KiraSupport"
        part.Size = Vector3.new(8, 1.2, 8)
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = true
        part.CanQuery = true
        part.CanTouch = false
        part.CastShadow = false
        part.Transparency = 1
        part.Material = Enum.Material.Concrete
        part.Parent = workspace
        engine.support = part
    end

    -- Expected hip offset
    local firstState = engine.confirmed[1]
    local expectedHip
    if type(firstState) == "table" and type(firstState.ExpectedHipHeight) == "number" then
        local half = type(firstState.ExpectedRootHalfHeight) == "number"
            and firstState.ExpectedRootHalfHeight or 1
        expectedHip = firstState.ExpectedHipHeight + half * 0.5
    else
        expectedHip = 2.51
    end

    local clearance = math.max(3.8, expectedHip)

    if engine.driving
        and typeof(engine.target) == "Vector3"
        and type(P1.isFlying) == "function"
        and P1.isFlying() then
        engine.supportY = root.Position.Y - clearance - 0.6
        engine.support.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, engine.supportY, root.Position.Z)
        return
    end

    local desiredY = root.Position.Y - clearance - 0.6
    local hint = tonumber(dyHint) or 0

    if engine.supportY == nil then
        engine.supportY = desiredY
    elseif hint > 8 then
        engine.supportY = math.max(engine.supportY, desiredY)
    elseif hint < -8 then
        engine.supportY = desiredY
    else
        local checkY = engine.supportY + 0.6
        if root.Position.Y - checkY > clearance + 8 then
            engine.supportY = desiredY
        end
    end

    engine.support.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, engine.supportY, root.Position.Z)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [7] Physics: velocity cap + state patch loop
--=====================================================================
local function capVelocity(root, humanoid, vel)
    local walk = humanoid.WalkSpeed
    local limit = math.max(10, walk or 16)

    local capped = vel.Magnitude > limit + 1 and vel.Unit * limit or vel

    pcall(function()
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = capped
    end)

    for _, state in ipairs(engine.confirmed) do
        patchValidatorState(state, root, humanoid, root.Position, capped)
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [8] Wrap PostSimulation validators
--=====================================================================
local function installWraps(enabled)
    if enabled then
        if not hookfunction or not getconnections then
            warn("[Kira/engine] cannot wrap PostSim validators")
            return 0
        end

        local wrapped = 0

        for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(RunService.PostSimulation)) do
            if not engine.conns[conn] then
                local isValidator, _ = looksLikeValidator(conn.Function)
                local fn = conn.Function

                if isValidator and not engine.wraps[fn] then
                    local entry = { old = fn }

                    local function wrapper(...)
                        local humanoid, root = getChar()

                        local savedRoot, savedVel, savedCF
                        if root and humanoid then
                            savedRoot = root
                            savedVel  = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            savedCF   = root.CFrame

                            if P1.isFlying and P1.isFlying() then
                                updateSupport(root, 0)
                            end

                            capVelocity(root, humanoid, savedVel)
                        end

                        local packed
                        local ok, err = pcall(function(...)
                            packed = table.pack(entry.old(...))
                        end, ...)

                        if savedRoot and savedCF then
                            local dist = (savedRoot.Position - savedCF.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist > 1.5 then
                                pcall(function()
                                    savedRoot.CFrame = savedCF
                                    savedRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = savedVel
                                end)
                                if os.clock() - (engine._lastUndoLog or 0) > 1 then
                                    engine._lastUndoLog = os.clock()
                                    warn("[Kira/engine] undid relocate:", math.floor(dist * 10 + 0.5) / 10)
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        if savedRoot and humanoid then
                            capVelocity(savedRoot, humanoid, savedVel or savedRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity)
                        end

                        if savedRoot and savedVel then
                            pcall(function()
                                savedRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = savedVel
                            end)
                        end

                        if not ok then error(err) end
                        return table.unpack(packed, 1, packed.n)
                    end

                    if type(newcclosure) == "function" then
                        wrapper = newcclosure(wrapper)
                    end

                    local okHook, oldFn = pcall(hookfunction, fn, wrapper)
                    if okHook then
                        entry.old = oldFn or fn
                        engine.wraps[fn] = entry.old
                        wrapped = wrapped + 1
                    else
                        warn("[Kira/engine] wrap fail:", tostring(oldFn))
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return wrapped
    end

    -- Unwrap
    local restore = restorefunction
    for fn, old in pairs(engine.wraps) do
        if restore then
            pcall(restore, fn)
        elseif hookfunction and old then
            pcall(hookfunction, fn, old)
        end
    end
    engine.wraps = {}
    warn("[Kira/engine] validator wraps restored")
    return 0
end

--=====================================================================
--  [9] Movement helpers
--=====================================================================
local function currentSpeed()
    if Flags.Flight then
        return math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.FlightSpeed) or 880, 150, 1300)
    end
    if engine.driving and typeof(engine.target) == "Vector3" then
        return math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.StealSpeed) or 300, 50, 1300)
    end
    if Flags.BypassSpeed == true then
        return math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.BypassCap) or 880, 150, 1300)
    end
    return engine.baseWalk
end

function P1.isFlying()
    if Flags.Flight then return true end
    if engine.plot and engine.plot.driving and engine.plot.driving() then return true end
    if engine.driving and Flags.StealTravel == "Flight" and typeof(engine.target) == "Vector3" then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function wasdVector()
    local look = cameraLookFlat()
    local right = Vector3.new(-look.Z, 0, look.X)
    local vec = Vector3.zero

    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then vec += look end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then vec -= look end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then vec += right end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then vec -= right end

    if vec.Magnitude < 0.001 then return Vector3.zero end
    return vec.Unit
end

local function flightVelocity()
    local speed = currentSpeed()
    local dir = wasdVector()
    local dy = 0

    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
        dy = 40
    elseif UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
        dy = -40
    end

    if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
        return Vector3.new(dir.X * speed, dy, dir.Z * speed)
    end
    return Vector3.new(0, dy, 0)
end

local function computeVelocity(humanoid, root)
    if Flags.Flight and (not engine.driving or not engine.target) then
        return flightVelocity()
    end

    local speed = currentSpeed()

    if engine.driving and engine.target then
        local toTarget = engine.target - root.Position
        local flat = Vector3.new(toTarget.X, 0, toTarget.Z)
        local dist = flat.Magnitude
        local dy = 0

        if P1.isFlying() then
            dy = 0

            if engine.driving then
                local floor = raycastFloorY(Vector3.new(root.Position.X, root.Position.Y + 4, root.Position.Z))
                if type(floor) == "number" and floor <= root.Position.Y + 1 and root.Position.Y > floor + 14 then
                    dy = math.clamp(floor + 3 - root.Position.Y, -28, 0)
                end
            elseif root.Position.Y > engine.target.Y + 0.6 then
                dy = math.clamp(engine.target.Y - root.Position.Y, -36, 0)
            end
        end

        if dist < 1.4 then
            if P1.isFlying() then return Vector3.new(0, dy, 0) end
            return Vector3.zero
        end

        local horiz = dist < 8
            and math.clamp(speed * (dist / 8), 18, speed)
            or speed

        return Vector3.new(flat.Unit.X * horiz, dy, flat.Unit.Z * horiz)
    end

    if engine.driving and P1.isFlying() then
        return Vector3.zero
    end

    if Flags.BypassSpeed == true then
        local md = humanoid.MoveDirection
        if md.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            return Vector3.new(md.X * speed, 0, md.Z * speed)
        end
    end

    return Vector3.zero
end

--=====================================================================
--  [10] Humanoid state restore
--=====================================================================
local function restoreHumanoid(humanoid, root, forceFall)
    cleanupSupport()

    if not humanoid or not root then
        local h, r = getChar()
        humanoid, root = h, r
    end
    if not humanoid then return end

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        humanoid.Sit = false
        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
        humanoid.AutoJumpEnabled = true
        humanoid.Jump = false

        if type(humanoid.WalkSpeed) == "number"
            and humanoid.WalkSpeed > 0
            and humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 36 then
            engine.baseWalk = humanoid.WalkSpeed
        end

        humanoid.WalkSpeed = engine.baseWalk

        if forceFall then
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall)
        end
    end)

    if root then
        pcall(function()
            root.Anchored = false
            if forceFall then
                local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, math.min(v.Y, -22), 0)
            end
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [11] Anti-Trap (v1158)
--=====================================================================
local function antiTrap(freezeReason)
    if not Flags.AntiTrap then
        engine.untrapArmed = false
        return false
    end

    local humanoid, root, char = getChar()
    if not humanoid or not root then return false end

    -- Remember default jump height
    if type(humanoid.JumpHeight) == "number" and humanoid.JumpHeight > 0.5 then
        engine.defaultJumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight
    end

    local flying = P1.isFlying()
    local isTrapped = char:GetAttribute("IsTrapped") == true
    local frozen = root.Anchored or humanoid.JumpHeight == 0

    if not isTrapped and not frozen then
        engine.untrapArmed = false
        return false
    end

    pcall(function()
        if isTrapped then
            char:SetAttribute("IsTrapped", nil)
        end

        root.Anchored = false

        if not flying then
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        end

        humanoid.Sit = false

        if engine.driving and Flags.StealTravel == "Flight" and not Flags.Flight then
            humanoid.AutoRotate = false
        else
            humanoid.AutoRotate = true
        end

        if humanoid.JumpHeight < 0.5 then
            humanoid.JumpHeight = engine.defaultJumpHeight
        end

        local state = humanoid:GetState()
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated then
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local bb = char:FindFirstChild("TrapBillboard", true)
        if bb then bb:Destroy() end
    end)

    if not engine.untrapArmed then
        engine.untrapArmed = true
        engine.untrapCount += 1
        warn("[Kira/steal] untrap", freezeReason or "freeze", engine.untrapCount)
    end

    return true
end

P1.antiTrap = antiTrap

--=====================================================================
--  [12] Character collision mute (anti-trap support)
--=====================================================================
local charMute = { saved = {}, char = nil, at = 0 }

local function updateCharMute()
    local shouldMute = (Flags.AntiTrap == true or Flags.AntiMob == true)

    if not shouldMute and not next(charMute.saved) then
        return
    end

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character

    if char ~= charMute.char then
        charMute.saved = {}
        charMute.char = char
        charMute.at = 0
        if not shouldMute then return end
    end

    if not char then return end

    if shouldMute then
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - (charMute.at or 0) < 0.25 then return end
        charMute.at = now
    end

    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart") then
            if shouldMute then
                if charMute.saved[d] == nil then
                    charMute.saved[d] = d.CanTouch
                end
                if d.CanTouch ~= false then
                    d.CanTouch = false
                end
            else
                local prev = charMute.saved[d]
                if prev ~= nil then
                    d.CanTouch = prev
                    charMute.saved[d] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if not shouldMute then
        charMute.saved = {}
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [13] Belt / treadmill detection
--=====================================================================
local function raycastBeltCheck(root, dir, maxDist)
    if not root or not dir or not (dir.Magnitude > 0.05) then return false end

    local dist = maxDist or 12
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

    local filter = { LocalPlayer.Character }
    if workspace.CurrentCamera then filter[#filter + 1] = workspace.CurrentCamera end
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter

    local origin = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.2, 0)
    local result = workspace:Raycast(origin, dir.Unit * dist, params)

    if not result or not result.Instance or not result.Instance.CanCollide then
        return false
    end

    local inst = result.Instance
    local fullName = inst:GetFullName()
    local lowName  = string.lower(inst.Name)
    local lowParent = inst.Parent and string.lower(inst.Parent.Name) or ""
    local size = inst.Size
    local normal = result.Normal

    local isBarrier = fullName:find("LobbyBoundaries", 1, true)
        or fullName:find("MapBound", 1, true)
        or lowName:find("boundar", 1, true)
        or lowName:find("mapedge", 1, true)
        or (size.Y >= 24 and math.min(size.X, size.Z) <= 14
            and math.max(size.X, size.Z) >= 40)

    if lowName:find("treadmill", 1, true)
        or lowName:find("belt", 1, true)
        or fullName:find("Treadmill", 1, true)
        or lowParent:find("treadmill", 1, true) then
        return true, Vector3.new(normal.X, 0, normal.Z), "belt"
    end

    if not isBarrier then return false end

    return true, Vector3.new(normal.X, 0, normal.Z)
end

P1.raycastBeltCheck = raycastBeltCheck

--=====================================================================
--  [14] Anti-Ragdoll (AntiMob)
--=====================================================================
local antiMob = {
    saved        = {},
    at           = 0,
    ragOff       = false,
    patched      = {},
    muted        = {},
    groups       = false,
    strikeWrapped= false,
    components   = {},
    runtimes     = {},
    hitArmed     = false,
}

local function restoreGuards()
    for part, data in pairs(antiMob.saved) do
        if part.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                part.CanCollide = data.collide
                part.CanTouch   = data.touch
                if data.group then
                    part.CollisionGroup = data.group
                end
            end)
        end
        antiMob.saved[part] = nil
    end
end

local function muteGuard(guard)
    for _, d in ipairs(guard:GetDescendants()) do
        local ok, isPart = pcall(function() return d:IsA("BasePart") end)
        if ok and isPart then
            if antiMob.saved[d] == nil then
                antiMob.saved[d] = {
                    collide = d.CanCollide,
                    touch   = d.CanTouch,
                    group   = d.CollisionGroup,
                }
            end
            pcall(function()
                d.CanTouch = false
                d.CanCollide = false
            end)
            pcall(function()
                d.CollisionGroup = "GuardsNoCollide"
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function setGuardGroups(muted)
    local PhysicsService = game:GetService("PhysicsService")
    pcall(function()
        PhysicsService:RegisterCollisionGroup("Guards")
        PhysicsService:RegisterCollisionGroup("Players")
        PhysicsService:RegisterCollisionGroup("GuardsNoCollide")
    end)
    pcall(function()
        PhysicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable("Guards", "Players", not muted)
        PhysicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable("Players", "Guards", not muted)
        PhysicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable("GuardsNoCollide", "Players", false)
        PhysicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable("Players", "GuardsNoCollide", false)
        PhysicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable("GuardsNoCollide", "Default", false)
        PhysicsService:CollisionGroupSetCollidable("GuardsNoCollide", "Guards", false)
    end)
    antiMob.groups = muted == true
end

local function requireShared(path)
    local cur = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
    for i = 1, #path do
        if not cur then return end
        cur = cur:FindFirstChild(path[i])
    end
    if cur and cur:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, cur)
        if ok then return mod end
    end
end

local function patchFn(tbl, key, wrapper)
    if type(tbl) ~= "table" or type(tbl[key]) ~= "function" then return false end

    local tag = tostring(tbl) .. "." .. tostring(key)
    if antiMob.patched[tag] then return true end

    local wrapped = wrapper(tbl[key])
    local final
    local ok
    local candidate
    repeat
        if final or type(newcclosure) == "function" then
            if not final then
                ok, candidate = pcall(newcclosure, wrapped)
            end
            if final or (ok and type(candidate) == "function") then
                final = false
                tbl[key] = candidate
                antiMob.patched[tag] = true
                return true
            end
        end
        candidate = wrapped
        final = true
    until not final
end

local function getRemote(x)
    if typeof(x) == "Instance" then return x end
    if type(x) ~= "table" then return nil end

    for _, k in ipairs({ "Remote", "remote", "Instance", "_remote", "_instance", "Event" }) do
        local v = x[k]
        if typeof(v) == "Instance" then return v end
    end
end

local function muteRemoteEvents(remote)
    local inst = getRemote(remote) or (typeof(remote) == "Instance" and remote)
    if not inst or antiMob.muted[inst] then return end

    local evt = inst.OnClientEvent
    if not evt or not getconnections then return end

    local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, evt)
    if not ok or type(conns) ~= "table" then return end

    local list = {}
    for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
        pcall(function()
            if c.Disable then c:Disable() end
        end)
        list[#list + 1] = c
    end
    antiMob.muted[inst] = list
end

local function restoreRemoteEvents()
    for inst, list in pairs(antiMob.muted) do
        for _, c in ipairs(list) do
            pcall(function()
                if c.Enable then c:Enable() end
            end)
        end
        antiMob.muted[inst] = nil
    end
end

local function disableAttackComponents(reallyDisable)
    if not reallyDisable then
        for comp, data in pairs(antiMob.components) do
            pcall(function()
                if data.handler then comp._attackHandler = data.handler end
                comp._enabled = data.enabled ~= false
            end)
            antiMob.components[comp] = nil
        end

        for rt in pairs(antiMob.runtimes) do
            pcall(function() rt:SetEnabled(true) end)
            antiMob.runtimes[rt] = nil
        end
        return
    end

    antiMob.noopAttack = antiMob.noopAttack or function() end

    for comp in pairs(antiMob.components) do
        comp._attackHandler = antiMob.noopAttack
        comp._enabled = false
    end
end

local function installAntiMobPatches()
    antiMob.noopAttack = antiMob.noopAttack or function() end

    for comp in pairs(antiMob.components) do
        comp._attackHandler = antiMob.noopAttack
        comp._enabled = false
    end

    -- GuardAreas.GuardDistance.XZ
    local guardDistance = requireShared({ "Modules", "GuardAreas", "GuardDistance" })
    local patchedXZ = patchFn(guardDistance, "XZ", function(orig)
        return function(a, b)
            if Flags.AntiMob then return 1000000000 end
            return orig(a, b)
        end
    end)

    -- GuardComponent._attemptAttack
    local guardComp = requireShared({ "Modules", "GuardAreas", "GuardComponent" })
    local patchedAtk = patchFn(guardComp, "_attemptAttack", function(orig)
        return function(...)
            if Flags.AntiMob then return end
            return orig(...)
        end
    end)

    patchFn(guardComp, "Step", function(orig)
        return function(self, ...)
            if Flags.AntiMob then return nil end
            return orig(self, ...)
        end
    end)

    -- Ragdoll module
    local ragdoll = requireShared({ "Modules", "Ragdoll" })

    patchFn(ragdoll, "IsRagdolled", function(orig)
        return function(char, ...)
            if Flags.AntiMob and char == LocalPlayer.Character then
                return false
            end
            return orig(char, ...)
        end
    end)

    for _, name in ipairs({
        "TimedRagdoll", "TimedRagdollAsync", "ApplyClientRagdoll", "Ragdoll"
    }) do
        patchFn(ragdoll, name, function(orig)
            return function(char, ...)
                if Flags.AntiMob then
                    local me = LocalPlayer.Character
                    if char == nil or char == me then return end
                end
                return orig(char, ...)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- RagdollJoints
    local joints = requireShared({ "Modules", "RagdollJoints" })
    patchFn(joints, "Bind", function(orig)
        return function(...)
            if Flags.AntiMob then return end
            return orig(...)
        end
    end)

    -- Remotes.GuardPatrol
    pcall(function()
        local remotes = requireShared({ "Remotes" })
        local gp = remotes and remotes.GuardPatrol
        if type(gp) ~= "table" then return end

        for k, v in pairs(gp) do
            local str = tostring(k)
            local inst = getRemote(v) or (typeof(v) == "Instance" and v)

            if str:find("Strike", 1, true) or str:find("Handoff", 1, true) then
                local tag = "gpfs." .. str
                if not antiMob.patched[tag] then
                    if inst and typeof(inst) == "Instance" then
                        gp[k] = setmetatable({
                            FireServer = function(_, ...)
                                if Flags.AntiMob then return end
                                return inst:FireServer(...)
                            end,
                        }, { __index = inst })
                        antiMob.patched[tag] = true
                    elseif type(v) == "table" and type(v.FireServer) == "function" then
                        patchFn(v, "FireServer", function(orig)
                            return function(...)
                                if Flags.AntiMob then return end
                                return orig(...)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

            if str:find("Strike", 1, true)
                or str:find("Handoff", 1, true)
                or str:find("SpeedToll", 1, true)
                or str:find("SpeedHit", 1, true)
                or str:find("Ragdoll", 1, true)
                or str:find("Limp", 1, true)
                or str:find("Slap", 1, true)
                or str:find("Jolt", 1, true) then
                muteRemoteEvents(inst or v)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Limpness.WriteLimpness / SharedFx.JoltOnce
    pcall(function()
        local remotes = requireShared({ "Remotes" })
        if type(remotes) ~= "table" then return end

        local limp = remotes.Limpness
        muteRemoteEvents(limp and limp.WriteLimpness)

        local fx = remotes.SharedFx
        muteRemoteEvents(fx and fx.JoltOnce)
    end)

    -- Scan Network / Packages.Networking for other strike events
    pcall(function()
        local roots = {}
        local net = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
        if net then roots[#roots + 1] = net end
        local pkg = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
        local nw = pkg and pkg:FindFirstChild("Networking")
        if nw then roots[#roots + 1] = nw end

        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    local n = d.Name
                    if n:find("Strike", 1, true)
                        or n:find("SpeedToll", 1, true)
                        or n:find("SpeedHit", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Handoff", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Ragdoll", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Limp", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Slap", 1, true)
                        or n:find("Jolt", 1, true) then
                        muteRemoteEvents(d)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- EggState.DropFieldEgg patch
    pcall(function()
        local eggState = P1.eggStateModule
        if not eggState or antiMob.patched["EggState.DropFieldEgg"] then return end

        patchFn(eggState, "DropFieldEgg", function(orig)
            return function(reason, ...)
                if Flags.AntiMob and (reason == "GuardHit" or reason == "PlayerSlap") then
                    return
                end
                if Flags.AutoSteal and engine.stealState and engine.stealState.running then
                    return
                end
                return orig(reason, ...)
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- EggWorld.AskFieldEggDrop
    pcall(function()
        local remotes = requireShared({ "Remotes" })
        local ew = remotes and remotes.EggWorld
        local ask = ew and ew.AskFieldEggDrop
        local inst = getRemote(ask) or (typeof(ask) == "Instance" and ask)

        if inst and typeof(inst) == "Instance" and not antiMob.patched.AskFieldEggDrop then
            ew.AskFieldEggDrop = setmetatable({
                InvokeServer = function(_, arg1, ...)
                    local reason = type(arg1) == "table" and arg1.Reason
                    if Flags.AntiMob and (reason == "GuardHit" or reason == "PlayerSlap") then
                        return
                    end
                    if Flags.AutoSteal and engine.stealState and engine.stealState.running then
                        return
                    end
                    return inst:InvokeServer(arg1, ...)
                end,
            }, { __index = inst })
            antiMob.patched.AskFieldEggDrop = true
            return
        end

        if type(ask) == "table" then
            patchFn(ask, "InvokeServer", function(orig)
                return function(self, arg1, ...)
                    local reason = type(arg1) == "table" and arg1.Reason
                    if Flags.AntiMob and (reason == "GuardHit" or reason == "PlayerSlap") then
                        return
                    end
                    if Flags.AutoSteal and engine.stealState and engine.stealState.running then
                        return
                    end
                    return orig(self, arg1, ...)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    -- Attribute change listener
    if not antiMob.attrConn then
        local conn = LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("RagdollEndTime"):Connect(function()
            if Flags.AntiMob then
                pcall(P1.ragdollRescue)
            end
        end)
        trackConn(conn)
        antiMob.attrConn = conn
    end

    local status = (not patchedXZ and "no-xz" or "xz")
        .. " " .. (not patchedAtk and "no-atk" or "atk")

    if status ~= antiMob.logged then
        antiMob.logged = status
        warn("[Kira/engine] anti ragdoll patch", status)
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [15] Ragdoll rescue
--=====================================================================
local function ragdollRescue()
    local humanoid, root, char = getChar()
    if not humanoid then return end

    local state = humanoid:GetState()
    local ragdollEnd = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")

    if type(ragdollEnd) ~= "number" and char then
        ragdollEnd = char:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
    end

    local serverSays = type(ragdollEnd) == "number"
        and ragdollEnd > workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - 0.05

    if not serverSays
        and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
        return
    end

    antiMob.ragOff = true
    local flying = P1.isFlying and P1.isFlying()

    pcall(function()
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)

        if flying then
            humanoid.PlatformStand = true
            return
        end

        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        humanoid.Sit = false
    end)

    if char then
        if not antiMob.joints then
            antiMob.joints = requireShared({ "Modules", "RagdollJoints" })
        end
        local joints = antiMob.joints
        if joints and type(joints.Release) == "function" then
            pcall(joints.Release, char)
        end

        pcall(function()
            char:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", 0)
        end)
    end

    if serverSays then
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", 0)
        end)
    end

    if root and not flying then
        pcall(function()
            local md = humanoid.MoveDirection
            local ws = humanoid.WalkSpeed or 0
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(md.X * ws, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, md.Z * ws)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
end

P1.ragdollRescue = ragdollRescue

--=====================================================================
--  [16] Anti-Mob tick
--=====================================================================
local function antiMobTick()
    if Flags.AntiMob ~= true then
        if not antiMob.hitArmed
            and not antiMob.ragOff
            and not antiMob.groups
            and not next(antiMob.saved) then
            return
        end

        restoreRemoteEvents()
        disableAttackComponents(false)

        if antiMob.groups then
            setGuardGroups(false)
        end
        if next(antiMob.saved) then
            restoreGuards()
        end
        if antiMob.ragOff then
            local h = select(1, getChar())
            if h then
                pcall(function()
                    h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
                    h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
                    h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, true)
                    h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                end)
            end
            antiMob.ragOff = false
        end
        antiMob.hitArmed = false
        return
    end

    if not antiMob.hitArmed then
        installAntiMobPatches()
        antiMob.hitArmed = true
    end

    if not antiMob.groups then
        setGuardGroups(true)
    end

    local humanoid = select(1, getChar())
    if humanoid then
        if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
            pcall(function()
                if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                    humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
                end
                humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
            end)
        end

        ragdollRescue()

        if humanoid ~= antiMob.hum then
            antiMob.hum = humanoid
            if antiMob.stConn then pcall(function() antiMob.stConn:Disconnect() end) end
            if antiMob.hpConn then pcall(function() antiMob.hpConn:Disconnect() end) end

            antiMob.stConn = trackConn(humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                if Flags.AntiMob ~= true then return end
                if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
                    ragdollRescue()
                end
            end))

            antiMob.hpConn = trackConn(humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(hp)
                if Flags.AntiMob == true and hp < humanoid.MaxHealth then
                    pcall(function()
                        if hp <= 0 then
                            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
                        end
                        humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
                    end)
                end
            end))
        end
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (antiMob.muteAt or 0) < 0.35 then return end
    antiMob.muteAt = now

    pcall(function()
        local objRoot = workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
        local areas = objRoot and objRoot:FindFirstChild("Areas")
        local guards = (areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas"))
            or workspace:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
        if not guards then return end

        for _, child in ipairs(guards:GetChildren()) do
            local guard = child:FindFirstChild("Guard")
            if guard and guard:IsA("Model") then
                muteGuard(guard)
            end

            for _, sub in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                if sub:IsA("Model")
                    and sub ~= guard
                    and (string.lower(sub.Name):find("guard", 1, true)
                        or sub:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")) then
                    muteGuard(sub)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [17] Movement tick (v1182)
--=====================================================================
local function movementTick(dt)
    if not engine.loaded then return end
    engine.dt = dt or engine.dt

    local flying = P1.isFlying()
    local humanoid, root = getChar()

    if engine.wasOn and not flying then
        -- Engine turned off
        local leaving = engine.plot and engine.plot.leaving and engine.plot.leaving()
        restoreHumanoid(humanoid, root, not leaving)
        engine.wasOn = false
        pcall(antiTrap, "engine")
        pcall(updateCharMute)
        pcall(antiMobTick)
        return
    end

    engine.wasOn = flying

    if flying then
        if root then
            pcall(function()
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(currentSpeed(), 16, 1300)
                end
            end)

            local vel = computeVelocity(humanoid, root)
            local predicted = root.Position + vel * math.clamp(engine.dt, 0, 0.05)

            updateSupport(root, vel.Y)

            local walk = humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed or currentSpeed()
            local limit = math.max(10, walk or 16)
            local capped = vel.Magnitude > limit + 1 and vel.Unit * limit or vel

            for _, state in ipairs(engine.confirmed) do
                patchValidatorState(state, root, humanoid, predicted, capped)
            end

            setCFrameWithVelocity(humanoid, root, vel)
        else
            cleanupSupport()
        end

        pcall(antiTrap, "engine")
        pcall(updateCharMute)
        pcall(antiMobTick)
        muteCharCollisions(false)
        return
    end

    -- Not flying — engine idle
    cleanupSupport()
    antiTrap("engine")
    updateCharMute()
    antiMobTick()

    if humanoid and type(humanoid.WalkSpeed) == "number"
        and humanoid.WalkSpeed > 0 and humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 36 then
        engine.baseWalk = humanoid.WalkSpeed
    end

    local usingSteal = engine.driving and typeof(engine.target) == "Vector3"

    if not usingSteal and Flags.BypassSpeed ~= true and Flags.Flight ~= true then
        pcall(function()
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = engine.baseWalk
            end
        end)
        return
    end

    if not root then return end

    local vel = computeVelocity(humanoid, root)
    local flat = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
    local inSteal = usingSteal and not P1.isFlying()

    local hit, normal, tag
    if inSteal and flat.Magnitude > 1 then
        hit, normal, tag = raycastBeltCheck(root, flat, 16)
    end

    if hit and tag == "belt" then
        if engine.stealState and engine.stealState.beltSunk then
            hit = false
        else
            local n2 = typeof(normal) == "Vector3" and Vector3.new(normal.X, 0, normal.Z) or Vector3.zero
            local toTarget = Vector3.new(engine.target.X - root.Position.X, 0, engine.target.Z - root.Position.Z)

            if n2.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                local unit = n2.Unit
                if toTarget.Magnitude > 2 and toTarget:Dot(unit) < 0 then
                    toTarget -= unit * toTarget:Dot(unit)
                end
                if toTarget.Magnitude < 4 then
                    toTarget = unit * 20
                end
                flat = toTarget.Unit * math.max(40, currentSpeed() * 0.5)
                vel = Vector3.new(flat.X, vel.Y, flat.Z)
                pcall(function() humanoid.Jump = true end)
            end
            hit = false
        end
    end

    if hit and typeof(normal) == "Vector3" and normal.Magnitude > 0.05 then
        local unit = Vector3.new(normal.X, 0, normal.Z)
        if unit.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            unit = unit.Unit
            local dot = flat:Dot(unit)
            if dot < 0 then
                flat -= unit * dot
            end

            if flat.Magnitude < 8 then
                local toTarget = Vector3.new(
                    engine.target.X - root.Position.X, 0,
                    engine.target.Z - root.Position.Z
                )
                local tangent = Vector3.new(-unit.Z, 0, unit.X)
                if tangent.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    if tangent:Dot(toTarget) < 0 then
                        tangent = -tangent
                    end
                    flat = tangent.Unit * math.max(40, currentSpeed() * 0.35)
                end
            end
            vel = Vector3.new(flat.X, vel.Y, flat.Z)
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        humanoid.Sit = false
        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
        humanoid.AutoJumpEnabled = true

        local leaving = engine.plot and engine.plot.leaving and engine.plot.leaving()

        if inSteal then
            local s = currentSpeed()
            if hit then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(s, 16, 90)
            else
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(s, 16, 1300)
            end
        elseif Flags.BypassSpeed == true then
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(Flags.BypassCap) or 880, 16, 1300)
        else
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = engine.baseWalk
        end

        if leaving or (inSteal and engine.stealState and (engine.stealState.stillFor or 0) > 0.4) then
            humanoid.Jump = true
        elseif not inSteal then
            humanoid.Jump = false
        end

        local state = humanoid:GetState()
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated then
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end

        if flat.Magnitude > 1 then
            humanoid:Move(flat.Unit, false)
        else
            humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
        end
    end)

    muteCharCollisions()

    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, vel.Z)
    local alv = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local walk = humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed or 16
    local limit = math.max(10, walk or 16)
    local capped = alv.Magnitude > limit + 1 and alv.Unit * limit or alv

    for _, state in ipairs(engine.confirmed) do
        patchValidatorState(state, root, humanoid, root.Position, capped)
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [18] Character collision restore
--=====================================================================
function muteCharCollisions(skipRestore)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character

    if not char then return end

    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BasePart")
            and d.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart"
            and not d:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Accessory")
            and d.CanCollide == false then
            d.CanCollide = true
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [19] CFrame + Velocity setter
--=====================================================================
function setCFrameWithVelocity(humanoid, root, vel)
    updateSupport(root, vel and vel.Y)

    local flyingNoFlight = engine.driving and Flags.StealTravel == "Flight" and not Flags.Flight

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.Jump = false
        humanoid.AutoJumpEnabled = false
        humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
        humanoid.PlatformStand = true

        if flyingNoFlight then
            humanoid.AutoRotate = false
        end
    end)

    if flyingNoFlight and root then
        pcall(function()
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

            local look = engine.stealState and engine.stealState.flyLook
            if typeof(look) ~= "Vector3" or look.Magnitude < 0.05 then
                local fl = Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, root.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
                look = fl.Magnitude < 0.05 and Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) or fl.Unit
                if engine.stealState then
                    engine.stealState.flyLook = look
                end
            end

            local px, py, pz = root.Position.X, root.Position.Y, root.Position.Z
            local predicted = Vector3.new(px, py, pz)

            if vel and engine.dt then
                predicted = Vector3.new(px, py, pz) + Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
                    * math.clamp(engine.dt, 0, 0.05)
            end

            if engine.driving and vel then
                vel = Vector3.new(vel.X, math.min(vel.Y, 0), vel.Z)
            elseif engine.target then
                local floor = raycastFloorY(predicted)
                local minY = type(floor) == "number" and floor + 3.15 or nil

                if typeof(engine.target) == "Vector3" then
                    local ty = engine.target.Y
                    if minY then ty = math.max(ty, minY) end
                    if ty < py then
                        py = ty
                        vel = Vector3.new(vel.X, math.min(vel.Y, 0), vel.Z)
                    end
                end

                if minY and py < minY then
                    py = minY
                    vel = Vector3.new(vel.X, math.max(vel.Y, 0), vel.Z)
                end
            end

            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(px, py, pz), Vector3.new(px, py, pz) + look)
        end)
    elseif root then
        if engine.stealState then
            engine.stealState.flyLook = nil
        end

        local floor = raycastFloorY(root.Position)
        if type(floor) == "number" and root.Position.Y < floor + 3.15 then
            local dy = floor + 3.15 - root.Position.Y
            pcall(function()
                root.CFrame = root.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, dy, 0)
            end)
            if vel then
                vel = Vector3.new(vel.X, math.max(vel.Y, 0), vel.Z)
            end
        end
    end

    if root and vel then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [20] Engine On/Off (v1183)
--=====================================================================
local function engineShouldRun()
    if Flags.AutoSteal or (Flags.AntiTrap or Flags.AntiMob) then return true end
    if engine.plot and engine.plot.wanted and engine.plot.wanted() then return true end
    if engine.plot and engine.plot.driving and engine.plot.driving() then return true end
    if engine.plot and engine.plot.leaving and engine.plot.leaving() then return true end
    return Flags.BypassSpeed or Flags.Flight
end

P1.engineShouldRun = engineShouldRun

local function setEngineOn(on)
    local target = not not on

    if target == engine.on then
        if not engine.on then return end
        if engineShouldRun() then
            discoverStates()
            confirmStates()
            installWraps(true)
            return
        end
        if next(engine.wraps) then
            installWraps(false)
        end
        return
    end

    if target then
        discoverStates()
        confirmStates()
        warn("[Kira/engine] movementStates =", #engine.confirmed)

        if engineShouldRun() then
            installWraps(true)
        end

        if engine.steppedConn then
            engine.steppedConn:Disconnect()
        end

        engine.steppedConn = trackConn(RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt)
            if not engine.loaded then return end

            if engine.stealTick and engine.stealState and engine.stealState.running and Flags.AutoSteal then
                local ok, err = pcall(engine.stealTick, dt)
                if not ok and os.clock() - (engine.stealState.lastErrAt or 0) > 2 then
                    engine.stealState.lastErrAt = os.clock()
                    warn("[Kira/steal] tick ERR:", tostring(err))
                end
            end

            pcall(movementTick, dt)

            if engine.plot and engine.plot.tick then pcall(engine.plot.tick) end
            if engine.esp and engine.esp.tick then pcall(engine.esp.tick) end
        end))

        engine.on = true

        if P1.isFlying() then
            local root = select(2, getChar())
            if root then updateSupport(root, 0) end
        end

        warn("[Kira/engine] ON speed =", currentSpeed(), "fly =", P1.isFlying(), "wrap =", engineShouldRun())
        return
    end

    local wasActive = engine.wasOn or engine.support ~= nil

    engine.on = false
    engine.driving = false
    engine.target = nil
    engine.wasOn = false
    installWraps(false)

    if engine.steppedConn then
        engine.steppedConn:Disconnect()
        engine.steppedConn = nil
    end

    local humanoid, root = getChar()
    restoreHumanoid(humanoid, root, wasActive)
    muteCharCollisions(false)
    warn("[Kira/engine] OFF")
end

P1.setEngineOn = setEngineOn

--=====================================================================
--  [21] Character lifecycle
--=====================================================================
trackConn(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
    engine.wasOn = false
    engine.target = nil
    cleanupSupport()
end))

trackConn(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    charMute.saved = {}
    charMute.char = nil
    engine.wasOn = false
    engine.target = nil

    if engine.stealState then
        engine.stealState.target = nil
        engine.stealState.lastPos = nil
        engine.stealState.stillFor = 0
        engine.stealState.pendingCarry = nil
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        if not engine.loaded then return end

        local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 15)
        if not engine.loaded then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 15)
        if not humanoid or not root then return end

        task.wait(0.55)
        if not engine.loaded or char.Parent == nil or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

        cleanupSupport()
        updateCharMute()
        antiMobTick()

        trackConn(humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            engine.wasOn = false
            engine.target = nil
            cleanupSupport()
        end))

        if engine.stealState and engine.stealState.running then
            engine.stealState.state = "Scan"
            engine.stealState.since = os.clock()
            engine.driving = false
            engine.target = nil
        end

        if type(humanoid.WalkSpeed) == "number"
            and humanoid.WalkSpeed > 0 and humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 36 then
            engine.baseWalk = humanoid.WalkSpeed
        end

        if P1.isFlying() and root then
            updateSupport(root, 0)
        end
    end)
end))

do
    local humanoid = select(1, getChar())
    if humanoid then
        trackConn(humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            engine.wasOn = false
            engine.target = nil
            cleanupSupport()
        end))
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [22] Expose internals
--=====================================================================
P1.engineInternals = {
    getChar           = getChar,
    cameraLookFlat    = cameraLookFlat,
    currentSpeed      = currentSpeed,
    computeVelocity   = computeVelocity,
    wasdVector        = wasdVector,
    flightVelocity    = flightVelocity,
    updateSupport     = updateSupport,
    cleanupSupport    = cleanupSupport,
    raycastFloorY     = raycastFloorY,
    restoreHumanoid   = restoreHumanoid,
    patchValidatorState = patchValidatorState,
    setCFrameWithVelocity = setCFrameWithVelocity,
    disconnectAll     = disconnectAll,
    trackConn         = trackConn,
    antiMobTick       = antiMobTick,
    charMute          = charMute,
    antiMob           = antiMob,
}

print("[Kira] Part 5/9 loaded — movement engine + anti-trap + anti-ragdoll ready.")
--=====================================================================
--  Kira Hub :: Steal an Egg
--  Decoded — Part 6/9
--  Auto Steal State Machine + Filters + Prompt + Plot Safety
--=====================================================================

local P1 = KIRA_PART1
assert(P1, "Part 1 must be loaded first")
assert(P1.engine, "Part 5 must be loaded first")

local MUTATIONS = P1.MUTATIONS

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

--=====================================================================
--  [1] Load game modules (EggState / Assets.Directory / Areas.Directory)
--=====================================================================
local EggState, AssetDirectory, AreaDirectory

do
    local ok, mod = pcall(function()
        return require(ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)
    end)
    if ok then
        EggState = mod
        P1.eggStateModule = mod
    else
        warn("[Kira/modules] EggState require failed:", tostring(mod))
    end

    local ok2, mod2 = pcall(function()
        return require(ReplicatedStorage.Data.Assets)
    end)
    if ok2 and type(mod2) == "table" then
        AssetDirectory = mod2.Directory
    else
        warn("[Kira/modules] Assets require failed:", tostring(mod2))
    end

    local ok3, mod3 = pcall(function()
        return require(ReplicatedStorage.Data.Areas)
    end)
    if ok3 and type(mod3) == "table" then
        AreaDirectory = mod3.Directory
        warn("[Kira/modules] areas dir ok")
    else
        warn("[Kira/modules] Areas require failed:", tostring(mod3))
    end
end

P1.AssetDirectory = AssetDirectory
P1.AreaDirectory  = AreaDirectory

--=====================================================================
--  [2] Threat/watchdog tables
--=====================================================================
local STEAL_STATE = {
    state = "Idle",
    since = 0,
    target = nil,
    hookSnap = nil,
    carrying = false,
    carryUid = nil,
    heldUid = nil,
    countedUid = nil,
    banked = 0,
    lost = 0,
    regrabs = 0,
    lastGrab = 0,
    lastBankTry = 0,
    lastBankAt = 0,
    lastErrAt = 0,
    lockUid = nil,
    lockPos = nil,
    lockAt = 0,
    haltUntil = 0,
    running = false,
    conn = nil,
    pendingCarry = nil,
    lastPos = nil,
    stillFor = 0,
    bat = nil,
    fed = 0,
    chests = 0,
    eventUid = nil,
    eventFromField = false,
    lastFeed = 0,
    lastChestTake = 0,
    feedLockUntil = 0,
    feedWasWait = false,
    eventSkip = {},
}

engine.stealState = STEAL_STATE
P1.stealState = STEAL_STATE

-- Field egg cache
local fieldCache = {}
local fieldCacheAt = 0
local fieldGrewAt = 0
local cycleLast = nil
local cycleChanged = false
local cycleCount = 0

--=====================================================================
--  [3] Cycle / Night detection
--=====================================================================
local function currentCycle()
    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() or 0

    local disabledAt = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleDisabledAt")
    if type(disabledAt) == "number" then now = math.min(now, disabledAt) end

    local anchorAt = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleAnchorAt")
    local anchorIdx = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleAnchorIndex")
    if type(anchorAt) ~= "number" then anchorAt = 0 end
    if type(anchorIdx) ~= "number" then anchorIdx = 0 end

    return math.max(0, anchorIdx + math.floor((now - anchorAt) / 300))
end

local function secondsUntilNextCycle()
    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()

    local disabledAt = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleDisabledAt")
    if type(disabledAt) == "number" then now = math.min(now, disabledAt) end

    local anchorAt = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleAnchorAt")
    local anchorIdx = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleAnchorIndex")
    if type(anchorAt) ~= "number" then anchorAt = 0 end
    if type(anchorIdx) ~= "number" then anchorIdx = 0 end

    local nextAt = anchorAt
        + (math.max(0, anchorIdx + math.floor((now - anchorAt) / 300)) + 1) * 300
    return math.max(0, nextAt - now)
end

local function secondsToNight()
    local ok, s = pcall(secondsUntilNextCycle)
    if ok and type(s) == "number" then
        return math.max(0, math.floor(s + 0.5))
    end
    return 0
end

local function isNight()
    local sec = secondsUntilNextCycle()
    local nightWindow = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleNightSeconds")
    if type(nightWindow) ~= "number" then nightWindow = 10 end
    return sec <= math.clamp(nightWindow, 1, 300)
end

P1.currentCycle = currentCycle
P1.secondsToNight = secondsToNight
P1.isNight = isNight

--=====================================================================
--  [4] Read field eggs (v1204)
--=====================================================================
local function readFieldEggs(force)
    local now = os.clock()
    local cycle = currentCycle()

    if cycle ~= cycleLast then
        cycleLast = cycle
        force = true
        cycleChanged = true
        cycleCount = 0
        fieldCache = {}
        if STEAL_STATE then
            STEAL_STATE.eggResetAt = os.clock()
            STEAL_STATE.eggResetN = 0
            STEAL_STATE.eggResetGrew = 0
        end
    end

    if not force and type(fieldCache) == "table" and now - fieldCacheAt < 0.4 then
        return fieldCache
    end

    if not EggState then return fieldCache end

    local read = EggState.ReadFieldEggs or EggState.SyncFieldEggs
    if type(read) ~= "function" then return fieldCache end

    local ok, result = pcall(read)
    if not ok then
        warn("[Kira/eggs] ReadFieldEggs ERR:", tostring(result))
        result = nil
    end

    if type(result) ~= "table" or type(result.Records) ~= "table" then
        fieldCacheAt = now - 0.28
        return fieldCache
    end

    local list = {}
    local seen = {}
    for _, rec in pairs(result.Records) do
        if type(rec) == "table" and rec.Uid and not seen[rec.Uid] then
            seen[rec.Uid] = true
            list[#list + 1] = rec
        end
    end

    if #list == 0 and #fieldCache > 0 and now - fieldGrewAt < 2.5 then
        fieldCacheAt = now
        return fieldCache
    end

    fieldCache = list
    fieldCacheAt = now
    if #list > 0 then fieldGrewAt = now end

    if STEAL_STATE and (STEAL_STATE.eggResetAt or 0) > 0
        and (STEAL_STATE.eggResetN or 0) < #list then
        STEAL_STATE.eggResetN = #list
        STEAL_STATE.eggResetGrew = now
    end

    return list
end

P1.readFieldEggs = readFieldEggs

-- FieldRefreshed connection
pcall(function()
    local evt = EggState and EggState.FieldRefreshed
    if type(evt) == "table" and type(evt.Connect) == "function" then
        trackConn(evt:Connect(function()
            local prev = type(fieldCache) == "table" and #fieldCache or 0
            fieldCacheAt = 0
            fieldGrewAt = 0
            cycleChanged = true
            if STEAL_STATE and prev > 6 then
                STEAL_STATE.eggResetAt = os.clock()
                STEAL_STATE.eggResetN = 0
                STEAL_STATE.eggResetGrew = 0
            end
        end))
    end
end)

--=====================================================================
--  [5] Generic helpers
--=====================================================================
local function asSet(list)
    local out = {}
    if type(list) ~= "table" then return out end
    for _, v in ipairs(list) do
        out[string.lower(tostring(v))] = true
    end
    return out
end

local function mergeListUnique(base, extra)
    local seen = {}
    for _, v in ipairs(base) do seen[string.lower(tostring(v))] = true end

    local added = {}
    for _, v in ipairs(extra) do
