        local s = tostring(v or "")
        if s ~= "" and s ~= "nil" and not seen[string.lower(s)] then
            base[#base + 1] = s
            seen[string.lower(s)] = true
            added[#added + 1] = s
        end
    end
    return added
end

local function numberFromString(str)
    if type(str) ~= "string" then return 0 end
    local s = string.lower(str:gsub(",", ""):gsub("%s+", ""))
    if s == "" or s == "any" or s:find("blank") then return 0 end
    local num, suffix = s:match("([%d%.]+)([kmb]?)")
    local n = tonumber(num)
    if not n then return 0 end
    if suffix == "k" then return n * 1000 end
    if suffix == "m" then return n * 1e6 end
    if suffix == "b" then return n * 1e9 end
    return n
end

P1.numberFromString = numberFromString

--=====================================================================
--  [6] Position extraction (v1209)
--=====================================================================
local function positionFromRecord(rec)
    if type(rec) ~= "table" then return nil end

    local function fromVec(v)
        if v == nil then return nil end
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            if typeof(v) == "Vector3" then
                return v + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
            end
            return v.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
        end)
        if ok then return res end
        return nil
    end

    local pos = fromVec(rec.BottomCFrame)
    if pos then return pos end

    pos = fromVec(rec.BoundsCFrame)
    if pos then return pos end

    pos = fromVec(rec.CFrame)
    if pos then return pos end

    pos = fromVec(rec.WorldCFrame)
    if pos then return pos end

    pos = fromVec(rec.PivotCFrame)
    if pos then return pos end

    return fromVec(rec.Position)
end

P1.positionFromRecord = positionFromRecord

--=====================================================================
--  [7] Plot detection + safety
--=====================================================================
local PLOT_CACHE = {}
local PLOT_AT = 0

local function refreshPlotCache()
    local now = os.clock()
    if PLOT_AT and now - PLOT_AT < 2 and #PLOT_CACHE > 0 then
        return PLOT_CACHE
    end

    local list = {}
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if plots then
        for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local center = child:FindFirstChild("CenterPoint", true)
                or child:FindFirstChild("SpawnPoint", true)
            if center and center:IsA("BasePart") then
                list[#list + 1] = { pos = center.Position, r = 48 }
            else
                local ok, cf = pcall(function() return child:GetPivot() end)
                if ok and typeof(cf) == "CFrame" then
                    list[#list + 1] = { pos = cf.Position, r = 48 }
                end
            end
        end
    end

    PLOT_CACHE = list
    PLOT_AT = now
    return list
end

local function isOnAnyPlot(pos)
    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
    local plots = refreshPlotCache()
    for _, p in ipairs(plots) do
        local dxz = Vector3.new(pos.X - p.pos.X, 0, pos.Z - p.pos.Z)
        if dxz.Magnitude <= p.r then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function isPlotEgg(rec, recPos)
    if type(rec) ~= "table" then return false end
    if rec.Placement ~= nil then return true end
    if rec.OwnerUserId and rec.State ~= "Dropped" then return true end

    local uid = string.lower(tostring(rec.Uid or ""))
    if uid:find("plot", 1, true)
        or uid:find(":pen", 1, true)
        or uid:find("hatch", 1, true) then
        return true
    end

    if rec.State == "Slot" or rec.State == "Dropped" then return false end

    if typeof(recPos) ~= "Vector3" then
        recPos = positionFromRecord(rec)
    end

    return isOnAnyPlot(recPos)
end

local function resolveOwnPlot()
    local resolved
    pcall(function()
        resolved = require(ReplicatedStorage.Client.PlotState).ResolvePlot()
    end)

    if type(resolved) == "table" and resolved.PlotFolder then
        local spawnCF = resolved.RespawnPointCFrame
        if typeof(spawnCF) == "CFrame" then
            return spawnCF.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), spawnCF,
                resolved.PlotFolder, resolved.CenterPoint
        end

        local sp = resolved.PlotFolder:FindFirstChild("SpawnPoint", true)
        if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") then
            return sp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), sp.CFrame,
                resolved.PlotFolder, resolved.CenterPoint
        end

        return resolved.PlotFolder:GetPivot().Position,
            resolved.PlotFolder:GetPivot(), resolved.PlotFolder,
            resolved.CenterPoint
    end

    -- Fallback 1: scan Plots folder for own name
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if plots then
        for _, child in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local found = false
            for _, d in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextLabel")
                    and string.find(d.Text, LocalPlayer.Name, 1, true) then
                    found = true
                    break
                end
            end

            if found then
                local sp = child:FindFirstChild("SpawnPoint", true)
                local cp = child:FindFirstChild("CenterPoint", true)
                if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") then
                    return sp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), sp.CFrame, child, cp
                end
                return child:GetPivot().Position, child:GetPivot(), child, cp
            end
        end
    end

    -- Fallback 2: read owned eggs
    if EggState and type(EggState.ReadOwnedEggs) == "function" then
        local ok, owned = pcall(EggState.ReadOwnedEggs)
        if ok and type(owned) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(owned) do
                if type(v) == "table"
                    and tonumber(v.OwnerUserId) == LocalPlayer.UserId then
                    local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(k))
                    if plot then
                        local sp = plot:FindFirstChild("SpawnPoint", true)
                        local cp = plot:FindFirstChild("CenterPoint", true)
                        if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") then
                            return sp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0),
                                sp.CFrame, plot, cp
                        end
                        return plot:GetPivot().Position, plot:GetPivot(),
                            plot, cp
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

local function isOnOwnPlot(pos)
    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end

    local _, _, plotFolder, centerPart = resolveOwnPlot()

    local centerPos
    if centerPart and typeof(centerPart) == "Instance"
        and centerPart:IsA("BasePart") then
        centerPos = centerPart.Position
    elseif plotFolder then
        local c = plotFolder:FindFirstChild("CenterPoint", true)
            or plotFolder:FindFirstChild("SpawnPoint", true)
        if c and c:IsA("BasePart") then
            centerPos = c.Position
        else
            pcall(function()
                centerPos = plotFolder:GetPivot().Position
            end)
        end
    end

    if typeof(centerPos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
    return Vector3.new(pos.X - centerPos.X, 0, pos.Z - centerPos.Z).Magnitude <= 56
end

local function findSpawn()
    local sp = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpawnLocation")
    if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") then
        return sp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), sp.CFrame, sp
    end
    local st = workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnTarget", true)
    if st and st:IsA("BasePart") then
        return st.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), st.CFrame, st
    end
    return resolveOwnPlot()
end

P1.refreshPlotCache = refreshPlotCache
P1.isOnAnyPlot      = isOnAnyPlot
P1.isPlotEgg        = isPlotEgg
P1.resolveOwnPlot   = resolveOwnPlot
P1.isOnOwnPlot      = isOnOwnPlot
P1.findSpawn        = findSpawn

--=====================================================================
--  [8] Treadmill detection (for plot safety)
--=====================================================================
local function findTreadmillBoundary(refPos)
    if typeof(refPos) ~= "Vector3" then refPos = Vector3.zero end

    local best, bestDist
    local function check(part)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        local name = string.lower(part.Name)
        local pname = part.Parent and string.lower(part.Parent.Name) or ""
        local isBelt = name:find("treadmill", 1, true)
            or name:find("belt", 1, true)
            or pname:find("treadmill", 1, true)
            or pname:find("belt", 1, true)

        if not isBelt and name ~= "bottom" then return end
        if name == "bottom" and not isBelt then return end

        local dist = (part.Position - refPos).Magnitude
        if not bestDist or dist < bestDist then
            best = part
            bestDist = dist
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        local _, _, folder = resolveOwnPlot()
        if folder then
            check(folder:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom", true))
            for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then check(d) end
            end
        end
    end)

    local origin = select(1, resolveOwnPlot())
    local renders = workspace:FindFirstChild("__ClientTreadmillRenders")
    if renders then
        for _, d in ipairs(renders:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart")
                and typeof(origin) == "Vector3"
                and (d.Position - origin).Magnitude < 90 then
                check(d)
            end
        end
    end

    return best
end

local function boundsFromTreadmill(refPos)
    local minX, maxX, minZ, maxZ
    local sumY, count = 0, 0

    local function addPart(part)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end

        local name = string.lower(part.Name)
        local pname = part.Parent and string.lower(part.Parent.Name) or ""
        local ok = name:find("treadmill", 1, true)
            or name:find("belt", 1, true)
            or pname:find("treadmill", 1, true)
            or pname:find("belt", 1, true)
            or name == "treadmillbottom"

        if not ok then return end

        local cf, size = part.CFrame, part.Size
        local hx = size.X * 0.5
        local hz = size.Z * 0.5
        local extentX = math.abs(cf.RightVector.X) * hx
            + math.abs(cf.LookVector.X) * hz
            + math.abs(cf.UpVector.X) * (size.Y * 0.5)
        local extentZ = math.abs(cf.RightVector.Z) * hx
            + math.abs(cf.LookVector.Z) * hz
            + math.abs(cf.UpVector.Z) * (size.Y * 0.5)

        local px, pz = cf.Position.X, cf.Position.Z
        minX = minX and math.min(minX, px - extentX) or (px - extentX)
        maxX = maxX and math.max(maxX, px + extentX) or (px + extentX)
        minZ = minZ and math.min(minZ, pz - extentZ) or (pz - extentZ)
        maxZ = maxZ and math.max(maxZ, pz + extentZ) or (pz + extentZ)
        sumY += cf.Position.Y
        count += 1
    end

    pcall(function()
        local _, _, folder = resolveOwnPlot()
        if folder then
            addPart(folder:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom", true))
            for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then addPart(d) end
            end
        end
    end)

    local origin = select(1, resolveOwnPlot())
    local renders = workspace:FindFirstChild("__ClientTreadmillRenders")
    if renders then
        for _, d in ipairs(renders:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart")
                and (typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3"
                    or (d.Position - origin).Magnitude < 90) then
                addPart(d)
            end
        end
    end

    addPart(findTreadmillBoundary(refPos))

    if count == 0 or not minX then return nil end

    return {
        cx = (minX + maxX) * 0.5,
        cz = (minZ + maxZ) * 0.5,
        hx = (maxX - minX) * 0.5,
        hz = (maxZ - minZ) * 0.5,
        y  = sumY / count,
    }
end

local function insideBounds(bounds, pos, pad)
    if not bounds or typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
    pad = pad or 0
    return math.abs(pos.X - bounds.cx) <= bounds.hx + pad
       and math.abs(pos.Z - bounds.cz) <= bounds.hz + pad
end

local function boundsIntersect(bounds, a, b, pad)
    if not bounds or typeof(a) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(b) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end
    pad = pad or 0

    local ax, az = a.X, a.Z
    local bx, bz = b.X, b.Z
    local minX = bounds.cx - bounds.hx - pad
    local maxX = bounds.cx + bounds.hx + pad
    local minZ = bounds.cz - bounds.hz - pad
    local maxZ = bounds.cz + bounds.hz + pad

    if (ax < minX and bx < minX)
        or (maxX < ax and maxX < bx)
        or (az < minZ and bz < minZ)
        or (maxZ < az and maxZ < bz) then
        return false
    end

    if insideBounds(bounds, a, pad) or insideBounds(bounds, b, pad) then
        return true
    end

    local dx, dz = bx - ax, bz - az
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local t = i / 9
        local p = Vector3.new(ax + dx * t, 0, az + dz * t)
        if insideBounds(bounds, p, pad) then return true end
    end

    return false
end

-- Safety adjustment when target is inside own plot
local function safeAdjust(from, to)
    if typeof(from) ~= "Vector3" then return to end

    local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
    local plotPos  = select(1, resolveOwnPlot())
    local bounds   = boundsFromTreadmill(from)

    local inside  = bounds and insideBounds(bounds, from, 12)
    local cross   = bounds and (typeof(to) == "Vector3"
        and boundsIntersect(bounds, from, to, 8))

    if spawnPos and Vector3.new(spawnPos.X - from.X, 0, spawnPos.Z - from.Z).Magnitude <= 62 then
        return to
    end

    if not isOnOwnPlot(from) and not inside and not cross then return to end
    if typeof(spawnPos) ~= "Vector3" then return to end

    local center = bounds and Vector3.new(bounds.cx, from.Y, bounds.cz)
        or (typeof(plotPos) == "Vector3" and plotPos or from)
    local away = Vector3.new(spawnPos.X - center.X, 0, spawnPos.Z - center.Z)

    if away.Magnitude < 2 then
        away = Vector3.new(spawnPos.X - from.X, 0, spawnPos.Z - from.Z)
    end
    if away.Magnitude < 0.1 then return to end

    local unit = away.Unit
    if Vector3.new(from.X - center.X, 0, from.Z - center.Z):Dot(unit) > 18
        and not inside then
        return to
    end

    local safe = Vector3.new(center.X + unit.X * 32, from.Y, center.Z + unit.Z * 32)
    if Vector3.new(from.X - safe.X, 0, from.Z - safe.Z).Magnitude < 12 then
        return to
    end
    return safe
end

local function safeRoute(to, from, force)
    if typeof(to) ~= "Vector3" then return to end
    if typeof(from) ~= "Vector3" then return from end

    local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
    if spawnPos
        and Vector3.new(spawnPos.X - from.X, 0, spawnPos.Z - from.Z).Magnitude <= 62 then
        force = true
    end

    if force then return to end

    local bounds = boundsFromTreadmill(from)
    local inPlot = isOnOwnPlot(from)
        or (bounds and insideBounds(bounds, from, 12))
        or (bounds and boundsIntersect(bounds, from, to, 8))

    if P1.isFlying() then return to end
    if not inPlot then return to end

    return safeAdjust(from, to)
end

local function safeZone()
    local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
    if typeof(spawnPos) ~= "Vector3" then return spawnPos, spawnPos end

    if typeof(engine.target) ~= "Vector3" then return spawnPos, spawnPos end

    if P1.isFlying() then
        return Vector3.new(spawnPos.X, engine.target.Y, spawnPos.Z), spawnPos
    end

    return safeAdjust(engine.target, spawnPos), spawnPos
end

P1.boundsFromTreadmill = boundsFromTreadmill
P1.insideBounds        = insideBounds
P1.safeAdjust          = safeAdjust
P1.safeRoute           = safeRoute
P1.safeZone            = safeZone

--=====================================================================
--  [9] Economy: rate calculation
--=====================================================================
local function ratePerSecond(rec, cfg)
    if type(cfg) ~= "table" and type(rec) == "table" then
        local cat = rec.AssetCategory or rec.Category
        cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
    end

    if type(rec) == "table" then
        local mps = tonumber(rec.MoneyPerSecond)
        if mps and mps > 0 then return mps end

        if type(rec.ItemData) == "table" then
            local mps2 = tonumber(rec.ItemData.MoneyPerSecond)
            if mps2 and mps2 > 0 then return mps2 end

            if type(rec.ItemData.Category) == "string"
                and tonumber(rec.ItemData.Scale) then
                local ok, rate = pcall(function()
                    return require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetEarnings)
                        .MutationOnlyRatePerSecond(rec.ItemData)
                end)
                if ok and tonumber(rate) then return tonumber(rate) end
            end
        end
    end

    local baseRate = type(cfg) == "table" and tonumber(cfg.EarningRate) or 0
    baseRate = baseRate or 0

    local cat = type(rec) == "table"
        and (rec.AssetCategory or rec.Category) or nil

    local muts = {}
    if type(rec) == "table" then
        if type(rec.Mutations) == "table" then
            muts = rec.Mutations
        elseif type(rec.ItemData) == "table"
            and type(rec.ItemData.Mutations) == "table" then
            muts = rec.ItemData.Mutations
        end
    end

    local scale
    if type(rec) == "table" then
        scale = tonumber(rec.Scale)
        if (not scale or scale <= 0)
            and type(rec.ItemData) == "table" then
            scale = tonumber(rec.ItemData.Scale)
        end
    end

    if not scale or scale <= 0 then
        local w1 = type(rec) == "table"
            and (tonumber(rec.Weight) or tonumber(rec.Kg)
                or tonumber(rec.ModelWeight))
        local w2 = type(cfg) == "table" and tonumber(cfg.ModelWeight)
        scale = (not w1 or w1 <= 0 or not w2 or w2 <= 0)
            and 1 or (w1 / w2) ^ (1 / 3)
    end

    if type(cat) == "string" then
        local ok, rate = pcall(function()
            return require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetEarnings)
                .MutationOnlyRatePerSecond({
                    Category = cat,
                    Mutations = muts,
                    Scale = scale,
                })
        end)
        if ok and tonumber(rate) then return tonumber(rate) end
    end

    local scaleFactor = scale <= 5 and scale ^ 1.85
        or (scale / 5) ^ 1.2 * 19.637875755794

    local mutMult = 1
    pcall(function()
        mutMult = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules.Mutations)
            .EarningsFor(muts)
    end)
    mutMult = tonumber(mutMult) or 1

    local out = baseRate * scaleFactor * mutMult
    if out < 1 and baseRate > 0 then out = 1 end
    return math.floor(out + 0.5)
end

P1.ratePerSecond = ratePerSecond

--=====================================================================
--  [10] Rarity index
--=====================================================================
local RARITY_INDEX = {
    common = 1, uncommon = 2, rare = 3, epic = 4, legendary = 5,
    mythic = 6, cosmic = 7, secret = 8, eternal = 9, divine = 10,
    titan = 11,
}

local function rarityIndex(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" or name == "?" then return 0 end
    for i, v in ipairs(RARITIES) do
        if string.lower(name) == string.lower(v) then return i end
    end
    return 0
end

local function rarityName(cfg)
    if type(cfg) ~= "table" or type(cfg.Rarity) ~= "table" then return "?" end
    return tostring(cfg.Rarity.DisplayName or cfg.Rarity.Name
        or cfg.Rarity._id or "?")
end

P1.rarityIndex = rarityIndex
P1.rarityName = rarityName

--=====================================================================
--  [11] Filter matching
--=====================================================================
local function matchesFilters(rec, cfg, override)
    if type(rec) ~= "table" then return false end

    -- Area check
    local areaId = tostring(rec.AreaId or "")
    local areaSet = asSet(Flags.Areas)
    if next(areaSet) and not areaSet[string.lower(areaId)] then
        return false
    end

    if override then return true end

    -- Rarity check
    if Flags.UseRarity then
        local set = asSet(Flags.Rarities)
        if next(set) then
            local rn = rarityName(cfg)
            if not set[string.lower(rn)] then return false end
        end
    end

    -- Mutation check
    if Flags.UseMutation then
        local set = asSet(Flags.Mutations)
        if next(set) then
            local found = false
            if type(rec.Mutations) == "table" then
                for _, m in ipairs(rec.Mutations) do
                    if set[string.lower(tostring(m or ""))] then
                        found = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if not found then return false end
        end
    end

    -- Weight check
    local minW = tonumber(Flags.MinWeight)
    if minW and minW > 0 and cfg
        and tonumber(cfg.ModelWeight)
        and minW > cfg.ModelWeight then
        return false
    end

    return true
end

P1.matchesFilters = matchesFilters

--=====================================================================
--  [12] Infested check (event)
--=====================================================================
local function isInfested(rec)
    if type(rec) ~= "table" then return false end
    if rec.HasParasite == true then return true end

    local base = string.lower(tostring(rec.BaseMutation or ""))
    if base == "monstrous"
        or base == "parasite"
        or base:find("infest", 1, true) then
        return true
    end

    local muts = rec.Mutations
    if type(muts) ~= "table" and type(rec.ItemData) == "table" then
        muts = rec.ItemData.Mutations
    end

    if type(muts) == "table" then
        for _, m in ipairs(muts) do
            local s = string.lower(tostring(m or ""))
            if s == "monstrous"
                or s == "parasite"
                or s:find("infest", 1, true) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

P1.isInfested = isInfested

--=====================================================================
--  [13] Best target selector (v1216)
--=====================================================================
local function candidateFrom(rec)
    if type(rec) ~= "table" or not rec.Uid then return nil end

    local carrier = tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId)
    if carrier and (carrier ~= 0 and carrier ~= LocalPlayer.UserId) then
        return nil
    end

    local state = rec.State
    if state ~= "Slot" and state ~= "Dropped" then return nil end

    local cat = rec.AssetCategory
    local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
    local pos = positionFromRecord(rec)

    if not pos or isPlotEgg(rec, pos) then return nil end

    return {
        rec = rec,
        cfg = cfg,
        pos = pos,
        earn = ratePerSecond(rec, cfg),
        rar = type(cfg) == "table" and type(cfg.Rarity) == "table"
            and (tonumber(cfg.Rarity.RarityNumber) or 0) or 0,
        infested = isInfested(rec),
        name = cfg and (cfg.DisplayName or rec.AssetCategory)
            or tostring(rec.AssetCategory),
        area = tostring(rec.AreaId or ""),
    }
end

local function pickBestTarget()
    local eggs = readFieldEggs()
    local mode = Flags.StealMode or "Best value"

    -- Check if we should keep chasing our lock
    local lockedUid = STEAL_STATE.lockUid or STEAL_STATE.heldUid

    if type(lockedUid) == "string" then
        local lockedRec
        for _, rec in ipairs(eggs) do
            if type(rec) == "table" and lockedUid == rec.Uid then
                lockedRec = rec
                break
            end
        end

        if lockedRec then
            local cand = candidateFrom(lockedRec)
            if cand then
                local ok = matchesFilters(lockedRec, cand.cfg)
                if ok and mode == "Gen ($/s) snipe" then
                    local floor = numberFromString(Flags.GenSnipeFloor)
                    ok = not (floor > 0) or not (floor > ratePerSecond(lockedRec, cand.cfg))
                end

                local isDropped = lockedRec.State == "Dropped"
                if ok or isDropped then
                    cand.matched = true
                    if typeof(cand.pos) == "Vector3" then
                        STEAL_STATE.lockPos = cand.pos
                        STEAL_STATE.lockAt = os.clock()
                    end
                    return cand
                end

                STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
                STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil
            end
        else
            -- Field still tracks our last position?
            if typeof(STEAL_STATE.lockPos) == "Vector3"
                and os.clock() - (STEAL_STATE.lockAt or 0) < 5 then
                return {
                    rec = { Uid = lockedUid, State = "Dropped" },
                    pos = STEAL_STATE.lockPos,
                    name = STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.name or "egg",
                    area = STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.area or "",
                    matched = true,
                    event = STEAL_STATE.eventFromField == true,
                }
            end

            STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
            if lockedUid == STEAL_STATE.heldUid then
                STEAL_STATE.heldUid = nil
            end
            if STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.rec
                and lockedUid == STEAL_STATE.target.rec.Uid then
                STEAL_STATE.target = nil
            end
        end
    end

    -- Build candidate list
    local list = {}
    for _, rec in ipairs(eggs) do
        if type(rec) == "table" then
            local cand = candidateFrom(rec)
            if cand then
                local ok = matchesFilters(rec, cand.cfg)
                if ok and mode == "Gen ($/s) snipe" then
                    local floor = numberFromString(Flags.GenSnipeFloor)
                    ok = not (floor > 0)
                        or not (floor > ratePerSecond(rec, cand.cfg))
                end

                -- Event: infested handling
                local asEvent = false
                if Flags.AutoEvent == true and cand.infested == true then
                    local areaId = tostring(rec.AreaId or "")
                    local areaSet = asSet(Flags.Areas)
                    asEvent = not next(areaSet) or areaSet[string.lower(areaId)] == true
                end

                if asEvent then
                    local keep = numberFromString(Flags.EventKeepGen)
                    if keep > 0 and keep <= (cand.earn or 0) then
                        asEvent = false
                    end
                end

                if asEvent then
                    cand.matched = false
                    cand.event = true
                    list[#list + 1] = cand
                elseif ok then
                    cand.matched = true
                    cand.event = false
                    list[#list + 1] = cand
                end
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(list, function(a, b)
        if a.matched ~= b.matched then
            return a.matched == true
        end

        if mode == "Egg type filter" or mode == "Rarity snipe" then
            if a.rar ~= b.rar then return a.rar > b.rar end
            if a.earn ~= b.earn then return a.earn > b.earn end
        else
            if a.earn ~= b.earn then return a.earn > b.earn end
            if a.rar ~= b.rar then return a.rar > b.rar end
        end

        return a.name < b.name
    end)

    return list[1]
end

P1.pickBestTarget = pickBestTarget

--=====================================================================
--  [14] Prompt system
--=====================================================================
local firePrompt = fireproximityprompt or fireproximityprompttrigger
local carryAreaPrompt
local lastPrompt

local function preparePrompt(prompt)
    if typeof(prompt) ~= "Instance" then return end
    local ok, isPrompt = pcall(function() return prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") end)
    if not ok or not isPrompt then return end

    pcall(function()
        prompt.HoldDuration = 0
        prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
        prompt.ClickablePrompt = true
    end)

    if prompt.Name == "CarryAreaEgg" then
        carryAreaPrompt = prompt
    end
end

local function promptLooksLikeSteal(prompt)
    if not prompt then return false end
    if prompt.Name == "CarryAreaEgg" then return true end

    local str = ""
    pcall(function()
        str = string.lower(
            tostring(prompt.Name) .. " "
            .. tostring(prompt.ActionText) .. " "
            .. tostring(prompt.ObjectText)
        )
    end)
    return str:find("steal", 1, true) ~= nil
        or str:find("carry", 1, true) ~= nil
end

local function firePromptNow(prompt)
    if not prompt then return end
    preparePrompt(prompt)

    if firePrompt then
        pcall(firePrompt, prompt, 0)
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        prompt:InputHoldBegin()
        prompt:InputHoldEnd()
    end)
end

local function findCarryPrompt()
    if lastPrompt and lastPrompt.Parent and promptLooksLikeSteal(lastPrompt) then
        return lastPrompt
    end

    if carryAreaPrompt and carryAreaPrompt.Parent then
        return carryAreaPrompt
    end

    local part = workspace:FindFirstChild("SmartPromptPart")
    local prompt = part and part:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg")
    if prompt and prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
        carryAreaPrompt = prompt
        return prompt
    end
end

P1.preparePrompt    = preparePrompt
P1.promptLooksLikeSteal = promptLooksLikeSteal
P1.firePromptNow    = firePromptNow
P1.findCarryPrompt  = findCarryPrompt

--=====================================================================
--  [15] Prompt-target validation
--=====================================================================
local function promptIsTarget(prompt, target, radius)
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not promptLooksLikeSteal(prompt) then
        return false
    end
    if not target then return false end

    radius = tonumber(radius) or 5

    -- Try Uid attributes on prompt/parents
    local uid = target.rec and target.rec.Uid and tostring(target.rec.Uid)
    if uid and uid ~= "" then
        local cur = prompt
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            if not cur then break end

            local matched
            pcall(function()
                for _, attr in ipairs({
                    "Uid", "EggUid", "RecordUid", "AssetUid", "EggId"
                }) do
                    local v = cur:GetAttribute(attr)
                    if v ~= nil and tostring(v) == uid then
                        matched = true
                        return
                    end
                end

                local name = tostring(cur.Name or "")
                if name ~= ""
                    and name ~= "CarryAreaEgg"
                    and name ~= "SmartPromptPart"
                    and name == uid then
                    matched = true
                end
            end)

            if matched then return true end
            cur = cur.Parent
        end
    end

    if typeof(target.pos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end

    local parent = prompt.Parent
    local ppos

    if parent:IsA("BasePart") then
        ppos = parent.Position
    elseif parent:IsA("Model") then
        local ok, pivot = pcall(function() return parent:GetPivot() end)
        ppos = ok and pivot and pivot.Position
    elseif parent.Parent and parent.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
        ppos = parent.Parent.Position
    end

    if typeof(ppos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end

    return radius >= Vector3.new(ppos.X - target.pos.X, 0, ppos.Z - target.pos.Z).Magnitude
end

STEAL_STATE.promptIsTarget = promptIsTarget

--=====================================================================
--  [16] Hazard muting
--=====================================================================
local HAZARDS = { saved = {}, on = false, at = 0 }

local function hazardMute(save)
    HAZARDS.saved = HAZARDS.saved or {}

    local function addPart(part)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        if HAZARDS.saved[part] == nil then
            HAZARDS.saved[part] = { c = part.CanCollide, t = part.CanTouch }
        end
        if part.CanCollide ~= false then part.CanCollide = false end
        if part.CanTouch ~= false then part.CanTouch = false end
    end

    if save then
        local now = os.clock()
        if HAZARDS.on and now - (HAZARDS.at or 0) < 2.4 then return end
        HAZARDS.on = true
        HAZARDS.at = now

        local objRoot = workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
        local machines = objRoot and objRoot:FindFirstChild("Machines")
        local fuse = machines and machines:FindFirstChild("FuseMachine")

        if fuse then
            addPart(fuse)
            for _, d in ipairs(fuse:GetDescendants()) do addPart(d) end
        end

        if machines then
            for _, d in ipairs(machines:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide == true then
                    addPart(d)
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end

    HAZARDS.on = false
    HAZARDS.at = 0

    for part, saved in pairs(HAZARDS.saved) do
        if part.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                if type(saved) == "table" then
                    part.CanCollide = saved.c == true
                    part.CanTouch = saved.t == true
                    return
                end
                part.CanCollide = saved == true
            end)
        end
    end
    HAZARDS.saved = {}
end

STEAL_STATE.muteHazards = hazardMute

--=====================================================================
--  [17] Belt muting (for treadmill)
--=====================================================================
local BELT = { saved = {}, on = false, at = 0, sunk = false }

local function isBeltPart(part)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return false end

    local name = string.lower(part.Name)
    local pname = part.Parent and string.lower(part.Parent.Name) or ""
    local full = name
    pcall(function() full = string.lower(part:GetFullName()) end)

    if name == "treadmillbottom"
        or (name == "bottom"
            and (pname:find("treadmill", 1, true)
                or full:find("treadmill", 1, true))) then
        return true
    end

    if (name:find("wear", 1, true)
        or name:find("sensor", 1, true)
        or name:find("trigger", 1, true)
        or name:find("hitbox", 1, true)
        or name:find("activate", 1, true)
        or name:find("detector", 1, true)
        or pname:find("wear", 1, true))
        and (full:find("treadmill", 1, true)
            or full:find("belt", 1, true)
            or pname:find("treadmill", 1, true)) then
        return true
    end

    if part.CanTouch == true and part.Size.Y <= 6
        and (name:find("treadmill", 1, true)
            or pname:find("treadmill", 1, true)
            or full:find("clienttreadmill", 1, true)) then
        return true
    end

    return false
end

local function sinkBeltPart(part)
    local saved = BELT.saved[part]
    if type(saved) ~= "table" then
        saved = {
            cf = part.CFrame,
            anchored = part.Anchored,
            t = part.CanTouch,
            c = part.CanCollide,
        }
        BELT.saved[part] = saved
    end
    pcall(function()
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanTouch = false
        part.CFrame = saved.cf * CFrame.new(0, -80, 0)
    end)
end

local function beltMute(sunk)
    BELT.saved = BELT.saved or {}

    if sunk then
        BELT.sunk = true

        local now = os.clock()
        if not BELT.on or now - (BELT.at or 0) >= 2.4 then
            BELT.on = true
            BELT.at = now
            pcall(function()
                local _, _, folder = resolveOwnPlot()
                if folder then
                    for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                        if isBeltPart(d) then sinkBeltPart(d) end
                    end
                end
                local renders = workspace:FindFirstChild("__ClientTreadmillRenders")
                if renders then
                    for _, d in ipairs(renders:GetDescendants()) do
                        if isBeltPart(d) then sinkBeltPart(d) end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        for part in pairs(BELT.saved) do
            if part.Parent then sinkBeltPart(part) end
        end
        return
    end

    BELT.sunk = false
    BELT.on = false
    BELT.at = 0

    for part, saved in pairs(BELT.saved) do
        if part.Parent and type(saved) == "table" then
            pcall(function()
                part.CFrame = saved.cf
                part.Anchored = saved.anchored == true
                part.CanTouch = saved.t == true
                part.CanCollide = saved.c == true
            end)
        end
    end
    BELT.saved = {}
end

STEAL_STATE.muteBelt = beltMute
engine.stealState.beltSunk = BELT.sunk

--=====================================================================
--  [18] Allow train
--=====================================================================
function STEAL_STATE.allowTrain()
    if Flags.AutoTreadmill ~= true then return false end
    if Flags.AutoSteal == true and STEAL_STATE.running then return false end
    if Flags.TrainWhenIdle ~= true then return false end
    return true
end

--=====================================================================
--  [19] Void rescue + unstuck
--=====================================================================
function STEAL_STATE.floorY()
    local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
    if typeof(spawnPos) == "Vector3" then return spawnPos.Y end
    return 70
end

function STEAL_STATE.rescueVoid(root, humanoid)
    if not Flags.AutoSteal or not STEAL_STATE.running then return false end
    if not root then return false end

    local floor = STEAL_STATE.floorY()
    if root.Position.Y >= floor - 8 then return false end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (STEAL_STATE.voidAt or 0) < 0.4 then return true end
    STEAL_STATE.voidAt = now

    local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())

    pcall(function()
        root.Anchored = false
        if typeof(spawnPos) == "Vector3" and spawnPos.Y >= 58 then
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(spawnPos.X, spawnPos.Y + 6, spawnPos.Z)
        else
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 74, 0)
        end
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        if humanoid then
            humanoid.Sit = false
            humanoid.PlatformStand = P1.isFlying() == true
        end
    end)

    engine.target = nil
    warn("[Kira/steal] void rescue")
    return true
end

function STEAL_STATE.resetStuck(root, humanoid)
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (STEAL_STATE.unstuckAt or 0) < 2.5 then return end
    STEAL_STATE.unstuckAt = now
    STEAL_STATE.stillFor = 0

    local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
    local floor = STEAL_STATE.floorY()

    local x = root.Position.X + 6
    local y = type(floor) == "number" and floor + 3 or root.Position.Y
    local z = root.Position.Z

    local tpos = STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.pos
    if typeof(tpos) ~= "Vector3" then tpos = STEAL_STATE.lockPos end

    if typeof(tpos) == "Vector3" then
        local d = Vector3.new(
            tpos.X - root.Position.X, 0,
            tpos.Z - root.Position.Z
        )
        if d.Magnitude > 8 then
            local u = d.Unit
            x = root.Position.X + u.X * 16
            z = root.Position.Z + u.Z * 16
        end
    end

    if STEAL_STATE.carrying or root.Position.Y < (type(floor) == "number"
        and floor - 4 or -1e9) then
        if typeof(spawnPos) == "Vector3" then
            x = spawnPos.X
            y = spawnPos.Y + 3
            z = spawnPos.Z
        else
            y = type(floor) == "number" and floor + 3 or root.Position.Y
        end
    end

    pcall(function()
        root.Anchored = false
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, y, z)
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        if humanoid then
            humanoid.Sit = false
            humanoid.PlatformStand = P1.isFlying() == true
        end
    end)

    warn("[Kira/steal] unstuck reset", STEAL_STATE.state or "")
end

--=====================================================================
--  [20] Live carry detection
--=====================================================================
function STEAL_STATE.liveCarry()
    local eggs = readFieldEggs()
    if type(eggs) ~= "table" then return false, nil end

    for i = 1, #eggs do
        local rec = eggs[i]
        if type(rec) == "table"
            and tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId) == LocalPlayer.UserId then
            return true, rec.Uid
        end
    end
    return false, nil
end

--=====================================================================
--  [21] Adoption of carry state
--=====================================================================
function STEAL_STATE.adoptCarry()
    local carrying, uid = STEAL_STATE.liveCarry()

    if carrying then
        STEAL_STATE.carrying = true
        if uid then
            STEAL_STATE.carryUid = uid
            STEAL_STATE.heldUid = uid
            STEAL_STATE.lockUid = uid
        end

        local state = STEAL_STATE.state
        if state == "Return" or state == "Bank" or state == "FeedGo"
            or state == "Feed" or state == "ChestGo" or state == "ChestOpen" then
            return
        end

        local tgt = STEAL_STATE.target
        if tgt and tgt.event then
            STEAL_STATE.eventUid = STEAL_STATE.heldUid or STEAL_STATE.carryUid
                or STEAL_STATE.eventUid
            STEAL_STATE.eventFromField = true

            local eu = STEAL_STATE.eventUid
            if eu and STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool
                and STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool(eu) then
                if STEAL_STATE.state ~= "FeedGo" then
                    STEAL_STATE.feedDropped = false
                    STEAL_STATE.feedPad = 5
                    STEAL_STATE.state = "FeedGo"
                    STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                    warn("[Kira/steal] -> FeedGo (adopted carry)")
                end
                return
            end
        end

        if STEAL_STATE.state ~= "Return" then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Return"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        end
        return
    end

    STEAL_STATE.carrying = false

    local state = STEAL_STATE.state
    if state == "Return" or state == "Bank" then
        STEAL_STATE.lockAt = os.clock()
        if STEAL_STATE.lockUid and typeof(STEAL_STATE.lockPos) == "Vector3" then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            return
        end
        STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        return
    end

    if state == "FeedGo" or state == "Feed" then
        local eu = STEAL_STATE.eventUid or STEAL_STATE.heldUid
        if STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool
            and STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool(eu) then
            return
        end

        if STEAL_STATE.lookWhy == "need infested"
            or STEAL_STATE.lookWhy == "equip infested"
            or STEAL_STATE.lookWhy == "pocket infested" then
            return
        end

        if STEAL_STATE.lockUid and typeof(STEAL_STATE.lockPos) == "Vector3" then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            return
        end
        STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [22] Keep hook snapshot
--=====================================================================
function STEAL_STATE.keepHook(data)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return end

    local rec = data.rec
    local cfg = data.cfg

    if type(cfg) ~= "table" and rec then
        local cat = rec.AssetCategory or rec.Category
        cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
    end

    local cat = data.cat or (rec and (rec.AssetCategory or rec.Category))
    local name = data.name
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == ""
        or name == "egg" or name == "?" then
        name = cfg and cfg.DisplayName or cat
    end

    local icon = data.icon
    if type(icon) ~= "string" or icon == "" then
        if P1.esp and P1.esp.liveIcon then
            local ok, res = pcall(P1.esp.liveIcon, cfg, cat, name)
            if ok and type(res) == "string" and res ~= "" then icon = res end
        end
        if (type(icon) ~= "string" or icon == "")
            and P1.esp and P1.esp.icon then
            local ok, res = pcall(P1.esp.icon, cfg, cat)
            if ok and type(res) == "string" and res ~= "" then icon = res end
        end
    end

    STEAL_STATE.hookSnap = {
        rec = rec,
        cfg = cfg,
        cat = cat,
        name = name,
        area = data.area or "",
        earn = tonumber(data.earn) or ratePerSecond(rec, cfg),
        rar = type(cfg) == "table" and type(cfg.Rarity) == "table"
            and tostring(cfg.Rarity.DisplayName or cfg.Rarity.Name
                or cfg.Rarity._id or "?") or "?",
        uid = rec and rec.Uid or data.uid,
        icon = icon,
    }
end

--=====================================================================
--  [23] Status line update (v1231)
--=====================================================================
local function updateStatus()
    local w = Widgets.AutoSteal
    if not w or not w.status then return end

    local state = STEAL_STATE.state
    local target = STEAL_STATE.target
    local label = "idle"

    if state == "Night" then
        label = "night · " .. tostring(secondsToNight()) .. "s left"
    elseif state == "Scan" then
        label = "looking"
        if type(STEAL_STATE.lookWhy) == "string" and STEAL_STATE.lookWhy ~= "" then
            label = "looking · " .. STEAL_STATE.lookWhy
        end
    elseif state == "Safe" then
        label = "going to safe zone"
    elseif state == "Return" then
        label = "bringing back"
    elseif state == "Bank" then
        label = "banking"
    elseif state == "Chase" then
        label = target and target.name
            and "chasing · " .. tostring(target.name) or "chasing"
    elseif state == "GoEgg" or state == "Grab" then
        local verb = state ~= "Grab" and "going to" or "grabbing"
        local area = target and target.area and tostring(target.area) or ""
        local name = target and target.name and tostring(target.name) or ""
        if area == "" or name == "" then
            label = (name == "" and area == "") and verb
                or (verb .. " · " .. (name ~= "" and name or area))
        else
            label = verb .. " · " .. area .. " · " .. name
        end
    elseif state == "FeedGo" then
        label = (STEAL_STATE.lookWhy ~= "need infested"
            and STEAL_STATE.lookWhy ~= "equip infested"
            and STEAL_STATE.lookWhy ~= "pocket infested")
            and "going to monster" or STEAL_STATE.lookWhy
    elseif state == "Feed" then
        label = "feeding monster"
    elseif state == "ChestGo" then
        label = "picking up chest"
    elseif state == "ChestOpen" then
        label = "opening chest"
    elseif type(state) == "string" and state ~= "" and state ~= "Idle" then
        label = string.lower(state)
    end

    pcall(function()
        w.status.Text = string.format(
            "%s · took %d · lost %d · re-grabbed %d",
            label, STEAL_STATE.banked, STEAL_STATE.lost, STEAL_STATE.regrabs
        )
    end)

    -- Auto Event status
    local evW = Widgets.AutoEvent
    if evW and evW.status then
        pcall(function()
            local s = "off"
            if Flags.AutoEvent then
                if STEAL_STATE.lookWhy == "need infested"
                    or STEAL_STATE.lookWhy == "pocket infested"
                    or STEAL_STATE.lookWhy == "equip infested" then
                    s = "waiting for egg"
                elseif state == "FeedGo" then
                    s = "going to monster"
                elseif state == "Feed" then
                    s = "feeding"
                elseif state == "ChestGo" then
                    s = "picking up chest"
                elseif state == "ChestOpen" then
                    s = "opening chest"
                elseif not STEAL_STATE.target or not STEAL_STATE.target.event then
                    s = "waiting for filters"
                else
                    s = "grabbing infested"
                end
            end
            evW.status.Text = s .. " · fed " .. tostring(STEAL_STATE.fed or 0)
                .. " · chests " .. tostring(STEAL_STATE.chests or 0)
        end)
    end
end

P1.updateStatus = updateStatus

--=====================================================================
--  [24] Count took +1 (v1232)
--=====================================================================
local function countTook(reason)
    local uid = STEAL_STATE.heldUid or STEAL_STATE.carryUid
    if not uid or uid == STEAL_STATE.countedUid then return false end

    local snap = STEAL_STATE.hookSnap or STEAL_STATE.target
    STEAL_STATE.countedUid = uid
    STEAL_STATE.banked += 1
    STEAL_STATE.lastBankAt = os.clock()
    STEAL_STATE.heldUid = nil
    STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
    STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil
    STEAL_STATE.target = nil

    warn("[Kira/steal] took +1:", STEAL_STATE.banked,
        reason or "", tostring(uid):sub(1, 12))

    updateStatus()

    if P1.webhook and P1.webhook.stolen then
        pcall(P1.webhook.stolen, snap)
    end

    STEAL_STATE.hookSnap = nil
    return true
end

P1.countTook = countTook

--=====================================================================
--  [25] Start moving to target (v1233)
--=====================================================================
local function beginMoveToTarget(newTarget, reason)
    if type(newTarget) == "table" then
        STEAL_STATE.target = newTarget
        STEAL_STATE.keepHook(newTarget)

        if newTarget.rec and newTarget.rec.Uid then
            STEAL_STATE.lockUid = newTarget.rec.Uid
            STEAL_STATE.lockAt = os.clock()
        end
        if typeof(newTarget.pos) == "Vector3" then
            STEAL_STATE.lockPos = newTarget.pos
        end
    end

    engine.driving = true

    local humanoid, root = getChar()
    if not root then return end

    -- Reject plot eggs
    if STEAL_STATE.target and typeof(STEAL_STATE.target.pos) == "Vector3" then
        if isPlotEgg(STEAL_STATE.target.rec, STEAL_STATE.target.pos) then
            STEAL_STATE.target = nil
            STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        if (root.Position - STEAL_STATE.target.pos).Magnitude <= 28 then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end
    end

    -- Already on spawn
    local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
    if spawnPos and Vector3.new(
        spawnPos.X - root.Position.X, 0, spawnPos.Z - root.Position.Z
    ).Magnitude <= 28 then
        STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        updateStatus()
        return
    end

    -- On own plot → safe first
    if root then
        local bounds = boundsFromTreadmill(root.Position)
        if isOnOwnPlot(root.Position)
            or (bounds and insideBounds(bounds, root.Position, 18)) then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Safe"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end
    end

    STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
    STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
    updateStatus()
end

P1.beginMoveToTarget = beginMoveToTarget

--=====================================================================
--  [26] Handle pending carry event
--=====================================================================
local function handlePendingCarry(root)
    local pending = STEAL_STATE.pendingCarry
    if not pending then return end

    STEAL_STATE.pendingCarry = nil
    fieldCacheAt = 0
    STEAL_STATE.stillFor = 0

    local wasCarrying = STEAL_STATE.carrying
    STEAL_STATE.carrying = pending.carrying == true

    if type(pending.uid) == "string" then
        STEAL_STATE.carryUid = pending.uid
    elseif not STEAL_STATE.carrying then
        STEAL_STATE.carryUid = nil
    end

    if STEAL_STATE.carrying then
        STEAL_STATE.heldUid = pending.uid or STEAL_STATE.heldUid
        STEAL_STATE.lockUid = STEAL_STATE.heldUid or STEAL_STATE.lockUid

        if STEAL_STATE.target then
            STEAL_STATE.keepHook(STEAL_STATE.target)
        end

        if STEAL_STATE.state == "Grab" or STEAL_STATE.state == "GoEgg"
            or STEAL_STATE.state == "Chase" then
            if STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.event then
                STEAL_STATE.eventUid = STEAL_STATE.heldUid or STEAL_STATE.carryUid
                STEAL_STATE.eventFromField = true

                local eu = STEAL_STATE.eventUid
                if eu then STEAL_STATE.wearEventEgg(eu) end

                if eu and STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool
                    and STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool(eu) then
                    STEAL_STATE.state = "FeedGo"
                    STEAL_STATE.feedDropped = false
                    STEAL_STATE.feedPad = 5
                    STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                    updateStatus()
                    return
                end

                STEAL_STATE.lookWhy = "pocket infested"
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            STEAL_STATE.state = "Return"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
        end
        return
    end

    -- No longer carrying but was → dropped / lost
    if not wasCarrying or not Flags.AutoSteal then return end

    local onSafe = spawnPos and Vector3.new(
        spawnPos.X - root.Position.X, 0, spawnPos.Z - root.Position.Z
    ).Magnitude <= 28

    if STEAL_STATE.state == "Bank"
        or (STEAL_STATE.state == "Return" and onSafe) then
        countTook("carry-ended-at-safe")
        STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        updateStatus()
        engine.target = nil
        return
    end

    if STEAL_STATE.state == "FeedGo" or STEAL_STATE.state == "Feed" then
        if STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool
            and STEAL_STATE.findEventEggTool(
                STEAL_STATE.eventUid or STEAL_STATE.heldUid) then
            return
        end

        STEAL_STATE.lost += 1
        STEAL_STATE.regrabs += 1
        STEAL_STATE.lockUid = pending.uid or STEAL_STATE.heldUid
            or STEAL_STATE.lockUid
        STEAL_STATE.lockAt = os.clock()
        if root then STEAL_STATE.lockPos = root.Position end

        STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        updateStatus()
        return
    end

    if STEAL_STATE.state == "Return" or STEAL_STATE.state == "GoEgg"
        or STEAL_STATE.state == "Grab" or STEAL_STATE.state == "Chase" then
        STEAL_STATE.lost += 1
        STEAL_STATE.regrabs += 1
        STEAL_STATE.lockUid = pending.uid or STEAL_STATE.heldUid
            or STEAL_STATE.lockUid
        STEAL_STATE.lockAt = os.clock()
        if root then STEAL_STATE.lockPos = root.Position end

        warn("[Kira/steal] dropped — re-grab",
            tostring(STEAL_STATE.lockUid or "?"))

        STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        updateStatus()
    end
end

P1.handlePendingCarry = handlePendingCarry

--=====================================================================
--  [27] Refresh target (v1235)
--=====================================================================
local function refreshTarget(tgt)
    if not tgt or not tgt.rec then return nil, "no-target" end
    local uid = tgt.rec.Uid
    if not uid then return nil, "no-uid" end

    local found
    for _, rec in ipairs(readFieldEggs()) do
        if type(rec) == "table" and uid == rec.Uid then
            found = rec
            break
        end
    end

    if not found then
        for _, rec in ipairs(readFieldEggs(true)) do
            if type(rec) == "table" and uid == rec.Uid then
                found = rec
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if not found then return nil, "gone" end
    if isPlotEgg(found, positionFromRecord(found)) then return nil, "plot" end

    tgt.rec = found
    tgt.carrier = tonumber(found.CarrierUserId)

    local state = found.State
    if state == "Slot" or state == "Dropped" then
        local pos = positionFromRecord(found)
        if pos then tgt.pos = pos end
        tgt.carrier = nil
        if not tgt.pos then return nil, "no-pos" end
        return tgt
    end

    if state == "Carried" then
        tgt.pos = positionFromRecord(found) or tgt.pos
        return tgt, "Carried"
    end

    return nil, tostring(state or "bad-state")
end

P1.refreshTarget = refreshTarget

--=====================================================================
--  [28] Attempt grab (v1236)
--=====================================================================
local function attemptGrab(tgt)
    if STEAL_STATE.carrying then return end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - STEAL_STATE.lastGrab < 0.03 then return end
    STEAL_STATE.lastGrab = now

    local rec = tgt and tgt.rec
    local uid = rec and rec.Uid

    if uid then
        STEAL_STATE.heldUid = uid
        STEAL_STATE.lockUid = uid
        STEAL_STATE.lockAt = os.clock()
        if tgt then STEAL_STATE.keepHook(tgt) end
        if typeof(tgt.pos) == "Vector3" then
            STEAL_STATE.lockPos = tgt.pos
        end

        if EggState and type(EggState.CarryFieldEgg) == "function" then
            local slotKey = rec.FirstAreaSlotKey or rec.AreaId or rec.SlotKey
            pcall(EggState.CarryFieldEgg, uid, slotKey)
        end
    end

    local prompt = findCarryPrompt()
    local _, root = getChar()
    if not root then return end

    local inRange = typeof(tgt.pos) == "Vector3"
        and Vector3.new(
            root.Position.X - tgt.pos.X, 0,
            root.Position.Z - tgt.pos.Z
        ).Magnitude <= 7

    if inRange and promptIsTarget(prompt, tgt, 5) then
        firePromptNow(prompt)
    end
end

P1.attemptGrab = attemptGrab

--=====================================================================
--  [29] Main tick (u1132)
--=====================================================================
local function stealTick(dt)
    if not engine.loaded or not Flags.AutoSteal then return end

    dt = tonumber(dt) or engine.dt or 1/60

    antiTrap("tick")
    STEAL_STATE.muteHazards(true)
    if STEAL_STATE.muteBelt then
        STEAL_STATE.muteBelt(STEAL_STATE.allowTrain() ~= true)
    end

    -- Night cycle check
    local night = isNight()

    if night then
        local keepGoing = STEAL_STATE.state == "Return"
            or STEAL_STATE.state == "Bank"
            or STEAL_STATE.state == "FeedGo"
            or STEAL_STATE.state == "Feed"
            or STEAL_STATE.state == "ChestGo"
            or STEAL_STATE.state == "ChestOpen"
            or STEAL_STATE.state == "Safe"

        if not keepGoing then
            engine.target = nil
            engine.driving = false
            STEAL_STATE.target = nil

            if STEAL_STATE.state ~= "Night" then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Night"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
            end

            local sec = secondsToNight()
            if sec ~= STEAL_STATE.nightLeft then
                STEAL_STATE.nightLeft = sec
                updateStatus()
            end
            return
        end
    elseif STEAL_STATE.state == "Night" then
        STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        updateStatus()
    end

    local humanoid, root = getChar()

    handlePendingCarry(root)
    STEAL_STATE.adoptCarry()

    if not root then
        engine.target = nil
        return
    end

    if STEAL_STATE.rescueVoid(root, humanoid) then return end

    local elapsed = os.clock()
    local stateTime = elapsed - STEAL_STATE.since

    -- Safe state: go to spawn before stealing
    if STEAL_STATE.state == "Safe" then
        engine.driving = true
        antiTrap("safe")
        ragdollRescue()

        if STEAL_STATE.target and root
            and (select(1, findSpawn())
                and Vector3.new(
                    select(1, findSpawn()).X - root.Position.X, 0,
                    select(1, findSpawn()).Z - root.Position.Z
                ).Magnitude <= 62
                or not isOnOwnPlot(root.Position)) then

            local bounds = boundsFromTreadmill(root.Position)
            if (select(1, findSpawn())
                and Vector3.new(
                    select(1, findSpawn()).X - root.Position.X, 0,
                    select(1, findSpawn()).Z - root.Position.Z
                ).Magnitude <= 62)
                or not bounds
                or not insideBounds(bounds, root.Position, 18) then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end
        end

        local safePos, spawnPos = safeZone()
        if typeof(spawnPos) ~= "Vector3" then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        engine.target = safePos

        if Vector3.new(
            root.Position.X - spawnPos.X, 0,
            root.Position.Z - spawnPos.Z
        ).Magnitude < 16 then
            if night then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Night"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            if STEAL_STATE.target then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        if stateTime > 14 then
            if STEAL_STATE.target then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
        end
        return
    end

    -- Stuck detection
    if engine.target then
        local pos = root.Position
        if STEAL_STATE.lastPos
            and (pos - STEAL_STATE.lastPos).Magnitude < 1.2 then
            STEAL_STATE.stillFor += dt
        else
            STEAL_STATE.stillFor = 0
        end
        STEAL_STATE.lastPos = pos

        if STEAL_STATE.stillFor > 0.85 then
            antiTrap("stuck")
            ragdollRescue()
            if not P1.isFlying() then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.Jump = true
                    humanoid.AutoJumpEnabled = true
                end)
            end
        end

        if STEAL_STATE.stillFor > 2.6 then
            local st = STEAL_STATE.state
            if st ~= "Feed" and st ~= "FeedGo"
                and st ~= "ChestGo" and st ~= "ChestOpen" then
                STEAL_STATE.resetStuck(root, humanoid)
            else
                STEAL_STATE.stillFor = 0
            end

            if STEAL_STATE.state == "GoEgg"
                and not STEAL_STATE.carrying
                and not STEAL_STATE.lockUid then
                STEAL_STATE.target = nil
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end
        end
    else
        STEAL_STATE.stillFor = 0
        STEAL_STATE.lastPos = root.Position
    end

    -- Fallback movement when engine off
    if engine.target and not engine.on then
        local d = Vector3.new(
            engine.target.X - root.Position.X, 0,
            engine.target.Z - root.Position.Z
        )
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            humanoid.Sit = false
            humanoid.AutoRotate = true

            if engine.driving then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(
                    tonumber(Flags.StealSpeed) or 16, 16, 1300)
            else
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = engine.baseWalk
            end

            if d.Magnitude > 1.2 then
                humanoid:Move(d.Unit, false)
            else
                humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Delegate event handling (from Part 7)
    if P1.runEvent and P1.runEvent(root, stateTime) then return end

    -- Main state machine
    local st = STEAL_STATE.state

    if st == "Idle" or st == "Scan" then
        if Flags.AutoEvent and not STEAL_STATE.chestSkip then
            if STEAL_STATE.findChestTool
                and STEAL_STATE.findChestTool() then
                engine.driving = true
                STEAL_STATE.state = "ChestOpen"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                STEAL_STATE.chestClaimed = false
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            if STEAL_STATE.worldChest and STEAL_STATE.worldChest() then
                engine.driving = true
                STEAL_STATE.state = "ChestGo"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                STEAL_STATE.chestAsked = false
                updateStatus()
                return
            end
        end

        local pick = pickBestTarget()
        if pick and pick.matched then
            engine.driving = true
            beginMoveToTarget(pick, pick.area or "egg")
            return
        end

        local feedReady = elapsed >= (STEAL_STATE.feedLockUntil or 0)

        if Flags.AutoEvent then
            local satchel
            if feedReady and STEAL_STATE.pickSatchelEvent then
                satchel = STEAL_STATE.pickSatchelEvent()
            end

            if satchel then
                STEAL_STATE.eventUid = satchel.uid
                STEAL_STATE.eventFromField = false
                engine.driving = true
                STEAL_STATE.state = "FeedGo"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                STEAL_STATE.feedDropped = false
                STEAL_STATE.feedPad = 5
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            if feedReady and pick and pick.event then
                engine.driving = true
                beginMoveToTarget(pick, "event")
                return
            end
        elseif pick and pick.matched then
            engine.driving = true
            beginMoveToTarget(pick, pick.area)
            return
        end

        STEAL_STATE.target = nil
        STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
        STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil

        if not P1.plot or not P1.plot.driving or not P1.plot.driving() then
            engine.driving = false
            engine.target = nil
        end

        local all = readFieldEggs()
        local total = type(all) == "table" and #all or 0
        local openCount = 0
        local matchCount = 0

        if type(all) == "table" then
            for i = 1, total do
                local rec = all[i]
                if type(rec) == "table" then
                    local carrier = tonumber(rec.CarrierUserId)
                    local isFree = (rec.State == "Slot" or rec.State == "Dropped")
                        and (not carrier or carrier == 0
                            or carrier == LocalPlayer.UserId)
                        and not isPlotEgg(rec)

                    if isFree then
                        openCount += 1
                        local cat = rec.AssetCategory
                        local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
                        if matchesFilters(rec, cfg) then
                            local ok = true
                            if (Flags.StealMode or "Best value") == "Gen ($/s) snipe" then
                                local floor = numberFromString(Flags.GenSnipeFloor)
                                ok = not (floor > 0)
                                    or not (floor > ratePerSecond(rec, cfg))
                            end
                            if ok then matchCount += 1 end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if not feedReady and Flags.AutoEvent then
            STEAL_STATE.lookWhy = "feed wait"
        elseif total == 0 then
            STEAL_STATE.lookWhy = "map empty"
        elseif openCount == 0 then
            STEAL_STATE.lookWhy = "nests empty"
        elseif matchCount == 0 then
            STEAL_STATE.lookWhy = "no match"
        else
            STEAL_STATE.lookWhy = tostring(matchCount) .. " open"
        end

        STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
        updateStatus()
        return
    end

    if st == "GoEgg" then
        local tgt = STEAL_STATE.target
        if not tgt then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        antiTrap("goegg")
        ragdollRescue()

        local refreshed, why = refreshTarget(tgt)

        if why == "plot" then
            STEAL_STATE.target = nil
            STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
            STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        if why == "Carried" then
            local carrier = tonumber(tgt.carrier
                or (tgt.rec and tgt.rec.CarrierUserId))
            if carrier == LocalPlayer.UserId then
                STEAL_STATE.carrying = true
                STEAL_STATE.carryUid = tgt.rec and tgt.rec.Uid
                STEAL_STATE.heldUid = STEAL_STATE.carryUid
                STEAL_STATE.adoptCarry()
                return
            end

            if Flags.BatAura then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Chase"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            if stateTime > 3 then
                STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
                STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil
                STEAL_STATE.target = nil
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
            end
            return
        end

        if refreshed then
            if typeof(tgt.pos) == "Vector3" then
                STEAL_STATE.lockPos = tgt.pos
            end

            local dest = tgt.pos
            if typeof(dest) == "Vector3" then
                dest = P1.isFlying()
                    and Vector3.new(dest.X, dest.Y + 1.5, dest.Z)
                    or Vector3.new(dest.X, dest.Y - 2.5, dest.Z)
            end

            engine.target = safeRoute(dest, root.Position, true)
            engine.driving = true

            if typeof(engine.target) == "Vector3"
                and typeof(dest) == "Vector3" then
                local dxz = Vector3.new(
                    engine.target.X - root.Position.X, 0,
                    engine.target.Z - root.Position.Z
                ).Magnitude
                local dxz2 = Vector3.new(
                    dest.X - root.Position.X, 0,
                    dest.Z - root.Position.Z
                ).Magnitude
                if dxz < 8 and dxz2 > 20 then
                    engine.target = dest
                end
            end

            local dist = Vector3.new(
                root.Position.X - dest.X, 0,
                root.Position.Z - dest.Z
            ).Magnitude
            local dy = root.Position.Y - dest.Y

            if not P1.isFlying() and dist <= 16 then
                engine.target = dest
            end

            local reachDist = (tgt.rec and tgt.rec.State == "Dropped")
                and 16 or 10

            if P1.isFlying() then
                if dist <= reachDist and dy < 18 then
                    attemptGrab(tgt)
                end
                if dist <= 5 and dy < 10 then
                    STEAL_STATE.state = "Grab"
                    STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                    updateStatus()
                    return
                end
            else
                local fullDist = (root.Position - dest).Magnitude
                if fullDist <= reachDist then attemptGrab(tgt) end
                if fullDist <= 4 then
                    STEAL_STATE.state = "Grab"
                    STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                    updateStatus()
                    return
                end
            end
        else
            fieldCacheAt = 0
            engine.target = nil
            engine.driving = false

            if stateTime > 2.5 then
                warn("[Kira/steal] egg gone:", why or "")
                STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
                STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil
                STEAL_STATE.target = nil
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end
        end

        local timeout = not STEAL_STATE.lockUid and 18 or 45
        if stateTime > timeout then
            warn("[Kira/steal] GoEgg timeout, rescan")
            fieldCacheAt = 0
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
        end
        return
    end

    if st == "Grab" then
        local tgt = STEAL_STATE.target
        if not tgt then
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        antiTrap("grab")
        ragdollRescue()

        local refreshed, why = refreshTarget(tgt)

        if why == "Carried" then
            local carrier = tonumber(tgt.carrier
                or (tgt.rec and tgt.rec.CarrierUserId))
            if carrier == LocalPlayer.UserId then
                STEAL_STATE.carrying = true
                STEAL_STATE.carryUid = tgt.rec and tgt.rec.Uid
                STEAL_STATE.heldUid = STEAL_STATE.carryUid
                STEAL_STATE.adoptCarry()
                return
            end

            if Flags.BatAura then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Chase"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            if stateTime > 3 then
                STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
                STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil
                STEAL_STATE.target = nil
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
            end
            return
        end

        if refreshed then
            local dest = tgt.pos
            if typeof(dest) == "Vector3" then
                dest = P1.isFlying()
                    and Vector3.new(dest.X, dest.Y + 1.5, dest.Z)
                    or Vector3.new(dest.X, dest.Y - 2.5, dest.Z)
            end

            engine.target = safeRoute(dest, root.Position, true)

            if typeof(tgt.pos) == "Vector3" then
                STEAL_STATE.lockPos = tgt.pos
                STEAL_STATE.lockAt = os.clock()
            end
        else
            engine.target = nil
            engine.driving = false

            if stateTime > 2.5 then
                warn("[Kira/steal] grab lost:", why or "")
                STEAL_STATE.lockUid = nil
                STEAL_STATE.lockPos = nil
                STEAL_STATE.target = nil
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end
        end

        engine.driving = true
        attemptGrab(tgt)

        if STEAL_STATE.carrying then
            STEAL_STATE.adoptCarry()
            return
        end

        local timeout = not STEAL_STATE.lockUid
            and ((not tgt.rec or tgt.rec.State ~= "Dropped") and 8 or 30)
            or 30
        if stateTime > timeout then
            warn("[Kira/steal] Grab timeout")
            fieldCacheAt = 0
