            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
        end
        return
    end

    if st == "Chase" then
        local tgt = STEAL_STATE.target
        if not tgt or not Flags.BatAura then
            STEAL_STATE.target = nil
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        local refreshed, why = refreshTarget(tgt)

        if why ~= "Carried" then
            if refreshed then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            STEAL_STATE.target = nil
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        local thiefPos, thiefPlayer
        if STEAL_STATE.bat then
            thiefPos, thiefPlayer = STEAL_STATE.bat.posOf(tgt.carrier)
        end

        if thiefPos then
            engine.target = thiefPos
            engine.driving = true
            if thiefPlayer and STEAL_STATE.bat then
                STEAL_STATE.bat.swingAt(thiefPlayer)
            end
        elseif stateTime > 4 then
            STEAL_STATE.target = nil
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        if stateTime > 20 then
            STEAL_STATE.target = nil
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
        end
        return
    end

    if st == "Return" then
        if not STEAL_STATE.carrying then
            if STEAL_STATE.lockUid and typeof(STEAL_STATE.lockPos) == "Vector3" then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        local safePos = safeZone()
        if typeof(select(1, findSpawn())) ~= "Vector3" then
            warn("[Kira/steal] no safe zone")
            engine.target = nil
            return
        end

        engine.target = safePos
        engine.driving = true

        local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
        if spawnPos and Vector3.new(
            spawnPos.X - root.Position.X, 0,
            spawnPos.Z - root.Position.Z
        ).Magnitude <= 28 then
            local carryingLive = STEAL_STATE.liveCarry
                and select(1, STEAL_STATE.liveCarry())
            if carryingLive then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "Bank"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            STEAL_STATE.carrying = false

            if STEAL_STATE.lockUid
                and typeof(STEAL_STATE.lockPos) == "Vector3" then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        if stateTime > 28 then
            warn("[Kira/steal] Return timeout")
            STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
        end
        return
    end

    if st == "Bank" then
        local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
        engine.target = typeof(spawnPos) == "Vector3" and safeZone() or spawnPos

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - STEAL_STATE.lastBankTry >= 0.45 then
            STEAL_STATE.lastBankTry = now
        end

        if not STEAL_STATE.carrying then
            countTook("empty-hands-at-safe")
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        local live = STEAL_STATE.liveCarry
            and select(1, STEAL_STATE.liveCarry())
        if not live then
            STEAL_STATE.carrying = false

            if STEAL_STATE.lockUid
                and typeof(STEAL_STATE.lockPos) == "Vector3" then
                STEAL_STATE.state = "GoEgg"
                STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
                updateStatus()
                return
            end

            STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
            return
        end

        if stateTime > 4 then
            warn("[Kira/steal] Bank timeout — still carrying")
            STEAL_STATE.state = "Return"
            STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
            updateStatus()
        end
        return
    end
end

P1.stealTick = stealTick
engine.stealTick = stealTick

--=====================================================================
--  [30] Stop loop (v1237)
--=====================================================================
local function stopSteal()
    STEAL_STATE.running = false
    engine.driving = false
    engine.target = nil
    STEAL_STATE.target = nil
    STEAL_STATE.eventUid = nil
    STEAL_STATE.pendingCarry = nil
    STEAL_STATE.haltUntil = os.clock() + 2.2

    STEAL_STATE.muteHazards(false)
    if STEAL_STATE.muteBelt then STEAL_STATE.muteBelt(false) end

    if STEAL_STATE.state ~= "Idle" then
        warn("[Kira/steal]", STEAL_STATE.state, "-> Idle (disabled)")
        STEAL_STATE.state = "Idle"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
    end

    updateStatus()

    if P1.plot and P1.plot.stop then pcall(P1.plot.stop) end

    local humanoid, root = getChar()
    pcall(function()
        if root then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        if humanoid then
            humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            humanoid.Sit = false
            if not Flags.Flight then
                humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Rescue if below floor
    if root and root.Position.Y < 58 then
        local spawnPos = select(1, findSpawn())
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(spawnPos) == "Vector3" and spawnPos.Y >= 58 then
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(spawnPos.X, spawnPos.Y + 6, spawnPos.Z)
            else
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 74, 0)
            end
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    if not Flags.Flight then
        restoreHumanoid(humanoid, root, true)
    end

    if P1.setEngineOn then
        P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
    end

    warn("[Kira/steal] loop stopped")
end

P1.stopSteal = stopSteal

--=====================================================================
--  [31] CarryChanged listener
--=====================================================================
if EggState and EggState.CarryChanged and EggState.CarryChanged.Connect then
    trackConn(EggState.CarryChanged:Connect(function(evt)
        if type(evt) ~= "table" then return end
        local uid = evt.Uid
        STEAL_STATE.pendingCarry = {
            carrying = evt.IsCarrying == true,
            uid = type(uid) == "string" and uid or nil,
        }
    end))
end

--=====================================================================
--  [32] Widget wiring
--=====================================================================
local function wire(flag, fn)
    local w = Widgets[flag]
    if not w then
        warn("[Kira/wire] missing widget:", flag)
        return
    end
    w.on = fn
end

wire("AutoSteal", function(on)
    if on then
        if STEAL_STATE.running then return end
        STEAL_STATE.running = true
        STEAL_STATE.stillFor = 0
        STEAL_STATE.lastPos = nil
        STEAL_STATE.pendingCarry = nil
        STEAL_STATE.state = "Scan"
        STEAL_STATE.since = os.clock()
        updateStatus()
        engine.driving = false
        engine.target = nil

        if P1.setEngineOn then
            P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
        end

        warn("[Kira/steal] loop started — travel:", Flags.StealTravel)
        return
    end
    stopSteal()
end)

wire("AutoEvent", function()
    updateStatus()
end)

wire("AntiTrap", function(on)
    warn("[Kira/engine] anti trap:", on)
    if P1.setEngineOn then
        P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
    end
    if internals and internals.charMute then
        -- will be handled by tick
    end
end)

wire("AntiMob", function(on)
    warn("[Kira/engine] anti ragdoll:", on)
    if P1.setEngineOn then
        P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
    end
end)

wire("StealTravel", function(mode)
    warn("[Kira/wire] travel mode:", tostring(mode))
    if Flags.AutoSteal and P1.setEngineOn then
        P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
    end
end)

wire("StealMode", function(mode)
    warn("[Kira/steal] pick mode:", tostring(mode))
end)

wire("StealSpeed", function(v)
    warn("[Kira/engine] steal speed:", v)
end)

wire("BypassCap", function(v)
    warn("[Kira/engine] bypass cap:", v)
end)

wire("Flight", function(on)
    warn("[Kira/engine] flight:", on)
    if on then
        internals.cleanupSupport()
        local _, root = getChar()
        if root then
            pcall(function()
                local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end
    else
        local humanoid, root = getChar()
        engine.wasOn = false
        restoreHumanoid(humanoid, root, true)
    end

    if P1.setEngineOn then
        P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
    end
end)

wire("FlightSpeed", function(v)
    if Flags.Flight then warn("[Kira/engine] flight speed:", v) end
end)

--=====================================================================
updateStatus()

print("[Kira] Part 6/9 loaded — Auto Steal state machine ready.")

--=====================================================================
--  [2] Number formatter
--=====================================================================
local function formatShort(n)
    local v = tonumber(n) or 0
    local a = math.abs(v)
    if a >= 1e12 then return string.format("%.2fT", v/1e12) end
    if a >= 1e9  then return string.format("%.2fB", v/1e9)  end
    if a >= 1e6  then return string.format("%.2fm", v/1e6)  end
    if a >= 1e3  then return string.format("%.1fk", v/1e3)  end
    if a >= 10   then return string.format("%.0f", v)      end
    return string.format("%.1f", v)
end

-- Try game's FormatAbbreviated
pcall(function()
    local fmt = require(ReplicatedStorage.UserGenerated.Strings.FormatAbbreviated)
    if type(fmt) == "function" then
        local old = formatShort
        formatShort = function(n)
            local v = tonumber(n) or 0
            local ok, res = pcall(fmt, v)
            if ok and type(res) == "string" and res ~= "" then return res end
            if math.abs(v) >= 1e6 then return string.format("%.2fm", v/1e6) end
            return string.format("%.0f", v)
        end
    end
end)

P1.formatShort = formatShort

--=====================================================================
--  [3] Rarity colors
--=====================================================================
local function rarityColor(idx)
    local n = tonumber(idx) or 0
    if n >= 11 then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 150) end
    if n >= 10 then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 210)  end
    if n >= 9  then return Color3.fromRGB(120, 210, 255) end
    if n >= 8  then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 110)  end
    if n >= 7  then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 186, 70)  end
    if n >= 6  then return Color3.fromRGB(186, 120, 255) end
    if n >= 5  then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 90)  end
    if n >= 4  then return Color3.fromRGB(160, 120, 255) end
    if n >= 3  then return Color3.fromRGB(90, 170, 255)  end
    if n >= 2  then return Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 120) end
    return Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [4] ESP folder + world gui
--=====================================================================
local function getESPWorld()
    local parent = ScreenGui and ScreenGui.Parent
    if not parent then
        local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        parent = pg or ScreenGui
    end

    -- Cleanup stale
    local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg then
        local old = pg:FindFirstChild(WORLD_NAME) or pg:FindFirstChild("KiraWorldGui")
        if old and old ~= ESP.world then
            pcall(function() old:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    local world = ESP.world
    if not world or not world.Parent or not world:IsA("ScreenGui") then
        world = parent:FindFirstChild(WORLD_NAME)
    end

    if world and world:IsA("ScreenGui") then
        if parent ~= world.Parent and parent then world.Parent = parent end
        world.Enabled = true
        world.ResetOnSpawn = false
        ESP.world = world
        return world
    end

    world = new("ScreenGui", {
        Name           = WORLD_NAME,
        ResetOnSpawn   = false,
        IgnoreGuiInset = true,
        DisplayOrder   = 80,
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        Parent         = parent,
    })
    ESP.world = world
    return world
end

local function getESPFolder()
    local existing = workspace:FindFirstChild("KiraEsp")
    if existing and existing:IsA("Folder") then
        ESP.folder = existing
        return existing
    end

    if ESP.folder and ESP.folder.Parent == workspace then
        return ESP.folder
    end

    if ESP.folder then
        pcall(function() ESP.folder:Destroy() end)
    end

    local f = new("Folder", { Name = "KiraEsp", Parent = workspace })
    ESP.folder = f
    return f
end

--=====================================================================
--  [5] Icon scanner (Directory → iconBy map)
--=====================================================================
local ICON_KEYS = {
    "IndexImage", "IndexIcon", "Icon", "Image", "ImageId",
    "IconImage", "Thumbnail", "AssetImage", "PetImage",
    "EggImage", "RenderImage", "Picture",
}

local function iconFromValue(v)
    if v == nil then return nil end

    local tp = typeof(v)
    if tp == "number" then
        if v > 100 then return "rbxassetid://" .. math.floor(v) end
        return nil
    end

    if tp == "string" then
        if v == "" or v == "0" or v == "rbxassetid://0" then return nil end
        if v:find("http", 1, true) or v:find("rbxasset", 1, true)
            or v:find("rbxthumb", 1, true) then
            return v
        end
        local num = tonumber(v)
        if num and num > 100 then
            return "rbxassetid://" .. math.floor(num)
        end
        return nil
    end

    if tp == "Instance" then
        if v:IsA("ImageLabel") or v:IsA("ImageButton") then
            return iconFromValue(v.Image)
        end
        if v:IsA("Decal") or v:IsA("Texture") then
            return iconFromValue(v.Texture)
        end
        local child = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageLabel", true)
            or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageButton", true)
            or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal", true)
        if child then return iconFromValue(child) end
        return nil
    end

    if tp == "table" then
        return iconFromValue(v.Image) or iconFromValue(v.ImageId)
            or iconFromValue(v.Icon) or iconFromValue(v.Id)
    end
end

local function cacheIcon(key, icon)
    if type(key) ~= "string" or key == "" or not icon then return end
    local k = string.lower(key)
    ESP.iconBy[k] = icon
    local stripped = k:gsub("[%s_%-]+", "")
    if stripped ~= k then ESP.iconBy[stripped] = icon end
end

local function registerCfg(catKey, cfg)
    if type(cfg) ~= "table" then return end

    local found
    for i = 1, #ICON_KEYS do
        found = iconFromValue(cfg[ICON_KEYS[i]])
        if found then break end
    end

    if not found and type(cfg.Egg) == "table" then
        for i = 1, #ICON_KEYS do
            found = iconFromValue(cfg.Egg[ICON_KEYS[i]])
            if found then break end
        end
    end

    if not found then return end

    cacheIcon(catKey, found)
    if type(cfg.DisplayName) == "string" then cacheIcon(cfg.DisplayName, found) end
    if type(cfg._id) == "string" then cacheIcon(cfg._id, found) end
    if type(cfg.Egg) == "table" and type(cfg.Egg.DisplayName) == "string" then
        cacheIcon(cfg.Egg.DisplayName, found)
    end
end

local function buildAliasMap()
    local map = {}
    if type(AssetDirectory) ~= "table" then return map end

    for k, v in pairs(AssetDirectory) do
        local s = tostring(k)
        map[string.lower(s)] = s

        if type(v) == "table" then
            if v.DisplayName then
                map[string.lower(tostring(v.DisplayName))] = s
            end
            if v._id then
                map[string.lower(tostring(v._id))] = s
            end
            if type(v.Egg) == "table" and v.Egg.DisplayName then
                map[string.lower(tostring(v.Egg.DisplayName))] = s
            end
        end
    end
    return map
end

local function scanIconsRecursive(root, limit)
    if not root then return end
    local ok, descendants = pcall(function() return root:GetDescendants() end)
    if not ok or type(descendants) ~= "table" then return end

    local n = 0
    for i = 1, #descendants do
        n = n + 1
        if n > limit then return end

        local d = descendants[i]
        local isImg = false
        pcall(function()
            isImg = d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton")
        end)

        if isImg then
            local icon = iconFromValue(d)
            if icon then
                cacheIcon(d.Name, icon)
                if d.Parent then cacheIcon(d.Parent.Name, icon) end

                pcall(function()
                    local cat = d:GetAttribute("AssetCategory")
                    if type(cat) == "string" then cacheIcon(cat, icon) end
                    local id = d:GetAttribute("Id")
                    if type(id) == "string" then cacheIcon(id, icon) end
                    if d.Parent then
                        local cat2 = d.Parent:GetAttribute("AssetCategory")
                        if type(cat2) == "string" then cacheIcon(cat2, icon) end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function looksLikeIndex(name)
    local s = string.lower(tostring(name or ""))
    return s:find("index", 1, true) ~= nil
        or s:find("bestiary", 1, true) ~= nil
        or s:find("collection", 1, true) ~= nil
        or s:find("pedia", 1, true) ~= nil
end

local function scanIcons(force)
    local now = os.clock()
    if not force and now - (ESP.iconScanAt or 0) < 8 then return end
    ESP.iconScanAt = now

    -- From Directory
    if type(AssetDirectory) == "table" then
        for k, v in pairs(AssetDirectory) do
            registerCfg(k, v)
        end
    end

    local aliases = buildAliasMap()
    local assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets")

    pcall(scanIconsRecursive, assets and assets:FindFirstChild("UI"), 6000)
    pcall(scanIconsRecursive, ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Directory"), 8000)
    pcall(scanIconsRecursive, ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Data"), 4000)

    -- Player GUI indexes
    local function scanGUI(root)
        if not root then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
            if child ~= ScreenGui and looksLikeIndex(child.Name) then
                pcall(scanIconsRecursive, child, 8000)
            end
        end
    end

    scanGUI(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"))
    pcall(scanGUI, game:GetService("StarterGui"))

    -- Cross-reference text labels to icons
    pcall(function()
        local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
            if child == ScreenGui or not looksLikeIndex(child.Name) then continue end

            for _, d in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                local isText = false
                pcall(function()
                    isText = d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton")
                end)

                if isText and type(d.Text) == "string" and d.Text ~= "" then
                    local cat = aliases[string.lower(d.Text)]
                    if cat and d.Parent then
                        for _, sib in ipairs(d.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
                            local isImg = false
                            pcall(function()
                                isImg = sib:IsA("ImageLabel") or sib:IsA("ImageButton")
                            end)
                            if isImg then
                                local icon = iconFromValue(sib)
                                if icon then
                                    cacheIcon(cat, icon)
                                    cacheIcon(d.Text, icon)
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function getIcon(cfg, cat, name)
    registerCfg(cat, cfg)

    local lookup = {}
    local function add(k)
        if type(k) ~= "string" or k == "" then return end
        local lower = string.lower(k)
        lookup[#lookup+1] = lower
        local stripped = lower:gsub("[%s_%-]+", "")
        if stripped ~= lower then lookup[#lookup+1] = stripped end
    end

    add(name)
    if type(cfg) == "table" then
        add(cfg.DisplayName)
        add(cfg._id)
        if type(cfg.Egg) == "table" then add(cfg.Egg.DisplayName) end
    end

    for i = 1, #lookup do
        local v = ESP.iconBy[lookup[i]]
        if v then return v end
    end
end

local function liveIcon(cfg, cat, name)
    local keys = {}
    local function add(k)
        if type(k) ~= "string" or k == "" then return end
        local lower = string.lower(k)
        keys[#keys+1] = lower
        local stripped = lower:gsub("[%s_%-]+", "")
        if stripped ~= lower then keys[#keys+1] = stripped end
    end

    add(name)
    if type(cfg) == "table" then
        add(cfg.DisplayName)
        add(cfg._id)
        if type(cfg.Egg) == "table" then add(cfg.Egg.DisplayName) end
    end

    local keySet = {}
    for _, k in ipairs(keys) do keySet[k] = true end

    -- Match against pool (billboard titles)
    for _, item in pairs(ESP.pool) do
        if item.icon and item.icon.Image and item.icon.Image ~= "" then
            local title = string.lower(tostring(item.title and item.title.Text or ""))
            title = title:gsub("^▸%s*", ""):gsub("^>%s*", "")
            local stripped = title:gsub("[%s_%-]+", "")
            if (title ~= "" and keySet[title])
                or (stripped ~= "" and keySet[stripped]) then
                return item.icon.Image
            end
        end
    end

    for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
        local v = ESP.iconBy[k]
        if v then return v end
    end
end

ESP.scanIcons = scanIcons
ESP.icon = getIcon
ESP.liveIcon = liveIcon

--=====================================================================
--  [6] Billboard construction
--=====================================================================
local function makeBillboard()
    local world = getESPWorld()

    local bb = new("BillboardGui", {
        AlwaysOnTop = true,
        LightInfluence = 0,
        MaxDistance = 1e6,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(152, 34),
        StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.2, 0),
        ResetOnSpawn = false,
        Active = false,
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        Parent = world,
    })

    local card = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 15, 14),
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.34,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        ZIndex = 1,
        Active = false,
    }, bb)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, card)

    local stroke = new("UIStroke", {
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 64, 56),
        Transparency = 0.38,
        Thickness = 1,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
    }, card)

    local accent = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210),
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, -8),
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 4),
        ZIndex = 2,
        Active = false,
    }, card)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2) }, accent)

    local icon = new("ImageLabel", {
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 26, 24),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 6),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22),
        Image = "",
        ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
        Visible = false,
        ZIndex = 2,
        Active = false,
    }, card)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, icon)

    local title = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 14),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(246, 242, 234),
        TextSize = 11,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        ZIndex = 2,
        Active = false,
    }, card)

    local sub = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 16),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 14),
        Font = FONTS.mono,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(168, 158, 144),
        TextSize = 9,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
        TextWrapped = false,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        ZIndex = 2,
        Active = false,
    }, card)

    return {
        bb = bb,
        card = card,
        stroke = stroke,
        accent = accent,
        icon = icon,
        title = title,
        sub = sub,
    }
end

local function paintBillboard(bill, target)
    local light = Flags.Theme == "Light"
    local isTarget = target and target.target

    if light then
        bill.card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(252, 250, 246)
        bill.card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.28
        bill.title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 24, 20)
        bill.sub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(92, 84, 74)
        bill.stroke.Color = isTarget and ActiveTheme.accent
            or Color3.fromRGB(188, 178, 164)
        bill.stroke.Transparency = (not isTarget) and 0.32 or 0.12
        if bill.icon then
            bill.icon.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 226, 216)
            bill.icon.BackgroundTransparency = (not bill.icon.Visible) and 1 or 0.42
        end
    else
        bill.card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 15, 14)
        bill.card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.34
        bill.title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(246, 242, 234)
        bill.sub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(168, 158, 144)
        bill.stroke.Color = isTarget and ActiveTheme.accent
            or Color3.fromRGB(58, 53, 46)
        bill.stroke.Transparency = (not isTarget) and 0.38 or 0.08
        if bill.icon then
            bill.icon.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 26, 24)
            bill.icon.BackgroundTransparency = (not bill.icon.Visible) and 1 or 0.5
        end
    end

    bill.stroke.Thickness = (not isTarget) and 1 or 1.5
end

local function spawnBillboard(key, worldPos, info)
    if not worldPos then return end

    local item = ESP.pool[key]
    if item and (not item.bb or not item.bb.Parent) then
        if item.dummy then pcall(function() item.dummy:Destroy() end) end
        item = nil
        ESP.pool[key] = nil
    end

    if not item then
        item = makeBillboard()
        ESP.pool[key] = item
    end

    item.alive = true
    item.pos = worldPos

    local folder = getESPFolder()

    if not item.dummy or not item.dummy.Parent then
        if item.dummy then pcall(function() item.dummy:Destroy() end) end
        local p = new("Part", {
            Name = "KiraEspAdorn",
            Anchored = true,
            CanCollide = false,
            CanQuery = false,
            CanTouch = false,
            CastShadow = false,
            Transparency = 1,
            Size = Vector3.new(0.15, 0.15, 0.15),
            Parent = folder,
        })
        item.dummy = p
    end

    pcall(function()
        item.dummy.CFrame = CFrame.new(worldPos)
    end)
    item.bb.Adornee = item.dummy
    item.bb.Parent = getESPWorld()
    item.bb.Enabled = true

    local color = info.color or Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210)
    item.title.Text = tostring(info.name or "")
    item.sub.Text = tostring(info.sub or "")
    item.accent.BackgroundColor3 = color

    local h = info.tall and 46 or 34
    local icon = info.icon
    local w = icon and 180 or 152

    if item.icon then
        if icon then
            item.icon.Image = icon
            item.icon.Visible = true
            item.title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 2)
            item.title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 14)
            item.sub.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 16)
            item.sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, info.tall and 26 or 14)
            item.sub.TextWrapped = info.tall == true

            local cleanName = tostring(info.name or ""):gsub("^▸%s*", ""):gsub("^>%s*", "")
            cacheIcon(cleanName, icon)
        else
            item.icon.Image = ""
            item.icon.Visible = false
            item.title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2)
            item.title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 14)
            item.sub.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 16)
            item.sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, info.tall and 26 or 14)
            item.sub.TextWrapped = info.tall == true
        end
    end

    paintBillboard(item, info)
    item.bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
end

local function reapPool()
    for k, item in pairs(ESP.pool) do
        if not item.alive then
            if item.bb then item.bb:Destroy() end
            if item.dummy then pcall(function() item.dummy:Destroy() end) end
            ESP.pool[k] = nil
        else
            item.alive = false
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [7] Weight + mutations extraction
--=====================================================================
local function weightFrom(rec, cfg)
    local w
    if type(rec) == "table" then
        w = tonumber(rec.Weight) or tonumber(rec.ModelWeight)
            or tonumber(rec.Kg) or tonumber(rec.BaseWeight)
    end
    if (not w or w <= 0) and type(cfg) == "table" then
        w = tonumber(cfg.ModelWeight) or tonumber(cfg.Weight) or tonumber(cfg.BaseWeight)
        if (not w or w <= 0) and type(cfg.Egg) == "table" then
            w = tonumber(cfg.Egg.ModelWeight) or tonumber(cfg.Egg.Weight)
        end
    end
    return w
end

local function weightText(rec, cfg)
    local w = weightFrom(rec, cfg)
    if not w or w <= 0 then return nil end
    if w >= 100 then return string.format("%.0f kg", w) end
    return string.format("%.1f kg", w)
end

local function mutationsText(rec)
    if type(rec) ~= "table" then return nil end
    if type(rec.Mutations) ~= "table" or #rec.Mutations == 0 then return nil end
    return table.concat(rec.Mutations, " · ")
end

--=====================================================================
--  [8] Chance extraction (1 in X)
--=====================================================================
local function chanceText(rec, cfg)
    if type(cfg) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local r = cfg.Rarity
    if type(r) ~= "table" then return nil end

    local def = r.DefaultRarityValue
    if type(def) ~= "string" or def == "" then return nil end

    local s = def:gsub(",", ""):gsub("1 in ", "1/"):gsub(" ", "")
    if string.sub(s, 1, 2) == "1/" then
        local n = tonumber(string.sub(s, 3))
        if not n then return s end
        if n >= 1e12 then return string.format("1/%.2ft", n/1e12) end
        if n >= 1e9  then return string.format("1/%.2fb", n/1e9)  end
        if n >= 1e6  then return string.format("1/%.2fm", n/1e6)  end
        if n >= 1e3  then return string.format("1/%.0fk", n/1e3)  end
        return s
    end
    return nil
end

--=====================================================================
--  [9] ESP tick (main)
--=====================================================================
local function spawnInfoFor(rec, cfg, isTarget)
    local name = cfg and (cfg.DisplayName
        or (type(cfg.Egg) == "table" and cfg.Egg.DisplayName))
        or tostring(rec.AssetCategory or "?")

    if isTarget then name = "▸ " .. tostring(name) end

    local earn = formatShort(ratePerSecond(rec, cfg)) .. "/s"
    local rar  = rarityName(cfg)
    local wtext= weightText(rec, cfg)
    local ctext= chanceText(cfg, rec)

    local lines = { earn, rar }
    if ctext then lines[#lines+1] = ctext end
    if wtext then lines[#lines+1] = wtext end

    local muts = mutationsText(rec)
    local sub = table.concat(lines, " · ")
    if muts and muts ~= "" then sub = sub .. "\n" .. muts end

    return {
        name = name,
        sub = sub,
        color = rarityColor(
            type(cfg) == "table" and type(cfg.Rarity) == "table"
                and tonumber(cfg.Rarity.RarityNumber) or 0
        ),
        target = isTarget,
        tall = muts ~= nil and muts ~= "",
        icon = getIcon(cfg, rec.AssetCategory, rec.AssetCategory),
        cfg = cfg,
        cat = rec.AssetCategory,
    }
end

local function tickESP()
    local wantESP = Flags.EggESP
    local wantPlot = Flags.PlotESP
    local wantBeam = Flags.ESPBeam

    if not wantESP and not wantPlot and not wantBeam then
        for _, item in pairs(ESP.pool) do
            if item.bb then item.bb.Enabled = false end
        end
        if ESP.beamObj then ESP.beamObj.Enabled = false end
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (ESP.lastTick or 0) < 0.08 then return end
    ESP.lastTick = now

    -- Field ESP
    if wantESP then
        local eggs = readFieldEggs()
        local activeUid = Flags.AutoSteal and STEAL_STATE.running
            and STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.rec
            and STEAL_STATE.target.rec.Uid

        for _, rec in ipairs(eggs) do
            pcall(function()
                if type(rec) ~= "table" or not rec.Uid then return end

                local state = rec.State
                if state ~= "Slot" and state ~= "Dropped" then return end

                local cat = rec.AssetCategory
                local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]

                local filterMode = Flags.ESPFilter or "All eggs"
                local passes

                if filterMode == "Stolen target only" then
                    passes = STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.rec
                        and STEAL_STATE.target.rec.Uid == rec.Uid
                else
                    passes = filterMode ~= "Eggs matching my filters"
                        or matchesFilters(rec, cfg)
                end

                if not passes then return end

                local pos = positionFromRecord(rec)
                if not pos then
                    local cached = ESP.pool["f:" .. rec.Uid]
                    pos = cached and cached.pos
                end

                if pos then
                    local isTarget = rec.Uid == activeUid
                    spawnBillboard("f:" .. rec.Uid, pos, spawnInfoFor(rec, cfg, isTarget))
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    -- Plot ESP
    if wantPlot then
        pcall(function()
            -- Build plot list
            local plotNow = os.clock()
            if plotNow - (ESP.plotAt or 0) >= 0.5 then
                ESP.plotAt = plotNow
                local list = {}
                local owned = nil

                if P1.eggStateModule and type(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs) == "function" then
                    local ok, res = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs, LocalPlayer.UserId)
                    if ok and type(res) == "table" then owned = res end
                end

                local _, _, plotFolder, centerPart = resolveOwnPlot()
                local pivotCF = CFrame.new()
                if centerPart and typeof(centerPart) == "Instance"
                    and centerPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pivotCF = centerPart.CFrame
                elseif plotFolder then
                    local c = plotFolder:FindFirstChild("CenterPoint", true)
                    if c and c:IsA("BasePart") then pivotCF = c.CFrame
                    else pcall(function() pivotCF = plotFolder:GetPivot() end) end
                end

                if type(owned) == "table" then
                    for k, v in pairs(owned) do
                        if type(v) == "table" and v.AssetCategory then
                            local uid = v.Uid or k
                            local cat = v.AssetCategory
                            local cfg = AssetDirectory and AssetDirectory[cat]
                            local pos
                            if type(v.Placement) == "table"
                                and v.Placement.LocalCFrame then
                                local ok, cf = pcall(function()
                                    return pivotCF * v.Placement.LocalCFrame
                                end)
                                if ok and cf then
                                    pos = cf.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                end
                            end

                            if pos then
                                -- Get ready state
                                local ready, left
                                if P1.eggStateModule
                                    and type(P1.eggStateModule.IsReadyToHatch) == "function" then
                                    local ok, r = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.IsReadyToHatch, uid)
                                    if ok and r then ready = true; left = 0 end
                                end

                                list[#list+1] = {
                                    key = "p:" .. tostring(v.Uid or k),
                                    pos = pos,
                                    cfg = cfg,
                                    rec = v,
                                    left = left,
                                    ready = ready,
                                }
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                ESP.plotList = list
            end

            for _, item in ipairs(ESP.plotList) do
                local cfg = item.cfg
                local earn = formatShort(ratePerSecond(item.rec, cfg)) .. "/s"
                local pname = cfg and (cfg.DisplayName
                    or (type(cfg.Egg) == "table" and cfg.Egg.DisplayName))
                    or (item.rec and item.rec.AssetCategory) or "plot egg"
                local stateTxt
                if item.ready then stateTxt = "ready"
                elseif item.left then
                    local total = math.max(0, math.floor(item.left))
                    local h = math.floor(total / 3600)
                    local m = math.floor((total % 3600) / 60)
                    local s = total % 60
                    stateTxt = h > 0
                        and string.format("%d:%02d:%02d", h, m, s)
                        or string.format("%d:%02d", m, s)
                else stateTxt = "hatching" end

                local ctext = chanceText(cfg, item.rec)
                local sub = earn .. "  ·  " .. stateTxt
                if ctext then sub = earn .. "  ·  " .. ctext .. "  ·  " .. stateTxt end

                spawnBillboard(item.key, item.pos, {
                    name = tostring(pname),
                    sub = sub,
                    color = item.ready and ActiveTheme.ok or ActiveTheme.dim,
                    target = item.ready == true,
                    icon = getIcon(cfg, item.rec and item.rec.AssetCategory),
                })
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Beam to current steal target
    if wantBeam and Flags.AutoSteal and STEAL_STATE.running then
        local _, root = getChar()
        if root and STEAL_STATE.target and STEAL_STATE.target.pos
            and (STEAL_STATE.target.cfg or STEAL_STATE.target.earn) then
            -- Beam
            local folder = getESPFolder()

            if not ESP.att0 or ESP.att0.Parent ~= root then
                if ESP.att0 then pcall(function() ESP.att0:Destroy() end) end
                ESP.att0 = new("Attachment", { Name = "KiraBeamA", Parent = root })
            end

            if not ESP.tip or not ESP.tip.Parent then
                ESP.tip = new("Part", {
                    Name = "KiraBeamTip",
                    Anchored = true,
                    CanCollide = false,
                    CanQuery = false,
                    CanTouch = false,
                    Transparency = 1,
                    Size = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
                    Parent = folder,
                })
            end

            pcall(function()
                ESP.tip.CFrame = CFrame.new(STEAL_STATE.target.pos)
            end)

            if not ESP.att1 or ESP.att1.Parent ~= ESP.tip then
                if ESP.att1 then pcall(function() ESP.att1:Destroy() end) end
                ESP.att1 = new("Attachment", { Name = "KiraBeamB", Parent = ESP.tip })
            end

            if not ESP.beamObj or not ESP.beamObj.Parent then
                ESP.beamObj = new("Beam", {
                    Name = "KiraBeam",
                    FaceCamera = true,
                    Width0 = 0.16,
                    Width1 = 0.05,
                    LightEmission = 0.7,
                    Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.15),
                    Parent = folder,
                })
            end

            ESP.beamObj.Attachment0 = ESP.att0
            ESP.beamObj.Attachment1 = ESP.att1
            ESP.beamObj.Color = ColorSequence.new(ActiveTheme.accent)
            ESP.beamObj.Enabled = true
        elseif ESP.beamObj then
            ESP.beamObj.Enabled = false
        end
    elseif ESP.beamObj then
        ESP.beamObj.Enabled = false
    end

    reapPool()
end

ESP.tick = tickESP

--=====================================================================
--  [10] Optimizer
--=====================================================================
local OPT_CLASSES = {
    ParticleEmitter = true,
    Trail = true,
    Beam = true,
    Fire = true,
    Smoke = true,
    Sparkles = true,
    Highlight = true,
    PointLight = true,
    SpotLight = true,
    SurfaceLight = true,
    Clouds = true,
}

local function optHide(inst)
    if not inst then return end

    local skip = false
    if not inst then skip = true
    elseif ScreenGui and inst:IsDescendantOf(ScreenGui) then skip = true
    elseif ESP.world and inst:IsDescendantOf(ESP.world) then skip = true
    else
        local n = inst.Name
        skip = n == "KiraSupport" or n == "Hub45Support"
            or n == "KiraWorldGui" or n == WORLD_NAME
            or n == SCREEN_NAME
    end

    if skip then return end

    if inst:IsA("ScreenGui") or inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ScreenGui") then
        return
    end

    if not OPT_CLASSES[inst.ClassName] then return end

    if not ESP.optInst then ESP.optInst = {} end

    if ESP.optInst[inst] == nil then
        local saved = {}
        pcall(function()
            if inst:IsA("Light") then
                saved.Enabled = inst.Enabled
                saved.Brightness = inst.Brightness
                return
            end
            if inst:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or inst:IsA("Trail")
                or inst:IsA("Beam") then
                saved.Enabled = inst.Enabled
                if inst:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                    saved.Rate = inst.Rate
                end
            else
                saved.Enabled = inst.Enabled
            end
        end)
        ESP.optInst[inst] = saved
    end

    pcall(function()
        inst.Enabled = false
        if inst:IsA("Light") then
            inst.Brightness = 0
            return
        end
        if inst:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            inst.Rate = 0
        end
    end)
end

local function setOptimizer(on)
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

    if on then
        if not ESP.optSaved then
            ESP.optSaved = {}
            pcall(function() ESP.optSaved.quality = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel end)
            pcall(function()
                ESP.optSaved.savedQuality = UserSettings().GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel
            end)
            pcall(function()
                ESP.optSaved.meshDetail = settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel
            end)
            pcall(function()
                ESP.optSaved.shadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
                ESP.optSaved.brightness = Lighting.Brightness
                ESP.optSaved.envDiff = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
                ESP.optSaved.envSpec = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
                ESP.optSaved.fogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
                ESP.optSaved.fogStart = Lighting.FogStart
                ESP.optSaved.exposure = Lighting.ExposureCompensation
            end)
            if Terrain then
                pcall(function()
                    ESP.optSaved.waterWave = Terrain.WaterWaveSize
                    ESP.optSaved.waterSpeed = Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed
                    ESP.optSaved.waterReflect = Terrain.WaterReflectance
                    ESP.optSaved.decoration = Terrain.Decoration
                end)
            end
        end

        pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end)
        pcall(function()
            UserSettings().GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel = Enum.SavedQualityLevel.QualityLevel1
        end)
        pcall(function()
            settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level04
        end)
        pcall(function()
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            Lighting.Brightness = 1
            Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
            Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
            Lighting.FogEnd = 250
            Lighting.FogStart = 0
            Lighting.ExposureCompensation = -0.4
        end)

        if Terrain then
            pcall(function()
                Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
                Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
                Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
                Terrain.Decoration = false
            end)
        end

        if not ESP.optFx then ESP.optFx = {} end

        for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("PostEffect") or child:IsA("Atmosphere")
                or child:IsA("Sky") or child:IsA("Clouds") then
                if ESP.optFx[child] == nil then
                    if child:IsA("PostEffect") or child:IsA("Clouds") then
                        ESP.optFx[child] = { Enabled = child.Enabled }
                    elseif child:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                        ESP.optFx[child] = {
                            Density = child.Density,
                            Offset = child.Offset,
                            Glare = child.Glare,
                            Haze = child.Haze,
                        }
                    end
                end

                pcall(function()
                    if child:IsA("PostEffect") or child:IsA("Clouds") then
                        child.Enabled = false
                        return
                    end
                    if child:IsA("Atmosphere") then
                        child.Density = 0
                        child.Glare = 0
                        child.Haze = 0
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        pcall(function()
            for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                optHide(d)
            end
        end)

        if ESP.optAdd then pcall(function() ESP.optAdd:Disconnect() end) end
        ESP.optAdd = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if Flags.Optimizer then optHide(d) end
        end)
        trackConn(ESP.optAdd)
        return
    end

    -- Restore
    if ESP.optSaved then
        if ESP.optAdd then
            pcall(function() ESP.optAdd:Disconnect() end)
            ESP.optAdd = nil
        end

        pcall(function()
            if ESP.optSaved.quality then
                settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = ESP.optSaved.quality
            end
            if ESP.optSaved.savedQuality then
                UserSettings().GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel = ESP.optSaved.savedQuality
            end
            if ESP.optSaved.meshDetail then
                settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = ESP.optSaved.meshDetail
            end

            Lighting.GlobalShadows = ESP.optSaved.shadows
            if ESP.optSaved.brightness then Lighting.Brightness = ESP.optSaved.brightness end
            if ESP.optSaved.envDiff then
                Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = ESP.optSaved.envDiff
            end
            if ESP.optSaved.envSpec then
                Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = ESP.optSaved.envSpec
            end
            if ESP.optSaved.fogEnd then Lighting.FogEnd = ESP.optSaved.fogEnd end
            if ESP.optSaved.fogStart then Lighting.FogStart = ESP.optSaved.fogStart end
            if ESP.optSaved.exposure then
                Lighting.ExposureCompensation = ESP.optSaved.exposure
            end

            if Terrain then
                if ESP.optSaved.waterWave ~= nil then
                    Terrain.WaterWaveSize = ESP.optSaved.waterWave
                end
                if ESP.optSaved.waterSpeed ~= nil then
                    Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = ESP.optSaved.waterSpeed
                end
                if ESP.optSaved.waterReflect ~= nil then
                    Terrain.WaterReflectance = ESP.optSaved.waterReflect
                end
                if ESP.optSaved.decoration ~= nil then
                    Terrain.Decoration = ESP.optSaved.decoration
                end
            end

            if ESP.optFx then
                for k, v in pairs(ESP.optFx) do
                    if k.Parent and type(v) == "table" then
                        pcall(function()
                            if v.Enabled ~= nil then k.Enabled = v.Enabled end
                            if v.Density then k.Density = v.Density end
                            if v.Glare then k.Glare = v.Glare end
                            if v.Haze then k.Haze = v.Haze end
                        end)
                    end
                end
                ESP.optFx = nil
            end

            if ESP.optInst then
                for k, v in pairs(ESP.optInst) do
                    if k.Parent and type(v) == "table" then
                        pcall(function()
                            if v.Enabled ~= nil then k.Enabled = v.Enabled end
                            if v.Brightness ~= nil then k.Brightness = v.Brightness end
                            if v.Rate ~= nil then k.Rate = v.Rate end
                        end)
                    end
                end
                ESP.optInst = nil
            end
        end)
        ESP.optSaved = nil
    end
end

ESP.opt = setOptimizer

--=====================================================================
--  [11] FPS cap
--=====================================================================
function ESP.fps()
    local cap = tonumber(Flags.FPSCap) or 0
    if not setfpscap then return end
    pcall(setfpscap, cap > 0 and cap or 0)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [12] Money + Income from HUD
--=====================================================================
local function readMoney()
    local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local hud = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("HUD")
    local gh = hud and hud:FindFirstChild("GameHUD")
    local bl = gh and gh:FindFirstChild("BottomLeft")
    local money = bl and bl:FindFirstChild("Money")
    local val = money and money:FindFirstChild("Value")

    if val and (val:IsA("TextLabel") or val:IsA("TextButton"))
        and val.Text ~= "" then
        return val.Text
    end

    local ls = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
    local m = ls and (ls:FindFirstChild("Money")
        or ls:FindFirstChild("Cash") or ls:FindFirstChild("Coins"))
    if m and m:IsA("ValueBase") then
        return "$" .. formatShort(m.Value)
    end

    return "—"
end

--=====================================================================
--  [13] Stats Panel
--=====================================================================
local STAT_KEYS = {
    "Money", "Income", "Pen", "Best pet", "Best egg",
    "Speed", "Session",
}

local function buildStatsPanel()
    if ESP.statsFrame and ESP.statsFrame.Parent then
        local rows = ESP.statsRows
        if type(rows) == "table"
            and rows.Money and rows.Money.Parent
            and rows.Session and rows.Session.Parent
            and ESP.statsFrame.Size.X.Offset >= 240 then
            return ESP.statsFrame
        end
        pcall(function() ESP.statsFrame:Destroy() end)
        ESP.statsFrame = nil
        ESP.statsRows = nil
    end

    local old = ScreenGui:FindFirstChild("StatsPanel")
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

    local frame = new("Frame", {
        Name = "StatsPanel",
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 72),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(248, 222),
        ZIndex = 40,
        Active = true,
        Visible = false,
        ClipsDescendants = false,
    }, ScreenGui)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10) }, frame)
    addStroke(frame, "line", 1)
    frame:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")

    local head = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.rail,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = "  Stats",
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 12,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        ZIndex = 9,
        Active = true,
    }, frame)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10) }, head)
    head:SetAttribute("th_bg", "rail")
    head:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

    local hBottom = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.rail,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10),
        ZIndex = 9,
    }, head)
    hBottom:SetAttribute("th_bg", "rail")

    local rows = {}
    local y = 28
    for _, key in ipairs(STAT_KEYS) do
        local tall = key == "Best pet" or key == "Best egg"
        local h = tall and 34 or 18

        local lab = new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, y),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72, h),
            Font = FONTS.body,
            Text = key,
            TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
            ZIndex = 9,
        }, frame)
        lab:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")

        local val = new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(82, y),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(156, h),
            Font = FONTS.mono,
            Text = "—",
            TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
            TextSize = 11,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
            TextWrapped = tall,
            TextTruncate = tall and Enum.TextTruncate.None or Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            ZIndex = 9,
        }, frame)
        val:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

        rows[key] = val
        y = y + (tall and 38 or 21)
    end

    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(248, y + 8)

    -- Drag support
    local dragging, inputStart, posStart
    head.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or tp == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            inputStart = input.Position
            posStart = frame.Position
        end
    end)
    trackConn(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or tp == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if not ESP.statsFrame or not ESP.statsFrame.Parent then
                dragging = false
                return
            end
            local d = input.Position - inputStart
            ESP.statsFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                posStart.X.Scale, posStart.X.Offset + d.X,
                posStart.Y.Scale, posStart.Y.Offset + d.Y
            )
            if ESP.sellFrame and ESP.sellFrame.Visible and not ESP.sellMoved then
                if ESP.relayoutSell then pcall(ESP.relayoutSell, ESP.sellFrame) end
            end
        end
    end))
    trackConn(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or tp == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end))

    ESP.statsFrame = frame
    ESP.statsRows = rows
    return frame
end

local function setStatsPanel(on)
    if not on then
        if ESP.statsFrame then ESP.statsFrame.Visible = false end
        return
    end
    local f = buildStatsPanel()
    if f then f.Visible = true end
end

ESP.stats = setStatsPanel

--=====================================================================
--  [14] Stats tick
--=====================================================================
local function tickStats()
    if not Flags.StatsPanel then
        if ESP.statsFrame and ESP.statsFrame.Visible then
            ESP.statsFrame.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (ESP.statsAt or 0) < 0.35 then return end
    ESP.statsAt = now

    local frame = buildStatsPanel()
    if not frame then return end
    frame.Visible = true

    if ESP.sellFrame and ESP.sellFrame.Visible and not ESP.sellMoved then
        if ESP.relayoutSell then pcall(ESP.relayoutSell, ESP.sellFrame) end
    end

    local rows = ESP.statsRows
    if type(rows) ~= "table" then return end

    -- Session
    local sessionSec = math.max(0, math.floor(now - (ESP.sessionAt or now)))
    local sh = math.floor(sessionSec / 3600)
    local sm = math.floor((sessionSec % 3600) / 60)
    local ss = sessionSec % 60
    local sessionTxt = sh > 0
        and string.format("%d:%02d:%02d", sh, sm, ss)
        or string.format("%d:%02d", sm, ss)
    local banked = tonumber(STEAL_STATE.banked) or 0
    if rows.Session and rows.Session.Parent then
        rows.Session.Text = sessionTxt .. " · " .. tostring(banked) .. " stolen"
    end

    -- Money
    pcall(function()
        local money = readMoney()
        if rows.Money and rows.Money.Parent then
            rows.Money.Text = tostring(money)
        end
    end)

    -- Income + Speed
    pcall(function()
        local ls = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
        local inc = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Money/s")
        local spd = ls and ls:FindFirstChild("Speed")
        local incTxt = inc and (formatShort(inc.Value) .. "/s") or "—"
        local spdTxt = spd and formatShort(spd.Value) or "—"

        if rows.Income and rows.Income.Parent then
            rows.Income.Text = tostring(incTxt)
        end
        if rows.Speed and rows.Speed.Parent then
            rows.Speed.Text = tostring(spdTxt)
        end
    end)

    -- Owned eggs (async)
    if not ESP.ownerBusy and now - (ESP.ownerAt or 0) > 1.5 then
        ESP.ownerAt = now
        ESP.ownerBusy = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local total, placed = 0, 0
            pcall(function()
                if P1.eggStateModule
                    and type(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs) == "function" then
                    local ok, owned = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs, LocalPlayer.UserId)
                    if ok and type(owned) == "table" then
                        for _, v in pairs(owned) do
                            if type(v) == "table" then
                                total += 1
                                if v.Placement then placed += 1 end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            ESP.ownedN = total
            ESP.placedN = placed
            ESP.ownerBusy = false
        end)
    end

    -- Pen + Best pet
    local penList, bestPetName, bestPetRate = 0, nil, 0
    pcall(function()
        local snapshot = ESP.penSnap
        if snapshot and type(snapshot.Records) == "table" then
            for _, v in pairs(snapshot.Records) do
                if type(v) == "table" then
                    penList += 1
                    local cat = v.AssetCategory
                    if not cat and type(v.ItemData) == "table" then
                        cat = v.ItemData.Category or v.ItemData.AssetCategory
                            or v.ItemData.Name or v.ItemData.DisplayName
                    end
                    local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
                    local rate = ratePerSecond(v, cfg)
                    if rate > bestPetRate then
                        bestPetRate = rate
                        bestPetName = cfg and (cfg.DisplayName
                            or (type(cfg.Egg) == "table" and cfg.Egg.DisplayName))
                            or tostring(cat or "?")
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    if penList > 0 then
        local placed = tonumber(ESP.placedN) or 0
        if rows.Pen and rows.Pen.Parent then
            rows.Pen.Text = tostring(penList) .. " pets · " .. tostring(placed) .. " eggs"
        end
    else
        local placed = tonumber(ESP.placedN) or 0
        if rows.Pen and rows.Pen.Parent then
            rows.Pen.Text = tostring(placed) .. " placed"
        end
    end

    if bestPetName then
        if rows["Best pet"] and rows["Best pet"].Parent then
            rows["Best pet"].Text = tostring(bestPetName)
                .. "\n" .. formatShort(bestPetRate) .. "/s"
        end
    else
        if rows["Best pet"] and rows["Best pet"].Parent then
            rows["Best pet"].Text = "—"
        end
    end

    -- Best egg
    local bestEggName, bestEggRate = nil, 0
    pcall(function()
        local eggs = readFieldEggs()
        if type(eggs) ~= "table" then eggs = {} end
        for _, rec in ipairs(eggs) do
            if type(rec) == "table"
                and (rec.State == "Slot" or rec.State == "Dropped")
                and not isPlotEgg(rec) then
                local cat = rec.AssetCategory
                local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
                local rate = ratePerSecond(rec, cfg)
                if rate > bestEggRate then
                    bestEggRate = rate
                    bestEggName = cfg and (cfg.DisplayName
                        or (type(cfg.Egg) == "table" and cfg.Egg.DisplayName))
                        or tostring(cat or "?")
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    if bestEggName then
        if rows["Best egg"] and rows["Best egg"].Parent then
            rows["Best egg"].Text = tostring(bestEggName)
                .. "\n" .. formatShort(bestEggRate) .. "/s"
        end
    else
        if rows["Best egg"] and rows["Best egg"].Parent then
            rows["Best egg"].Text = "—"
        end
    end
end

ESP.statsTick = tickStats

--=====================================================================
--  [15] Sell Preview Panel
--=====================================================================
local SELL_CACHE = { pets = {}, eggs = {}, floor = 0 }

local function refreshSellList()
    -- Uses plot module (Part 8) — will get values via P1.plot
    if P1.plot and P1.plot.sellSnapshot then
        local ok, pets, eggs, floor = pcall(P1.plot.sellSnapshot)
        if ok and type(pets) == "table" and type(eggs) == "table" then
            SELL_CACHE.pets = pets
            SELL_CACHE.eggs = eggs
            SELL_CACHE.floor = floor or 0
            return
        end
    end
    -- Fallback: read directly
    SELL_CACHE.pets = SELL_CACHE.pets or {}
    SELL_CACHE.eggs = SELL_CACHE.eggs or {}
    SELL_CACHE.floor = SELL_CACHE.floor or 0
end

local function groupList(list, kind)
    local buckets = {}
    for _, item in ipairs(list) do
        local cat = tostring(item.cat or "?")
        local key = kind .. ":" .. cat
        local b = buckets[key]
        if not b then
            b = {
                kind = kind,
                cat = cat,
                cfg = item.cfg,
                earn = item.earn,
                rec = item.rec,
                price = 0,
                n = 0,
            }
            buckets[key] = b
        end
        b.n += 1
        b.price += tonumber(item.price) or 0
        if (item.earn or 0) < (b.earn or 0) then
            b.earn = item.earn
        end
    end

    local out = {}
    for _, v in pairs(buckets) do out[#out+1] = v end
    table.sort(out, function(a, b)
        if a.earn ~= b.earn then return a.earn < b.earn end
        return tostring(a.cat) < tostring(b.cat)
    end)
    return out
end

local function closeSellPanel()
    if ESP.sellDrag then pcall(function() ESP.sellDrag:Disconnect() end) end
    if ESP.sellEnd then pcall(function() ESP.sellEnd:Disconnect() end) end
    if ESP.sellFrame then pcall(function() ESP.sellFrame:Destroy() end) end

    ESP.sellDrag = nil
    ESP.sellEnd = nil
    ESP.sellFrame = nil
    ESP.sellTips = nil
    ESP.sellMoved = nil
    ESP.sellPos = nil

    local old = ScreenGui:FindFirstChild("SellPreviewPanel")
    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end

    local stats = ESP.statsFrame
    if stats then
        local inStats = stats:FindFirstChild("SellPreviewPanel")
            or stats:FindFirstChild("SellPreview")
        if inStats then pcall(function() inStats:Destroy() end) end
    end
end

local function relayoutSell(frame)
    if not frame or not frame.Parent then return end
    if frame.Parent ~= ScreenGui then frame.Parent = ScreenGui end

    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(248, 348)
    frame.ZIndex = 90
    frame.Visible = true

    if ESP.sellMoved and ESP.sellPos then
        frame.Position = ESP.sellPos
        return
    end

    local stats = ESP.statsFrame
    if stats and stats.Parent and stats.Visible then
        local sp = ScreenGui.AbsolutePosition
        local sabs = stats.AbsolutePosition
        local ssz = stats.AbsoluteSize
        frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
            sabs.X - sp.X,
            sabs.Y - sp.Y + ssz.Y + 8
        )
        return
    end

    frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 258)
end

ESP.relayoutSell = relayoutSell

local function buildSellPanel()
    if ESP.sellFrame and ESP.sellFrame.Parent
        and ESP.sellTips and ESP.sellTips.pets and ESP.sellTips.eggs then
        if (ESP.sellFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y or 0) >= 320 then
            relayoutSell(ESP.sellFrame)
            return ESP.sellFrame
        end
        closeSellPanel()
    end

    closeSellPanel()

    local frame = new("Frame", {
        Name = "SellPreviewPanel",
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(248, 348),
        ZIndex = 90,
        Active = true,
        Visible = true,
    }, ScreenGui)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10) }, frame)
    addStroke(frame, "line", 1)
    frame:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")

    relayoutSell(frame)

    local head = new("TextButton", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.rail,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = "  Sell preview",
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 12,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 60,
        Active = true,
    }, frame)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10) }, head)
    head:SetAttribute("th_bg", "rail")
    head:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")

    local headBottom = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.rail,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10),
        ZIndex = 60,
    }, head)
    headBottom:SetAttribute("th_bg", "rail")

    local close = new("TextButton", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0),
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20),
        Font = FONTS.mid,
        Text = "×",
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.text,
        TextSize = 14,
        AutoButtonColor = false,
        ZIndex = 61,
        Active = true,
    }, head)
    new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, close)
    addStroke(close, "line", 1)
    close:SetAttribute("th_bg", "fill")
    close:SetAttribute("th_text", "text")
    applyHover(close, "fill", "lift")

    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if ESP.sellFrame then ESP.sellFrame.Visible = false end
    end)

    local sub = new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 28),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16),
        Font = FONTS.body,
        Text = "",
        TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
        TextSize = 11,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        ZIndex = 41,
    }, frame)
    sub:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")

    local scroll = new("ScrollingFrame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 46),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -102),
        AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        ScrollBarThickness = 4,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = ActiveTheme.line,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        ZIndex = 41,
    }, frame)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 8),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
    }, scroll)

    local function makeSection(order, title)
        local section = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            LayoutOrder = order,
            ZIndex = 42,
        }, scroll)
        new("UIListLayout", {
            FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        }, section)

        local label = new("TextLabel", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14),
            Font = FONTS.mono,
            Text = title,
            TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
            TextSize = 10,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            LayoutOrder = 1,
            ZIndex = 42,
        }, section)
        label:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")

        local grid = new("Frame", {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            LayoutOrder = 2,
            ZIndex = 42,
        }, section)
        new("UIGridLayout", {
            CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
            CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40),
            FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
            HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left,
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        }, grid)

        local gridLayout = grid:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGridLayout")
        if gridLayout then
            local function fit()
                if not grid or not gridLayout then return end
                local h = math.max(0, math.ceil(gridLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y))
                grid.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                grid.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
            end
            gridLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(fit)
            return { lab = label, grid = grid, title = title, fit = fit }
        end
        return { lab = label, grid = grid, title = title }
    end

    local petsSection = makeSection(1, "Pets")
    local eggsSection = makeSection(2, "Eggs")

    -- Bottom tips
    local tipBar = new("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.card,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 56),
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        ZIndex = 50,
    }, frame)
    tipBar:SetAttribute("th_bg", "card")

    local tipLine = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.line,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
        ZIndex = 51,
    }, tipBar)
    tipLine:SetAttribute("th_bg", "line")

    local tipInner = new("Frame", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -1),
        ZIndex = 51,
    }, tipBar)
    addPadding(tipInner, 10, 6, 10, 6)
    new("UIListLayout", {
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 2),
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,

        BackgroundTransparency = 1,

        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        LayoutOrder = 3,
