        name = tipName,
        a = tipA,
        b = tipB,
        sub = sub,
        pets = petsSection,
        eggs = eggsSection,
    }

    -- Drag
    local dragging, inputStart, posStart
    head.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and tp ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end
        local ip = input.Position
        local ap = close.AbsolutePosition
        local as = close.AbsoluteSize
        if ip.X >= ap.X and ip.X <= ap.X + as.X
            and ip.Y >= ap.Y and ip.Y <= ap.Y + as.Y then
            return
        end

        dragging = true
        inputStart = input.Position
        posStart = frame.Position
        ESP.sellMoved = true
        ESP.sellPos = posStart
    end)

    ESP.sellDrag = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and tp ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end
        if not ESP.sellFrame or not ESP.sellFrame.Parent or not posStart then
            dragging = false
            return
        end
        local d = input.Position - inputStart
        local newPos = UDim2.new(
            posStart.X.Scale, posStart.X.Offset + d.X,
            posStart.Y.Scale, posStart.Y.Offset + d.Y
        )
        ESP.sellFrame.Position = newPos
        ESP.sellPos = newPos
    end)
    trackConn(ESP.sellDrag)

    ESP.sellEnd = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        local tp = input.UserInputType
        if tp == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or tp == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
            if ESP.sellFrame then ESP.sellPos = ESP.sellFrame.Position end
        end
    end)
    trackConn(ESP.sellEnd)

    ESP.sellFrame = frame
    return frame
end

local function getItemInfo(item)
    local cfg = item.cfg
    local name = (type(cfg) == "table" and (cfg.DisplayName
        or (type(cfg.Egg) == "table" and cfg.Egg.DisplayName))) or item.cat or "?"

    local rar = rarityName(cfg)
    local w = weightFrom(item.rec, cfg)
    local wtxt = w and (w >= 100 and string.format("%.0fkg", w)
        or string.format("%.1fkg", w))

    local muts = ""
    if type(item.rec) == "table" and type(item.rec.Mutations) == "table"
        and #item.rec.Mutations > 0 then
        muts = table.concat(item.rec.Mutations, " · ")
    end

    local lineA = formatShort(item.earn) .. "/s · " .. rar
    if wtxt then lineA = lineA .. " · " .. wtxt end
    lineA = lineA .. " · " .. tostring(item.kind)

    local lineB = "×" .. tostring(item.n or 1)
    if (item.price or 0) > 0 then
        lineB = lineB .. " · $" .. formatShort(item.price)
    end
    if muts ~= "" then lineB = lineB .. " · " .. muts end

    return name, lineA, lineB
end

local function fillGrid(section, list)
    for _, child in ipairs(section.grid:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("GuiObject") then child:Destroy() end
    end

    if #list == 0 then
        section.lab.Text = section.title .. " · none"
        return
    end

    local total = 0
    for _, it in ipairs(list) do total += it.n or 1 end
    section.lab.Text = section.title .. " · " .. tostring(total)

    for i = 1, #list do
        local item = list[i]
        local name, lineA, lineB = getItemInfo(item)

        local btn = new("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.fill,
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            LayoutOrder = i,
            ZIndex = 43,
        }, section.grid)
        new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, btn)
        btn:SetAttribute("th_bg", "fill")
        addStroke(btn, item.kind ~= "egg" and "line" or "accent", 1)

        local icon = getIcon(item.cfg, item.cat)
        if icon then
            local img = new("ImageLabel", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32),
                Image = icon,
                ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                ZIndex = 44,
            }, btn)
        else
            local initial = string.sub(name, 1, 1)
            if initial == "" then initial = "?" end
            local lbl = new("TextLabel", {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                Font = FONTS.mid,
                Text = string.upper(initial),
                TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.dim,
                TextSize = 14,
                ZIndex = 44,
            }, btn)
            lbl:SetAttribute("th_text", "dim")
        end

        if (item.n or 1) > 1 then
            local badge = new("TextLabel", {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
                BackgroundColor3 = ActiveTheme.accent,
                Position = UDim2.new(1, 2, 1, 2),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 12),
                Font = FONTS.mono,
                Text = (item.n > 9) and "9+" or tostring(item.n),
                TextColor3 = ActiveTheme.ink,
                TextSize = 8,
                ZIndex = 45,
            }, btn)
            new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, badge)
            badge:SetAttribute("th_bg", "accent")
            badge:SetAttribute("th_text", "ink")
        end

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if ESP.sellTips then
                ESP.sellTips.name.Text = name
                ESP.sellTips.a.Text = lineA
                ESP.sellTips.b.Text = lineB
            end
        end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if ESP.sellTips then
                ESP.sellTips.name.Text = "Hover icon to display stats"
                ESP.sellTips.a.Text = ""
                ESP.sellTips.b.Text = ""
            end
        end)
    end

    if section.fit then
        pcall(section.fit)
        task.defer(section.fit)
    end
end

local function openSellPreview(pets, eggs, floor, headerText)
    floor = tonumber(floor) or 0
    headerText = headerText or ""

    local petGroups = groupList(pets or {}, "pet")
    local eggGroups = groupList(eggs or {}, "egg")

    local subText
    if headerText ~= "" then
        subText = headerText
    elseif floor <= 0 then
        subText = "set a $/s floor or nothing sells"
    elseif (#pets + #eggs) ~= 0 then
        subText = #pets .. " pets · " .. #eggs .. " eggs under "
            .. formatShort(floor) .. "/s"
    else
        subText = "nothing under " .. formatShort(floor) .. "/s — plot/pen pets stay"
    end

    -- Mark auto-hook for stats panel
    Flags.StatsPanel = true
    if Widgets.StatsPanel and Widgets.StatsPanel.set then
        pcall(Widgets.StatsPanel.set, true)
    end
    pcall(setStatsPanel, true)

    local frame = buildSellPanel()
    if not frame then
        warn("[Kira/esp] preview build failed")
        return false
    end

    relayoutSell(frame)
    frame.Visible = true

    if ESP.sellTips then
        ESP.sellTips.sub.Text = tostring(subText)
        ESP.sellTips.name.Text = "Hover icon to display stats"
        ESP.sellTips.a.Text = ""
        ESP.sellTips.b.Text = ""
        pcall(fillGrid, ESP.sellTips.pets, petGroups)
        pcall(fillGrid, ESP.sellTips.eggs, eggGroups)
    end

    return true
end

ESP.sellPreview = openSellPreview
ESP.closeSellPreview = function()
    if ESP.sellFrame then ESP.sellFrame.Visible = false end
end
ESP.buildSellPanel = buildSellPanel
ESP.fillGrid = fillGrid

--=====================================================================
--  [16] Wire ESP-related widgets
--=====================================================================
local function wire(flag, fn)
    local w = Widgets[flag]
    if not w then
        warn("[Kira/wire/esp] missing:", flag)
        return
    end
    w.on = fn
end

wire("EggESP", function() warn("[Kira/esp] field", Flags.EggESP) end)
wire("ESPFilter", function(v) warn("[Kira/esp] filter", tostring(v)) end)
wire("ESPBeam", function() warn("[Kira/esp] beam", Flags.ESPBeam) end)
wire("PlotESP", function()
    warn("[Kira/esp] plot", Flags.PlotESP)
    ESP.plotAt = 0
end)
wire("StatsPanel", function(on)
    if ESP.stats then ESP.stats(on) end
    warn("[Kira/esp] stats panel", on)
end)
wire("Optimizer", function(on)
    setOptimizer(on)
    warn("[Kira/esp] optimizer", on)
end)
wire("FPSCap", function(v)
    ESP.fps()
    warn("[Kira/esp] fps cap", v)
end)

--=====================================================================
--  [17] Render / run loop
--=====================================================================
local stepConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
    if not engine.loaded then return end
    if engine.on then return end -- engine already ticking
    pcall(tickESP)
end)
trackConn(stepConn)

local renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not engine.loaded then return end
    pcall(tickStats)
end)
trackConn(renderConn)

-- Initial scan
task.defer(function()
    pcall(scanIcons)
end)

--=====================================================================
--  [18] Cleanup
--=====================================================================
ESP.clear = function()
    if ESP.optAdd then
        pcall(function() ESP.optAdd:Disconnect() end)
        ESP.optAdd = nil
    end

    if stepConn then pcall(function() stepConn:Disconnect() end) end
    if renderConn then pcall(function() renderConn:Disconnect() end) end

    if ESP.statsDrag then pcall(function() ESP.statsDrag:Disconnect() end) end
    if ESP.statsEnd then pcall(function() ESP.statsEnd:Disconnect() end) end

    if ESP.statsFrame then
        pcall(function() ESP.statsFrame:Destroy() end)
        ESP.statsFrame = nil
        ESP.statsRows = nil
    end

    closeSellPanel()

    for _, item in pairs(ESP.pool) do
        if item.bb then item.bb:Destroy() end
        if item.dummy then pcall(function() item.dummy:Destroy() end) end
    end
    ESP.pool = {}

    for _, key in ipairs({ "att0", "att1", "beamObj", "tip", "folder", "world" }) do
        local obj = ESP[key]
        if obj then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
            ESP[key] = nil
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [2] Remote helpers
--=====================================================================
local function requireShared(path)
    local cur = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared")
    for i = 1, #path do
        if not cur then return end
        cur = cur:FindFirstChild(path[i])
    end
    if cur and cur:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        local ok, mod = pcall(require, cur)
        if ok then return mod end
    end
end

local function getRemote(bucket, key)
    local remotes = requireShared({ "Remotes" })
    local node = remotes and remotes[bucket]
    local target = node and node[key]
    if target == nil then return nil end

    if typeof(target) == "Instance" then return target end
    if type(target) == "table" then
        for _, k in ipairs({ "Remote", "remote", "Instance",
            "_remote", "_instance", "Event" }) do
            if typeof(target[k]) == "Instance" then return target[k] end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function invokeRemote(bucket, key, ...)
    local remote = getRemote(bucket, key)
    if not remote then return false, "no remote" end
    return remote:InvokeServer(...)
end

local function fireRemote(bucket, key, ...)
    local n = select("#", ...)
    local a, b, c = ...
    local remotes = requireShared({ "Remotes" })
    local node = remotes and remotes[bucket]
    local target = node and node[key]
    if target == nil then return false end

    local function doFire(obj)
        if n <= 0 then obj:FireServer()
        elseif n == 1 then obj:FireServer(a)
        elseif n == 2 then obj:FireServer(a, b)
        else obj:FireServer(a, b, c) end
    end

    if pcall(doFire, target) then return true end

    local inst = getRemote(bucket, key)
    return inst ~= nil and pcall(doFire, inst)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [3] Save + Plot + Bases modules
--=====================================================================
local function readSave()
    local Save = ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save

    local mod
    if PLOT.saveMod == false then return nil end
    if PLOT.saveMod ~= nil then mod = PLOT.saveMod
    else
        local ok, res = pcall(require, Save)
        PLOT.saveMod = ok and type(res) == "table" and (res or false) or false
        mod = PLOT.saveMod ~= false and PLOT.saveMod or nil
    end

    if type(mod) == "table" and type(mod.Get) == "function" then
        local ok, res = pcall(mod.Get, LocalPlayer, false)
        if ok and type(res) == "table" then return res end
        local ok2, res2 = pcall(mod.Get)
        if ok2 and type(res2) == "table" then return res2 end
    end
end

local function readPlotState()
    local now = os.clock()
    if PLOT.info and now - (PLOT.infoAt or 0) < 0.45 then
        return PLOT.info
    end

    local mod
    if PLOT.plotMod == false then return PLOT.info end
    if PLOT.plotMod ~= nil then mod = PLOT.plotMod
    else
        local ok, res = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Client.PlotState)
        PLOT.plotMod = ok and type(res) == "table" and (res or false) or false
        mod = PLOT.plotMod ~= false and PLOT.plotMod or nil
    end

    local resolved
    if mod and type(mod.ResolvePlot) == "function" then
        pcall(function() resolved = mod.ResolvePlot() end)
    end

    PLOT.info = type(resolved) == "table" and resolved or nil
    PLOT.infoAt = now
    return PLOT.info
end

local function readTrails()
    local mod
    if PLOT.trailsMod == false then return nil end
    if PLOT.trailsMod ~= nil then mod = PLOT.trailsMod
    else
        local ok, res = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Data.Trails)
        PLOT.trailsMod = ok and type(res) == "table" and (res or false) or false
        mod = PLOT.trailsMod ~= false and PLOT.trailsMod or nil
    end
    return mod
end

local function readTreadmills()
    local mod
    if PLOT.tmsMod == false then return nil end
    if PLOT.tmsMod ~= nil then mod = PLOT.tmsMod
    else
        local ok, res = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Data.Treadmills)
        PLOT.tmsMod = ok and type(res) == "table" and (res or false) or false
        mod = PLOT.tmsMod ~= false and PLOT.tmsMod or nil
    end
    return mod
end

local function readBases()
    local mod
    if PLOT.basesMod == false then return nil end
    if PLOT.basesMod ~= nil then mod = PLOT.basesMod
    else
        local ok, res = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage.Data.Bases)
        PLOT.basesMod = ok and type(res) == "table" and (res or false) or false
        mod = PLOT.basesMod ~= false and PLOT.basesMod or nil
    end
    return mod
end

--=====================================================================
--  [4] Pet area / treadmill position helpers
--=====================================================================
local function findBeltPart()
    local now = os.clock()
    if PLOT.beltPart and PLOT.beltPart.Parent
        and now - (PLOT.beltAt or 0) < 0.85 then
        return PLOT.beltPart
    end

    local belt
    local state = readPlotState()
    local folder = state and state.PlotFolder

    if folder then
        belt = folder:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom", true)
        if not belt or not belt:IsA("BasePart") then
            belt = nil
            local bestSize = 0
            for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local lower = string.lower(d.Name)
                    if lower:find("treadmill", 1, true)
                        or lower == "bottom"
                        or lower:find("belt", 1, true) then
                        local vol = d.Size.X * d.Size.Y * d.Size.Z
                        if vol > bestSize then
                            belt = d
                            bestSize = vol
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if not belt then
        local renders = workspace:FindFirstChild("__ClientTreadmillRenders")
        if renders then
            local b = renders:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom", true)
                or renders:FindFirstChild("Bottom", true)
            if b and b:IsA("BasePart") then belt = b end
        end
    end

    PLOT.beltPart = belt
    PLOT.beltAt = now
    return belt
end

local function getPetAreaPos()
    local state = readPlotState()
    local area = state and state.PetArea
    local belt = findBeltPart()

    if area and area:IsA("BasePart") then
        local pos = area.Position
        if belt then
            local away = Vector3.new(pos.X - belt.Position.X, 0, pos.Z - belt.Position.Z)
            if away.Magnitude > 1 then
                pos += away.Unit * math.min(12, away.Magnitude * 0.15)
            end
        end
        return Vector3.new(pos.X, area.Position.Y + 4.5, pos.Z), area
    end

    return select(1, findSpawn()), nil
end

local function insideBox(part, pos, pad)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") or typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end
    pad = pad or 0
    local local_ = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(pos)
    local hx = part.Size.X * 0.5 - pad
    local hz = part.Size.Z * 0.5 - pad
    return math.abs(local_.X) <= math.max(hx, 0.5)
       and math.abs(local_.Z) <= math.max(hz, 0.5)
end

local function isNearBelt(root)
    local belt = findBeltPart()
    if not belt or not root then return false end

    if insideBox(belt, root.Position, -2) then return true end
    return Vector3.new(
        root.Position.X - belt.Position.X, 0,
        root.Position.Z - belt.Position.Z
    ).Magnitude < 6
end

--=====================================================================
--  [5] Walk/drive helpers
--=====================================================================
local function flyTo(pos, force)
    local _, root = getChar()
    if not root or typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end

    engine.target = (not force) and pos or pos
    if not force and Vector3.new(
        pos.X - root.Position.X, 0, pos.Z - root.Position.Z
    ).Magnitude > 14 then
        engine.target = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y + 18, pos.Z)
    end
    engine.driving = true

    return Vector3.new(
        pos.X - root.Position.X, 0,
        pos.Z - root.Position.Z
    ).Magnitude < 10
        and math.abs(pos.Y - root.Position.Y) < 10
end

local function hopToward(target)
    local humanoid, root = getChar()
    if not humanoid or not root then return end

    engine.driving = false
    engine.target = nil

    local desired = target or select(1, getPetAreaPos())
    local belt = findBeltPart()

    local dir = Vector3.zero
    if desired then
        dir = Vector3.new(
            desired.X - root.Position.X, 0,
            desired.Z - root.Position.Z
        )
    end
    if dir.Magnitude < 0.5 and belt then
        dir = Vector3.new(
            root.Position.X - belt.Position.X, 0,
            root.Position.Z - belt.Position.Z
        )
    end

    local unit = dir.Magnitude > 0.5 and dir.Unit or Vector3.zero

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        humanoid.Sit = false
        humanoid.AutoRotate = true

        if type(humanoid.JumpHeight) == "number" and humanoid.JumpHeight < 0.5 then
            humanoid.JumpHeight = 7.2
        end
        if type(humanoid.JumpPower) == "number" then
            humanoid.JumpPower = math.max(humanoid.JumpPower, 50)
        end

        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        humanoid.Jump = true

        if unit.Magnitude > 0.5 then
            humanoid:Move(unit, false)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        root.Anchored = false
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
            unit.X * 46, 78, unit.Z * 46)
    end)
end

local function leaveBelt(skipDrive)
    PLOT.training = false

    local _, root = getChar()
    local busy
    if not STEAL_STATE or not STEAL_STATE.running then
        busy = false
    else
        local st = STEAL_STATE.state
        busy = st == "GoEgg" or st == "Grab" or st == "Return"
            or st == "Bank" or st == "Chase" or st == "Safe"
            or st == "FeedGo" or st == "Feed"
            or st == "ChestGo" or st == "ChestOpen"
    end

    if root and isNearBelt(root) then
        PLOT.job = "leave"
        hopToward(skipDrive and nil or busy)
        return
    end

    if PLOT.job == "belt" or PLOT.job == "leave" then
        PLOT.job = "idle"
        if not busy and (not STEAL_STATE or not STEAL_STATE.running) then
            engine.driving = false
            engine.target = nil
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [6] Place eggs
--=====================================================================
local function ownedEggsList()
    local owned
    if P1.eggStateModule and type(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs) == "function" then
        local ok, res = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs, LocalPlayer.UserId)
        if ok and type(res) == "table" then owned = res end
    end
    if type(owned) ~= "table" then return {}, 0 end

    local floor = numberFromString(Flags.PlaceMinGen)
    local list = {}
    local placedN = 0
    local placeAll = Flags.NeverPlaceRarity == "Place all"

    for k, v in pairs(owned) do
        if type(v) == "table" then
            if v.Placement ~= nil then
                placedN += 1
            else
                local cat = v.AssetCategory
                local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
                local rate = ratePerSecond(v, cfg)

                if floor <= 0 or floor <= rate then
                    local keep = false
                    if not placeAll and type(cfg) == "table"
                        and type(cfg.Rarity) == "table" then
                        local ri = P1.rarityIndex(P1.rarityName(cfg))
                        local lim = P1.rarityIndex(Flags.NeverPlaceRarity)
                        keep = ri > 0 and lim > 0 and lim <= ri
                    end

                    if not keep then
                        list[#list+1] = {
                            uid = v.Uid or k,
                            earn = rate,
                        }
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.earn > b.earn end)
    return list, placedN
end

local function maxPlacedSlots()
    local save = readSave()
    local bases = readBases()
    local level = tonumber(save and save.BaseUpgradeLevel) or 0
    local entry = bases and bases.BASES
        and (bases.BASES[level] or bases.BASES[level + 1])
    return tonumber(entry and entry.MaxAssets) or 99
end

local function randomPlacementCFrame()
    local state = readPlotState()
    local area = state and state.PetArea
    local center = state and state.CenterPoint
    local belt = findBeltPart()

    if not area or not area:IsA("BasePart") or not center then
        local _, _, _, centerPart = resolveOwnPlot()
        if centerPart and center then
            return center.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(centerPart.CFrame)
        end
        return centerPart
    end

    local result
    for _ = 1, 8 do
        local rx = (math.random() - 0.5) * math.min(area.Size.X - 8, 28)
        local rz = (math.random() - 0.5) * math.min(area.Size.Z - 8, 22)
        result = area.CFrame * CFrame.new(rx, 0.5, rz)

        if not belt or Vector3.new(
            result.X - belt.Position.X, 0,
            result.Z - belt.Position.Z
        ).Magnitude >= 14 then
            return center.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(result)
        end
    end

    return center.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(result)
end

local function tryPlaceEgg()
    if Flags.AutoPlaceEggs ~= true then return end
    if not P1.eggStateModule
        or type(P1.eggStateModule.PlantEgg) ~= "function" then return end

    local busy
    if not STEAL_STATE or not STEAL_STATE.running then busy = false
    else
        local st = STEAL_STATE.state
        busy = st == "GoEgg" or st == "Grab" or st == "Return"
            or st == "Bank" or st == "Chase" or st == "Safe"
            or st == "FeedGo" or st == "Feed"
            or st == "ChestGo" or st == "ChestOpen"
    end
    if busy then return end

    local _, root = getChar()
    local state = readPlotState()
    local area = state and state.PetArea
    if not area or not root then return end

    local onPad = (isNearBelt(root) and math.abs(
        root.Position.Y - (findBeltPart() and findBeltPart().Position.Y or 0)) < 10)
        or insideBox(area, root.Position, 2)

    if not onPad or engine.support then return end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - PLOT.lastPlace < 1.15 then return end

    local list, placedN = ownedEggsList()
    if #list == 0 then return end
    if placedN >= maxPlacedSlots() then
        PLOT.lastPlaceWhy = "pen full"
        return
    end

    local placement = randomPlacementCFrame()
    if not placement then
        PLOT.lastPlaceWhy = "no pad"
        return
    end

    PLOT.lastPlace = now
    local uid = list[1].uid

    if type(P1.eggStateModule.WearEggTool) == "function" then
        pcall(P1.eggStateModule.WearEggTool, uid)
    end

    local ok, result, reason = pcall(function()
        return P1.eggStateModule.PlantEgg(uid, placement)
    end)

    if type(P1.eggStateModule.DoffEggTool) == "function" then
        pcall(P1.eggStateModule.DoffEggTool, uid)
    end

    if ok and result == true then
        PLOT.placed += 1
        PLOT.lastPlaceWhy = nil
        warn("[Kira/plot] placed", uid)

        if ESP.bumpPlot then ESP.plotAt = 0 end
        return
    end

    PLOT.lastPlaceWhy = tostring(reason or result or (not ok and "err" or "rejected"))
    warn("[Kira/plot] place fail", uid, PLOT.lastPlaceWhy)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [7] Hatch eggs
--=====================================================================
local function hasReadyEgg()
    if Flags.AutoHatch ~= true then return false end
    if not P1.eggStateModule
        or type(P1.eggStateModule.IsReadyToHatch) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end

    local owned
    if type(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs) == "function" then
        local ok, res = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs, LocalPlayer.UserId)
        if ok and type(res) == "table" then owned = res end
    end
    if type(owned) ~= "table" then return false end

    for k, v in pairs(owned) do
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Placement ~= nil then
            local ok, ready = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.IsReadyToHatch, v.Uid or k)
            if ok and ready == true then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function tryHatch()
    if Flags.AutoHatch ~= true then return end
    if not P1.eggStateModule then return end

    local _, root = getChar()
    local state = readPlotState()
    local area = state and state.PetArea
    if not area or not root then return end

    local onPad = (isNearBelt(root) and math.abs(
        root.Position.Y - (findBeltPart() and findBeltPart().Position.Y or 0)) < 10)
        or insideBox(area, root.Position, 2)

    if not onPad or engine.support then return end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - PLOT.lastHatch < 0.9 then return end

    local owned
    if type(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs) == "function" then
        local ok, res = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs, LocalPlayer.UserId)
        if ok and type(res) == "table" then owned = res end
    end
    if type(owned) ~= "table" then return end

    for k, v in pairs(owned) do
        if type(v) == "table" and v.Placement ~= nil then
            local uid = v.Uid or k
            local ready = false
            if type(P1.eggStateModule.IsReadyToHatch) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.IsReadyToHatch, uid)
                ready = ok and res == true
            end

            if ready then
                PLOT.lastHatch = now
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    if type(P1.eggStateModule.BeginHatch) == "function" then
                        P1.eggStateModule.BeginHatch(uid)
                    end
                    if type(P1.eggStateModule.FinishHatch) == "function" then
                        P1.eggStateModule.FinishHatch(uid)
                    end
                end)

                if ok then
                    PLOT.hatched += 1
                    warn("[Kira/plot] hatched", uid)

                    if P1.webhook and P1.webhook.hatched then
                        local cat = v.AssetCategory or v.Category
                        local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
                        pcall(P1.webhook.hatched, {
                            name = cfg and cfg.DisplayName or v.AssetCategory,
                            earn = ratePerSecond(v, cfg),
                            rar = type(cfg) == "table"
                                and type(cfg.Rarity) == "table"
                                and tostring(cfg.Rarity.DisplayName
                                    or cfg.Rarity.Name
                                    or cfg.Rarity._id or "?") or "?",
                            cfg = cfg,
                            cat = cat,
                            rec = v,
                        })
                    end

                    if ESP.bumpPlot then ESP.plotAt = 0 end
                end
                return
            end
        end
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [8] Upgrades: Trails / Treadmill / Pen
--=====================================================================
local function tryUpgTrails()
    if Flags.UpgTrails ~= true then return end

    local trails = readTrails()
    local directory = trails and (trails.Directory or trails)
    local save = readSave()
    if type(directory) ~= "table" or type(save) ~= "table" then return end

    local inv = type(save.TrailInventory) == "table" and save.TrailInventory or {}

    local list = {}
    for _, v in pairs(directory) do
        if type(v) == "table" and type(v._id) == "string" then
            list[#list+1] = v
        end
    end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b)
        return (tonumber(a.Price) or 0) < (tonumber(b.Price) or 0)
    end)

    for i = 1, #list do
        local entry = list[i]
        if inv[entry._id] ~= true then
            local price = tonumber(entry.Price) or 0
            if price > 0 then
                local keep = numberFromString(Flags.KeepMoney)
                local money = tonumber(readSave() and readSave().Money) or 0
                if money - price >= keep then
                    local ok, bought = pcall(invokeRemote,
                        "Trailwear", "AskPurchase", entry._id)
                    if ok and bought == true then
                        PLOT.bought += 1
                        warn("[Kira/plot] trail bought", entry._id)
                        pcall(invokeRemote, "Trailwear", "AskChoose", entry._id)
                    end
                end
            end
            return
        end
    end

    -- All owned → equip latest
    local equipped
    for i = 1, #list do
        if inv[list[i]._id] == true then equipped = list[i] end
    end
    if equipped and save.EquippedTrail ~= equipped._id then
        pcall(invokeRemote, "Trailwear", "AskChoose", equipped._id)
    end
end

local function tryUpgTreadmill()
    if Flags.UpgTreadmill ~= true then return end

    local tms = readTreadmills()
    local save = readSave()
    if type(tms) ~= "table" or type(save) ~= "table" then return end

    local level = (tonumber(save.TreadmillUpgradeLevel) or 0) + 1
    local entry
    if type(tms.GetByUpgradeLevel) == "function" then
        pcall(function() entry = tms.GetByUpgradeLevel(level) end)
    end
    if type(entry) ~= "table" then return end

    local price = tonumber(entry.Price) or 0
    if price > 0 then
        local keep = numberFromString(Flags.KeepMoney)
        local money = tonumber(readSave() and readSave().Money) or 0
        if money - price < keep then return end
    end

    local ok, bought = pcall(invokeRemote, "Treadmill", "AskTierRaise", entry._id)
    if ok and bought == true then
        warn("[Kira/plot] treadmill upgrade", entry._id)
    end
end

local function tryUpgPen()
    if Flags.UpgPen ~= true then return end

    local save = readSave()
    if type(save) ~= "table" then return end

    local bases = readBases()
    local level = (tonumber(save.BaseUpgradeLevel) or 0) + 1
    local entry = bases and bases.BASES and bases.BASES[level]
    if type(entry) ~= "table" then return end

    local cost = tonumber(entry.Cost) or 0
    if cost > 0 then
        local keep = numberFromString(Flags.KeepMoney)
        local money = tonumber(readSave() and readSave().Money) or 0
        if money - cost < keep then return end
    end

    fireRemote("Homestead", "AskBaseTierRaise")
    warn("[Kira/plot] pen tier", level)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [9] Selling system
--=====================================================================
local function equippedSet(save)
    local set = {}
    if type(save) == "table" and type(save.EquippedAssets) == "table" then
        for _, v in pairs(save.EquippedAssets) do
            set[tostring(v)] = true
        end
    end
    return set
end

local function computeSellCandidates()
    local floor = numberFromString(Flags.SellUnderGen)
    local pets, eggs = {}, {}

    if floor <= 0 then
        PLOT.sellPets = pets
        PLOT.sellEggs = eggs
        PLOT.sellFloor = floor
        return pets, eggs, floor
    end

    local save = readSave()
    local equipped = equippedSet(save)

    if type(save) == "table" and type(save.Inventory) == "table" then
        for k, v in pairs(save.Inventory) do
            if type(v) == "table" then
                local uid = tostring(k)
                local fav = v.IsFavorite == true
                if not equipped[uid] and not fav and v.InFuse ~= true then
                    local cat = v.Category or v.AssetCategory
                    local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
                    local rate = ratePerSecond(v, cfg)
                    if rate < floor then
                        pets[#pets+1] = {
                            uid = uid,
                            cat = cat,
                            cfg = cfg,
                            earn = rate,
                            rec = v,
                            price = 0,
                        }
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local owned
    if P1.eggStateModule and type(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs) == "function" then
        local ok, res = pcall(P1.eggStateModule.ReadOwnerEggs, LocalPlayer.UserId)
        if ok and type(res) == "table" then owned = res end
    end
    if type(owned) ~= "table" then
        owned = save and save.EggInventory
    end

    if type(owned) == "table" then
        for k, v in pairs(owned) do
            if type(v) == "table" and v.Placement == nil then
                local cat = v.AssetCategory or v.Category
                local cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
                local rate = ratePerSecond(v, cfg)
                if rate < floor then
                    eggs[#eggs+1] = {
                        uid = tostring(v.Uid or k),
                        cat = cat,
                        cfg = cfg,
                        earn = rate,
                        rec = v,
                        price = 0,
                    }
                end
            end
        end
    end

    PLOT.sellPets = pets
    PLOT.sellEggs = eggs
    PLOT.sellFloor = floor

    return pets, eggs, floor
end

local function findSellStand()
    local stands = workspace:FindFirstChild("Stands")
    local prompts = stands and stands:FindFirstChild("Prompts")
    local node = prompts and (prompts:FindFirstChild("SellHeldAsset")
        or prompts:FindFirstChild("SellAll"))
    if not node or not node:IsA("BasePart") then return end

    local prompt = node:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt")
    if prompt then
        pcall(function()
            prompt.HoldDuration = 0
            prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
            prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 14
            prompt.ClickablePrompt = true
        end)
    end

    local look = Vector3.new(
        node.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, node.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
    if look.Magnitude < 0.15 then
        look = Vector3.new(
            node.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, node.CFrame.RightVector.Z)
    end

    local dir = look.Magnitude > 0.15 and look.Unit or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
    local a = node.Position + dir * 7
    local b = node.Position - dir * 7

    local _, root = getChar()
    local pick = a
    if root and Vector3.new(
        root.Position.X - a.X, 0, root.Position.Z - a.Z
    ).Magnitude > Vector3.new(
        root.Position.X - b.X, 0, root.Position.Z - b.Z
    ).Magnitude + 1.5 then
        pick = b
    end

    local y = node.Position.Y + 3.2

    pcall(function()
        local origin = Vector3.new(pick.X, node.Position.Y + 16, pick.Z)
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
            LocalPlayer.Character, node,
        }
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -80, 0), params)
        if hit then y = hit.Position.Y + 3 end
    end)

    if root and Vector3.new(
        root.Position.X - pick.X, 0,
        root.Position.Z - pick.Z
    ).Magnitude < 6 then
        y = math.min(y, root.Position.Y)
    end

    return Vector3.new(pick.X, y, pick.Z), node, prompt
end

local function findCarryToolUid(tool)
    if not tool then return nil end
    if tool:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Gear" then return nil end
    local uid = tool:GetAttribute("UID") or tool:GetAttribute("Uid")
    return type(uid) == "string" and uid ~= "" and uid or nil
end

local function findToolByUid(uid)
    local function scan(container)
        if not container then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") then
                local id = child:GetAttribute("UID") or child:GetAttribute("Uid")
                if id == uid then return child end
            end
        end
    end
    return scan(LocalPlayer.Character)
        or scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"))
end

local function unequipCurrent()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
    if not tool then return end

    local uid = findCarryToolUid(tool)
    local itemType = tool:GetAttribute("ItemType")

    if itemType == "AssetEgg" and uid then
        pcall(invokeRemote, "EggWorld", "AskDoffTool", uid)
        return
    end
    if itemType == "Asset" and uid then
        pcall(invokeRemote, "PenRoster", "AskDoff", uid)
        return
    end

    local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function() humanoid:UnequipTools() end)
    end
end

local function equipUid(item)
    local uid = item and item.uid and tostring(item.uid)
    if not uid then return false end

    local current = findCarryToolUid(
        LocalPlayer.Character
        and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool"))
    if current == uid then return true end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (PLOT.lastWear or 0) < 0.4 then return false end

    local existing = LocalPlayer.Character
        and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
    if existing and findCarryToolUid(existing) ~= uid then
        PLOT.lastWear = now
        unequipCurrent()
        return false
    end

    local tool = findToolByUid(uid)
    local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character
        and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if tool and humanoid then
        PLOT.lastWear = now
        if tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
            pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(tool) end)
        end
        local nowUid = findCarryToolUid(
            LocalPlayer.Character
            and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool"))
        return nowUid == uid
    end

    PLOT.lastWear = now
    if item.kind == "egg" then
        invokeRemote("EggWorld", "AskWearTool", uid)
    else
        invokeRemote("PenRoster", "AskWear", uid)
    end
    return false
end

local function sellUid(uid)
    fireRemote("PetSatchel", "SellPet", { uid })
    local _, _, prompt = findSellStand()
    if prompt then
        pcall(function()
            prompt:InputHoldBegin()
            prompt:InputHoldEnd()
        end)
    end
end

local function chooseNextSell()
    local pets, eggs = computeSellCandidates()

    if Flags.AutoSellPets == true and #pets > 0 then
        table.sort(pets, function(a, b) return a.earn < b.earn end)
        pets[1].kind = "pet"
        return pets[1]
    end

    if Flags.AutoSellEggs == true and #eggs > 0 then
        table.sort(eggs, function(a, b) return a.earn < b.earn end)
        eggs[1].kind = "egg"
        return eggs[1]
    end
end

local function trySell()
    if not Flags.AutoSellPets and not Flags.AutoSellEggs then return end

    -- Detect completion of previous sell
    if PLOT.expectSold then
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
        local uid = findCarryToolUid(tool)

        if uid ~= PLOT.expectSold.uid then
            if PLOT.expectSold.kind == "egg" then
                PLOT.soldEggs += 1
            else
                PLOT.soldPets += 1
            end

            if P1.webhook and P1.webhook.sold then
                pcall(P1.webhook.sold, PLOT.expectSold)
            end

            PLOT.expectSold = nil
        end
    end

    local _, root = getChar()
    local standPos, _, prompt = findSellStand()

    if standPos then
        local dist = not root and 999 or Vector3.new(
            root.Position.X - standPos.X, 0,
            root.Position.Z - standPos.Z
        ).Magnitude
        if not root or dist >= 5.5 then return end
    end

    local pick = chooseNextSell()
    if not pick then return end

    if not equipUid(pick) then
        PLOT.sellWhy = "equipping"
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - PLOT.lastSell < 0.55 then return end
    PLOT.lastSell = now
    PLOT.sellWhy = "selling"
    sellUid(pick.uid)

    PLOT.expectSold = {
        uid = pick.uid,
        kind = pick.kind,
        name = pick.cfg and pick.cfg.DisplayName or pick.cat,
        earn = pick.earn,
        cfg = pick.cfg,
        cat = pick.cat,
    }

    warn("[Kira/plot] sell held", pick.kind, pick.uid)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [10] Claim index
--=====================================================================
local function tryClaimIndex()
    if Flags.ClaimIndex ~= true then return end
    if PLOT.claimBusy or PLOT.job == "sell" then return end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (PLOT.lastClaim or 0) < 8 then return end
    PLOT.lastClaim = now
    PLOT.claimBusy = true

    local remote = getRemote("Codex", "AskRedeemAll")
    if not remote then
        PLOT.claimWhy = "no remote"
        PLOT.claimBusy = false
        return
    end

    local ok, result, message, payload = pcall(function()
        return remote:InvokeServer()
    end)

    if not ok then
        PLOT.claimWhy = tostring(result)
    elseif result == true then
        local count = 0
        if type(payload) == "table" then
            for _ in ipairs(payload) do count += 1 end
            if count == 0 then
                for _ in pairs(payload) do count += 1 end
            end
        end

        if count > 0 then
            PLOT.claimIndex += count
            PLOT.claimWhy = "claimed " .. count
            if P1.webhook and P1.webhook.rewards then
                pcall(P1.webhook.rewards, count)
            end
        else
            PLOT.claimWhy = "nothing to claim"
        end
    else
        PLOT.claimWhy = tostring(message or "nothing to claim")
    end

    PLOT.claimBusy = false
end

--=====================================================================
--  [11] Treadmill training
--=====================================================================
local function tryTreadmill()
    if Flags.AutoTreadmill ~= true then
        leaveBelt()
        return
    end

    if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running
        and STEAL_STATE.allowTrain
        and not STEAL_STATE.allowTrain() then
        return
    end

    -- Check early reset window
    local earlyReady = (tonumber(Flags.ReadyEarly) or 4) >= P1.secondsToNight()
    local autoStealBusy = Flags.AutoSteal == true
        and STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running
        and (not STEAL_STATE.allowTrain or STEAL_STATE.allowTrain() ~= true)

    if earlyReady or autoStealBusy then
        if PLOT.training or PLOT.job == "belt" then leaveBelt() end
        return
    end

    -- Don't train while auto place is pending
    if Flags.AutoPlaceEggs == true then
        local list, placedN = ownedEggsList()
        if #list > 0 and placedN < maxPlacedSlots() then
            local now = os.clock()
            local save = readSave()
            local power = tonumber(save and save.SpeedPower) or 0

            if PLOT.lastPower and now > PLOT.lastPowerAt then
                local dt = now - PLOT.lastPowerAt
                if dt > 0.2 then
                    local delta = math.max(0, power - PLOT.lastPower)
                    PLOT.trainEarned += delta
                    PLOT.trainRate = delta / dt
                end
            end

            PLOT.lastPower = power
            PLOT.lastPowerAt = now

            local belt = findBeltPart()
            local beltTop = belt and belt.Position + Vector3.new(
                0, belt.Size.Y * 0.5 + 3.2, 0)
            local _, root = getChar()
            if not beltTop or not root then return end

            PLOT.job = "belt"

            if not belt or not root or not (
                isNearBelt(root) and math.abs(
                    root.Position.Y - belt.Position.Y) < 10) then
                PLOT.training = false
                flyTo(beltTop)
                return
            end

            engine.driving = false
            engine.target = nil

            if engine.support or now - PLOT.lastTrain < 1.2 then return end
            PLOT.lastTrain = now

            local ok, result, reason = pcall(function()
                return invokeRemote("Treadmill", "AskWearStill")
            end)

            local training
            if ok and result == true then training = true
            else
                local b = findBeltPart()
                training = b and root and isNearBelt(root)
                    and math.abs(root.Position.Y - b.Position.Y) < 10
            end

            PLOT.training = training

            if not ok or result ~= true then
                warn("[Kira/plot] wear still fail:", tostring(reason or result))
            end
            return
        end
    end

    if hasReadyEgg() then
        PLOT.job = "idle"
        if PLOT.training then leaveBelt() end
        return
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [12] Status painter
--=====================================================================
local function paintStatus()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - PLOT.lastPaint < 0.45 then return end
    PLOT.lastPaint = now

    -- Auto place
    local placeText = "idle"
    if Flags.AutoPlaceEggs then
        placeText = PLOT.job ~= "pad"
            and (PLOT.lastPlaceWhy and tostring(PLOT.lastPlaceWhy) or "running")
            or "flying to plot"
    end
    local placeStr = placeText .. " · placed " .. PLOT.placed
        .. " · hatched " .. PLOT.hatched
    local w = Widgets.AutoPlaceEggs
    if w and w.status then pcall(function() w.status.Text = placeStr end) end

    -- Upgrades
    local trailStr = (not Flags.UpgTrails and "idle" or "running")
        .. " · bought " .. PLOT.bought
    local w2 = Widgets.UpgTrails
    if w2 and w2.status then pcall(function() w2.status.Text = trailStr end) end

    -- Sell
    local pets, eggs, floor = computeSellCandidates()
    local petsN = #pets
    local eggsN = #eggs
    local sellState = "off"
    local busy = false

    if Flags.AutoSellPets or Flags.AutoSellEggs then
        if floor <= 0 then
            sellState = "set a $/s floor"
            busy = true
        end

        if not busy then
            if PLOT.job ~= "sell" then
                sellState = (petsN + eggsN) ~= 0
                    and "selling" or "nothing under the floor"
                busy = true
            end

            if not busy then
                local _, root = getChar()
                if root then
                    local standPos = findSellStand()
                    if standPos and Vector3.new(
                        root.Position.X - standPos.X, 0,
                        root.Position.Z - standPos.Z
                    ).Magnitude < 5.5 then
                        sellState = PLOT.sellWhy or "equipping"
                        busy = true
                    end
                end

                if not busy then
                    sellState = "flying to seller"
                    busy = true
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local w3 = Widgets.AutoSellPets
    if w3 and w3.status then
        pcall(function()
            w3.status.Text = sellState .. " · sold " .. PLOT.soldPets
                .. " · " .. petsN .. " waiting"
        end)
    end

    local w4 = Widgets.AutoSellEggs
    if w4 and w4.status then
        pcall(function()
            w4.status.Text = sellState .. " · sold " .. PLOT.soldEggs
                .. " · " .. eggsN .. " waiting"
        end)
    end

    -- Claim
    local claimStr = (not Flags.ClaimIndex and "off"
        or (PLOT.claimWhy or "running"))
        .. " · claimed " .. PLOT.claimIndex
    local w5 = Widgets.ClaimIndex
    if w5 and w5.status then pcall(function() w5.status.Text = claimStr end) end

    -- Treadmill
    local trainStr
    if PLOT.job == "leave" then
        trainStr = "leaving · " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainRate)
            .. "/s · earned " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainEarned) .. " this session"
    elseif PLOT.job == "belt" and not PLOT.training then
        trainStr = "flying to treadmill · " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainRate)
            .. "/s · earned " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainEarned) .. " this session"
    elseif PLOT.training then
        trainStr = "training · " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainRate)
            .. "/s · earned " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainEarned) .. " this session"
    else
        trainStr = "not training · " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainRate)
            .. "/s · earned " .. formatShort(PLOT.trainEarned) .. " this session"
    end

    local w6 = Widgets.AutoTreadmill
    if w6 and w6.status then pcall(function() w6.status.Text = trainStr end) end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [13] Main tick
--=====================================================================
local function plotTick()
    if not engine.loaded then return end
    if not engineShouldRunPlot() then return end

    plotDrive()
    tryPlaceEgg()
    tryHatch()

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - PLOT.lastUpg >= 4
        and (Flags.UpgTrails or Flags.UpgTreadmill or Flags.UpgPen) then
        PLOT.lastUpg = now
        tryUpgTrails()
        tryUpgTreadmill()
        tryUpgPen()
    end

    if Flags.EquipBest == true then
        if now - PLOT.lastEquip >= 4 then
            PLOT.lastEquip = now
            pcall(function()
                invokeRemote("Haul", "WearBest")
            end)
        end
    end

    trySell()
    tryClaimIndex()
    paintStatus()
end

local function engineShouldRunPlot()
    return Flags.AutoPlaceEggs
        or Flags.AutoHatch
        or Flags.EquipBest
        or Flags.UpgTrails
        or Flags.UpgTreadmill
        or Flags.UpgPen
        or Flags.AutoSellPets
        or Flags.AutoSellEggs
        or Flags.AutoTreadmill
        or Flags.ClaimIndex
end

--=====================================================================
--  [14] Plot drive (walk to plot/belt/seller)
--=====================================================================
local function plotDrive()
    local _, root = getChar()
    if not root then return end

    -- Abort if steal is busy
    local busy
    if not STEAL_STATE or not STEAL_STATE.running then busy = false
    else
        local st = STEAL_STATE.state
        busy = st == "GoEgg" or st == "Grab" or st == "Return"
            or st == "Bank" or st == "Chase" or st == "Safe"
            or st == "FeedGo" or st == "Feed"
            or st == "ChestGo" or st == "ChestOpen"
    end

    if busy then
        if PLOT.job == "sell" then PLOT.job = "idle" end
        if isNearBelt(root) then
            PLOT.job = "leave"
            hopToward(true)
        end
        return
    end

    -- Handle sell
    if (Flags.AutoSellPets or Flags.AutoSellEggs)
        and numberFromString(Flags.SellUnderGen) > 0 then
        local pets, eggs = computeSellCandidates()
        local n = (Flags.AutoSellPets and #pets or 0)
            + (Flags.AutoSellEggs and #eggs or 0)

        if n > 0 then
            if isNearBelt(root) then
                PLOT.job = "leave"
                hopToward()
                return
            end

            local standPos = findSellStand()
            if standPos then
                PLOT.job = "sell"
                flyTo(standPos, true)
                return
            end

            PLOT.job = "sell"
            if engine.driving or engine.target then
                engine.driving = false
                engine.target = nil
            end
            return
        end

        if PLOT.job == "sell" then
            PLOT.job = "idle"
            engine.driving = false
            engine.target = nil
        end
    elseif PLOT.job == "sell" then
        PLOT.job = "idle"
        engine.driving = false
        engine.target = nil
    end

    -- Handle place
    if Flags.AutoPlaceEggs == true then
        local list, placedN = ownedEggsList()
        if #list > 0 and placedN < maxPlacedSlots() then
            if isNearBelt(root) then
                PLOT.job = "leave"
                hopToward()
                return
            end

            PLOT.job = "pad"
            local padPos = getPetAreaPos()
            if padPos then
                local state = readPlotState()
                local area = state and state.PetArea
                local onPad = area and insideBox(area, root.Position, 2)
                if onPad then
                    engine.driving = false
                    engine.target = nil
                    return
                end
                flyTo(padPos)
            end
            return
        end
    end

    -- Handle treadmill
    if Flags.AutoTreadmill == true
        and (Flags.AutoSteal ~= true
            or not STEAL_STATE
            or (not STEAL_STATE.running
                or (STEAL_STATE.allowTrain
                    and STEAL_STATE.allowTrain() == true))) then
        tryTreadmill()
        return
    end

    if PLOT.job == "pad" or PLOT.job == "belt" or PLOT.job == "sell" then
        PLOT.job = "idle"
    end

    if not STEAL_STATE or not STEAL_STATE.running then
        engine.driving = false
        engine.target = nil
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [15] Sell preview API
--=====================================================================
local function sellSnapshot()
    local pets, eggs, floor = computeSellCandidates()
    return pets, eggs, floor
end

function P1.plot.sellSnapshot()
    return sellSnapshot()
end

local function queuePreview()
    local pets, eggs, floor = computeSellCandidates()

    if ESP.sellPreview then
        ESP.sellPreview(pets, eggs, floor, "opening…")
    end

    PLOT.previewQueued = true

    if PLOT.previewConn then return end

    local conn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not engine.loaded then return end
        if not PLOT.previewQueued then return end

        PLOT.previewQueued = false
        local self = PLOT.previewConn
        PLOT.previewConn = nil

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local p, e, f = computeSellCandidates()
            if ESP.sellPreview then
                ESP.sellPreview(p, e, f)
            end
        end)

        if not ok then
            warn("[Kira/plot] preview ERR:", tostring(err))
        end

        if self then pcall(function() self:Disconnect() end) end
    end)
    trackConn(conn)
    PLOT.previewConn = conn
end

P1.plot.queuePreview = queuePreview

--=====================================================================
--  [16] Bootstrap + widget wiring
--=====================================================================
local function plotWanted()
    return engineShouldRunPlot()
end

local function plotDriving()
    return engine.driving == true
        and (PLOT.job == "pad" or PLOT.job == "belt" or PLOT.job == "sell")
end

local function plotLeaving()
    return PLOT.job == "leave"
end

local function plotTickSafe()
    local ok, err = pcall(plotTick)
    if not ok then
        if os.clock() - (PLOT.lastErrAt or 0) > 2 then
            PLOT.lastErrAt = os.clock()
            warn("[Kira/plot] tick ERR:", tostring(err))
        end
    end
end

local function plotStop()
    PLOT.training = false
    PLOT.job = "idle"

    if not STEAL_STATE or not STEAL_STATE.running then
        engine.driving = false
        engine.target = nil
    end

    if engine.on then
        if P1.setEngineOn then
            P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
        end
    end
end

local function plotSync()
    if not engineShouldRunPlot() then
        leaveBelt()
    end

    if P1.setEngineOn then
        P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
    end
end

local function plotKickBelt()
    local _, root = getChar()
    if not root or not isNearBelt(root) then
        if PLOT.training or PLOT.job == "belt" then
            PLOT.training = false
            PLOT.job = "idle"
        end
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (PLOT.lastKick or 0) < 0.55 then return end
    PLOT.lastKick = now

    PLOT.training = false
    PLOT.job = "leave"

    local busy
    if not STEAL_STATE or not STEAL_STATE.running then busy = false
    else
        local st = STEAL_STATE.state
        busy = st == "GoEgg" or st == "Grab" or st == "Return"
            or st == "Bank" or st == "Chase" or st == "Safe"
            or st == "FeedGo" or st == "Feed"
            or st == "ChestGo" or st == "ChestOpen"
    end

    if not busy then busy = (STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running) == true end
    hopToward(busy)
end

local function plotHolding()
    return PLOT.training == true or PLOT.job == "sell"
end

local function plotStats()
    return {
        hatched = PLOT.hatched,
        soldPets = PLOT.soldPets,
        soldEggs = PLOT.soldEggs,
        placed = PLOT.placed,
        claimIndex = PLOT.claimIndex,
        claimCash = PLOT.claimCash,
    }
end

-- Attach the API
PLOT.tick = plotTickSafe
PLOT.sync = plotSync
PLOT.stop = plotStop
PLOT.wanted = plotWanted
PLOT.driving = plotDriving
PLOT.leaving = plotLeaving
PLOT.leave = leaveBelt
PLOT.kickBelt = plotKickBelt
PLOT.holding = plotHolding
PLOT.stats = plotStats
PLOT.preview = function()
    local pets, eggs = computeSellCandidates()
    if ESP.sellPreview then ESP.sellPreview(pets, eggs) end
end
PLOT.computeSellCandidates = computeSellCandidates
PLOT.findSellStand = findSellStand

-- Wire plot widgets
local function wire(flag, fn)
    local w = Widgets[flag]
    if w then w.on = fn
    else warn("[Kira/wire/plot] missing:", flag) end
end

wire("ClaimIndex", plotSync)
wire("AutoPlaceEggs", plotSync)
wire("AutoHatch", plotSync)
wire("EquipBest", plotSync)
wire("UpgTrails", plotSync)
wire("UpgTreadmill", plotSync)
wire("UpgPen", plotSync)
wire("AutoSellPets", plotSync)
wire("AutoSellEggs", plotSync)
wire("AutoTreadmill", function(on)
    if not on then leaveBelt() end
    plotSync()
end)
wire("SellPreview", function()
    P1.plot.queuePreview()
end)

--=====================================================================
--  [17] Boot + tick
--=====================================================================
plotSync()

--=====================================================================
--  Kira Hub :: Steal an Egg
--  Decoded — Part 9/9
--  Auto Hop + Webhook + Boot + Shutdown (FINAL)
--=====================================================================

local P1 = KIRA_PART1
assert(P1, "Part 1 must be loaded first")
assert(P1.plot, "Part 8 must be loaded first")

local META          = P1.META
local Flags         = P1.Flags
local Widgets       = P1.Widgets
local LocalPlayer   = P1.LocalPlayer
local isMobile      = P1.isMobile
local ScreenGui     = P1.ScreenGui
local SCREEN_NAME   = P1.SCREEN_NAME
local WORLD_NAME    = P1.WORLD_NAME

local HttpService       = game:GetService("HttpService")
local RunService        = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService  = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TeleportService   = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local engine       = P1.engine
local internals    = P1.engineInternals
local getChar      = internals.getChar
local trackConn    = internals.trackConn
local disconnectAll= internals.disconnectAll

local STEAL_STATE  = P1.stealState
local ESP          = P1.esp
local PLOT         = P1.plot
local AssetDirectory = P1.AssetDirectory

local formatShort  = P1.formatShort
local ratePerSecond = P1.ratePerSecond
local numberFromString = P1.numberFromString
local findSpawn    = P1.findSpawn

local function safeName(s)
    return P1.sanitizeName(s)
end

--=====================================================================
--  [1] AUTO HOP
--=====================================================================
local HOP = (function()
    local PlaceId = game.PlaceId
    local sessionStart = os.clock()
    local hopCount = 0
    local status = "off"
    local serverList = {}
    local teleporting = false
    local hopPending = false
    local lastHopAt = 0
    local cooldownUntil = 0
    local nextDelay = 10
    local lastFailAt = 0
    local rateLimitedUntil = 0
    local lastIdleReset = 0

    local bankedSnapshot = tonumber(STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.banked) or 0
    local phase = "idle"
    local stayingForEggs = false

    local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    env.KiraHopUsed  = type(env.KiraHopUsed)  == "table" and env.KiraHopUsed  or {}
    env.KiraHopRing  = type(env.KiraHopRing)  == "table" and env.KiraHopRing  or {}
    env.KiraHopCache = type(env.KiraHopCache) == "table" and env.KiraHopCache or {}

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.1] Storage helpers
    --=====================================================================
    local function parseSeconds(str)
        if type(str) ~= "string" then return 0 end
        local s = string.lower(str:gsub(",", ""):gsub("%s+", ""))
        if s == "" or s == "off" or s:find("blank", 1, true) then return 0 end
        return tonumber(s:match("([%d%.]+)")) or 0
    end

    local function ringPush(id)
        if type(id) ~= "string" or id == "" then return end
        local ring = env.KiraHopRing
        for i = #ring, 1, -1 do
            if id == ring[i] then table.remove(ring, i) end
        end
        ring[#ring+1] = id
        while #ring > 48 do table.remove(ring, 1) end
    end

    local function isUsed(id)
        local s = tostring(id or "")
        if s == "" or s == tostring(game.JobId) then return true end

        for i = 1, #env.KiraHopRing do
            if s == env.KiraHopRing[i] then return true end
        end

        local t = env.KiraHopUsed[s]
        if type(t) ~= "number" then return false end
        if os.time() - t > 2100 then
            env.KiraHopUsed[s] = nil
            return false
        end
        return true
    end

    local function loadUsed()
        if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
        local ok, raw = pcall(readfile,
            ("Kira/%s/cache/hop-used.json"):format(safeName(META.Game)))
        if not ok or type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" then return end

        local data
        if not pcall(function() data = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end) then return end
        if type(data) ~= "table" then return end
        if tonumber(data.place) and tonumber(data.place) ~= PlaceId then return end

        local now = os.time()
        if type(data.jobs) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(data.jobs) do
                local t = type(v) == "number" and v
                    or type(v) == "table" and tonumber(v.t)
                local s = tostring(k)
                if t and now - t <= 2100
                    and t > (tonumber(env.KiraHopUsed[s]) or 0) then
                    env.KiraHopUsed[s] = t
                end
            end
        end

        if type(data.ring) == "table" then
            local seen, out = {}, {}
            for i = 1, #data.ring do
                local v = data.ring[i]
                if type(v) == "table" then v = v.id end
                if type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" and not seen[v] then
                    seen[v] = true
                    out[#out+1] = v
                end
            end
            for i = 1, #env.KiraHopRing do
                local v = env.KiraHopRing[i]
                if type(v) == "table" then v = v.id end
                if type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" and not seen[v] then
                    seen[v] = true
                    out[#out+1] = v
                end
            end
            env.KiraHopRing = out
            while #out > 48 do table.remove(out, 1) end
        end
    end

    local function saveUsed()
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
        if type(makefolder) == "function" then
            pcall(makefolder, "Kira")
            pcall(makefolder, "Kira/" .. safeName(META.Game))
            pcall(makefolder, "Kira/" .. safeName(META.Game) .. "/cache")
            pcall(makefolder, "Kira/" .. safeName(META.Game) .. "/configs")
        end

        local now = os.time()
        local jobs = {}
        for k, v in pairs(env.KiraHopUsed) do
            if type(v) == "number" and now - v <= 2100 then
                jobs[tostring(k)] = { t = v, by = P1.UserIdStr }
            end
        end

        local payload = {
            v = 1,
            place = PlaceId,
            jobs = jobs,
            ring = env.KiraHopRing,
        }

        local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
        end)
        if ok and type(encoded) == "string" then
            pcall(writefile,
                ("Kira/%s/cache/hop-used.json"):format(safeName(META.Game)),
                encoded)
        end
    end

    loadUsed()
    local myJob = tostring(game.JobId or "")
    if myJob ~= "" then
        env.KiraHopUsed[myJob] = os.time()
        ringPush(myJob)
    end
    saveUsed()

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.2] HTTP fetch (via exec request if available)
    --=====================================================================
    local function httpGet(url, timeout)
        timeout = tonumber(timeout) or 2.2
        local req = syn and syn.request
            or http_request or request
            or (http and http.request)
            or (fluxus and fluxus.request)

        local done, result, err = false, nil, nil

        if req then
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, res = pcall(req, {
                    Url = url,
                    Method = "GET",
                    Headers = { Accept = "application/json" },
                })
                done = true
                if ok then result = res else err = res end
            end)
        elseif type(game.HttpGet) == "function" then
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, res = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, url)
                done = true
                if ok then
                    result = { StatusCode = 200, Body = res }
                else
                    err = res
                end
            end)
        else
            return nil, "no http"
        end

        local start = os.clock()
        while not done and os.clock() - start < timeout do
            task.wait(0.05)
        end

        if not done then return nil, "slow" end
        if err then return nil, tostring(err) end

        local status = result
            and tonumber(result.StatusCode or result.status_code or result.Status)
        local body = result and (result.Body or result.body)

        if status == 429 then
            local wait = nextDelay
            local headers = result and (result.Headers or result.headers)
            if type(headers) == "table" then
                local ra = tonumber(headers["Retry-After"]
                    or headers["retry-after"] or headers["Retry-after"])
                if ra and ra > 0 then wait = math.max(wait, ra) end
            end
            rateLimitedUntil = os.clock() + wait
            nextDelay = math.min(60, math.max(12, nextDelay * 2))
            return nil, "rate limited"
        end

        if status and status >= 400 then
            return nil, "http " .. tostring(status)
        end

        if type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then
            return nil, "empty"
        end

        nextDelay = 10
        return body
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.3] Cache key + list presets
    --=====================================================================
    local function cacheKey()
        local pages = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(Flags.HopPages) or 3), 1, 10)
        local skipFull = Flags.HopSkipFull ~= false
        local highest = Flags.HopPlayers == "Highest"
        return tostring(PlaceId)
            .. ":" .. (highest and "D" or "A")
            .. ":" .. (skipFull and "1" or "0")
            .. ":" .. tostring(pages)
    end

    local function buildServerList(raw)
        serverList = {}
        if type(raw) ~= "table" then return serverList end
        loadUsed()

        local myJob = tostring(game.JobId)
        for i = 1, #raw do
            local entry = raw[i]
            local id = entry and tostring(entry.id)
            if id and id ~= "" and id ~= myJob and not isUsed(id) then
                serverList[#serverList+1] = {
                    id = entry.id,
                    playing = tonumber(entry.playing) or 0,
                    maxPlayers = tonumber(entry.maxPlayers) or 0,
                }
            end
        end
        return serverList
    end

    local function tryCached(allowStale)
        local cache = env.KiraHopCache
        if type(cache) ~= "table" or cache.key ~= cacheKey()
            or type(cache.list) ~= "table" or #cache.list == 0 then
            cache = nil

            if type(readfile) == "function" then
                local ok, raw = pcall(readfile,
                    ("Kira/%s/cache/hop-list.json"):format(safeName(META.Game)))
                if ok and type(raw) == "string" and raw ~= "" then
                    local data
                    pcall(function() data = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
                    if type(data) == "table" and data.key == cacheKey()
                        and type(data.list) == "table" and #data.list > 0 then
                        cache = data
                        env.KiraHopCache = data
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if type(cache) ~= "table" or type(cache.list) ~= "table"
            or #cache.list == 0 then return end

        local age = os.time() - (tonumber(cache.at) or 0)
        if age > 90 and not allowStale then return end

        buildServerList(cache.list)
        if #serverList > 0 then return serverList, age end
    end

    local function writeCache(raw)
        if type(raw) ~= "table" or #raw == 0 then return end
        local payload = { key = cacheKey(), at = os.time(), list = raw }
        env.KiraHopCache = payload

        if type(writefile) == "function" then
            if type(makefolder) == "function" then
                pcall(makefolder, "Kira")
                pcall(makefolder, "Kira/" .. safeName(META.Game))
                pcall(makefolder, "Kira/" .. safeName(META.Game) .. "/cache")
                pcall(makefolder, "Kira/" .. safeName(META.Game) .. "/configs")
            end
            local ok, encoded = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
            end)
            if ok and type(encoded) == "string" then
                pcall(writefile,
                    ("Kira/%s/cache/hop-list.json"):format(safeName(META.Game)),
                    encoded)
            end
        end
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.4] Fetch from Roblox API
    --=====================================================================
    local function fetchServers()
        if tryCached(false) then return serverList, "cached" end
        if os.clock() < rateLimitedUntil then
            if tryCached(true) then return serverList, "cached" end
            return serverList, "rate limited"
        end

        local pages = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(Flags.HopPages) or 3), 1, 10)
        local skipFull = Flags.HopSkipFull ~= false
        local highest = Flags.HopPlayers == "Highest"
        local all = {}
        local cursor = ""

        for i = 1, pages do
            if i > 1 then task.wait(0.12) end

            local url = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"
                .. tostring(PlaceId) .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder="
                .. (highest and "Desc" or "Asc")
                .. "&excludeFullGames=" .. (skipFull and "true" or "false")
                .. "&limit=100"

            if cursor ~= "" then
                local enc = cursor
                pcall(function() enc = HttpService:UrlEncode(cursor) end)
                url = url .. "&cursor=" .. enc
            end

            local body, err = httpGet(url, 2.2)
            if not body then
                if #all > 0 then break end
                if tryCached(true) then return serverList, "cached" end
                return nil, err or "fetch"
            end

            local data
            if not pcall(function() data = HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end) then
                if #all > 0 then break end
                if tryCached(true) then return serverList, "cached" end
                return nil, "bad json"
            end

            local list = data and data.data
            if type(list) ~= "table" then
                if #all > 0 then break end
                if tryCached(true) then return serverList, "cached" end
                return nil, "bad json"
            end

            for j = 1, #list do
                local entry = list[j]
                if type(entry) == "table" and type(entry.id) == "string" then
                    all[#all+1] = {
                        id = entry.id,
                        playing = tonumber(entry.playing) or 0,
                        maxPlayers = tonumber(entry.maxPlayers) or 0,
                    }
                end
            end

            cursor = type(data.nextPageCursor) == "string"
                and data.nextPageCursor or ""
            if cursor == "" then break end
        end

        table.sort(all, function(a, b)
            if highest then return a.playing > b.playing end
            return a.playing < b.playing
        end)

        writeCache(all)
        buildServerList(all)
        return serverList
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.5] Ring cleanup + pick
    --=====================================================================
    local function pruneRing()
        local ring = env.KiraHopRing
        local seen, keep = {}, {}
        for i = math.max(1, #ring - 7), #ring do
            local id = ring[i]
            if type(id) == "string" and not seen[id] then
                seen[id] = true
                keep[#keep+1] = id
            end
        end

        local me = tostring(game.JobId)
