        if not seen[me] then keep[#keep+1] = me end
        env.KiraHopRing = keep

        local now = os.time()
        for k, v in pairs(env.KiraHopUsed) do
            if k ~= me and type(v) == "number" and now - v > 480 then
                env.KiraHopUsed[k] = nil
            end
        end

        saveUsed()
    end

    local function pickServer()
        loadUsed()

        local skipFull = Flags.HopSkipFull ~= false
        local me = tostring(game.JobId)
        local fresh, backup = {}, {}

        while #serverList > 0 do
            local entry = table.remove(serverList, 1)
            local id = entry and tostring(entry.id)

            if id and id ~= "" and id ~= me and not isUsed(id) then
                local max = tonumber(entry.maxPlayers) or 0
                if not skipFull or max <= 0 or max > (tonumber(entry.playing) or 0) then
                    if #fresh < 12 then
                        fresh[#fresh+1] = entry
                    else
                        backup[#backup+1] = entry
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        serverList = backup

        while #fresh > 0 do
            local idx = math.random(1, #fresh)
            local pick = table.remove(fresh, idx)
            local id = tostring(pick.id)
            loadUsed()
            if id ~= me and not isUsed(id) then
                local tag = tostring(id or "")
                if tag ~= "" then
                    env.KiraHopUsed[tag] = os.time()
                    ringPush(tag)
                end
                saveUsed()

                for i = #fresh, 1, -1 do
                    table.insert(serverList, 1, fresh[i])
                end
                return pick
            end
        end
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.6] Reason checkers
    --=====================================================================
    local function hasEggsAvailable()
        if Flags.AutoEvent == true then
            local st = STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.state
            if st == "FeedGo" or st == "Feed"
                or st == "ChestGo" or st == "ChestOpen" then
                return true
            end

            if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.findChestTool
                and STEAL_STATE.findChestTool() then return true end

            if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.target
                and STEAL_STATE.target.event then return true end

            if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.pickSatchelEvent then
                local ok, satchel = pcall(STEAL_STATE.pickSatchelEvent)
                if ok and type(satchel) == "table" then return satchel end
            end
        end

        if type(P1.pickBestTarget) ~= "function" then return end
        local ok, pick = pcall(P1.pickBestTarget)
        if ok and type(pick) == "table" and pick.matched == true
            and (pick.cfg or pick.earn) then
            return pick
        end
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.7] Hop action
    --=====================================================================
    local function doHop(reason)
        if hopPending or teleporting then return false end
        if os.clock() - lastHopAt < 2.4 then return false end

        hopPending = true
        lastHopAt = os.clock()
        pcall(P1.saveConfig, true)
        status = reason or "hopping"

        local label = not Flags.AutoHop and "off"
            or (status ~= "" and status or "on")
        local w = Widgets.AutoHop
        if w and w.status then
            pcall(function()
                w.status.Text = label .. " · " .. tostring(hopCount)
                    .. (hopCount ~= 1 and " hops this session"
                        or " hop this session")
            end)
        end

        if #serverList == 0 then
            local _, why = fetchServers()
            if #serverList == 0 then
                pruneRing()
                tryCached(true)

                if #serverList == 0 then
                    hopPending = false
                    status = tostring(why or "no servers")

                    local w2 = Widgets.AutoHop
                    if w2 and w2.status then
                        pcall(function()
                            w2.status.Text = status .. " · " .. tostring(hopCount)
                        end)
                    end

                    local now = Widgets.HopNow
                    if now and now.status then
                        pcall(function() now.status.Text = status end)
                    end

                    return false
                end
            end
        end

        hopCount += 1
        local startJob = tostring(game.JobId)
        local teleported = false

        while engine.loaded and hopPending do
            local pick = pickServer()
            if not pick then
                pruneRing()
                tryCached(true)
                pick = pickServer()
            end
            if not pick then break end

            env.KiraHopUntil = 0
            teleporting = true
            status = (reason or "hop") .. " · " .. tostring(#serverList) .. " left"

            local w3 = Widgets.AutoHop
            if w3 and w3.status then
                pcall(function()
                    w3.status.Text = status .. " · " .. tostring(hopCount)
                end)
            end

            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(
                    PlaceId, tostring(pick.id), LocalPlayer)
            end)

            if not ok then
                teleporting = false
                lastFailAt = os.clock()
                warn("[Kira/hop] fail:", tostring(err))
            else
                local start = os.clock()
                while engine.loaded and teleporting
                    and os.clock() - start < 2.2 do
                    if startJob ~= tostring(game.JobId) then
                        teleported = true
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end

                if startJob ~= tostring(game.JobId) then
                    teleported = true
                    break
                end

                pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportCancel() end)
                teleporting = false
                lastFailAt = os.clock()
            end
        end

        hopPending = false
        teleporting = false

        if teleported then return true end

        status = "no servers"
        local w4 = Widgets.AutoHop
        if w4 and w4.status then
            pcall(function()
                w4.status.Text = status .. " · " .. tostring(hopCount)
            end)
        end
        return false
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.8] Teleport fail handler
    --=====================================================================
    trackConn(TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function(player)
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then return end
        teleporting = false
        lastFailAt = os.clock()
    end))

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.9] Reason evaluator (tick)
    --=====================================================================
    local function tickReason()
        if not engine.loaded then return end

        local now = os.clock()
        local dt = now - sessionStart
        sessionStart = now

        if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.banked ~= bankedSnapshot then
            bankedSnapshot = STEAL_STATE.banked
            lastIdleReset = 0
        end

        local night = P1.isNight()
        local nightWindow = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleNightSeconds")
        if type(nightWindow) ~= "number" then nightWindow = 10 end
        local inNight = P1.secondsToNight() <= math.clamp(nightWindow, 1, 300)

        local hasEggs = false
        if not inNight then
            hasEggs = not not hasEggsAvailable()
        end

        if Flags.AutoSteal and STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running and not inNight then
            if hasEggs then
                lastIdleReset = 0
            else
                lastIdleReset += dt
            end
        end

        if not Flags.AutoHop then
            if phase ~= "idle" or stayingForEggs then
                phase = "idle"
                stayingForEggs = false
            end
            status = "off"
            local w = Widgets.AutoHop
            if w and w.status then
                pcall(function()
                    w.status.Text = "off · " .. tostring(hopCount)
                end)
            end
            return
        end

        if teleporting or hopPending then
            return
        end

        if now < sessionStart + 1.6 then
            status = "settling"
            return
        end

        if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.carrying == true then
            status = "carrying"
            return
        end

        phase = "idle"
        stayingForEggs = false

        local idleTimeout = parseSeconds(Flags.HopIdle)
        if idleTimeout > 0 then
            if idleTimeout < 5 then idleTimeout = 5 end

            if Flags.AutoSteal and STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running then
                if hasEggsAvailable() then
                    status = "eggs · grabbing"
                    return
                end

                status = "idle " .. tostring(math.floor(lastIdleReset))
                    .. "/" .. tostring(math.floor(idleTimeout)) .. "s"

                if idleTimeout <= lastIdleReset then
                    doHop("idle")
                    return
                end
            end
        end

        local maxMinutes = parseSeconds(Flags.HopAfter)
        if maxMinutes > 0 then
            if maxMinutes < 1 then maxMinutes = 1 end
            if maxMinutes <= (now - sessionStart) / 60 then
                status = "time"
                doHop("time")
                return
            end
        end

        if hasEggsAvailable() then
            status = "eggs · grabbing"
        elseif idleTimeout > 0 and Flags.AutoSteal
            and STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running then
            status = "idle " .. tostring(math.floor(lastIdleReset))
                .. "/" .. tostring(math.floor(idleTimeout)) .. "s"
        elseif stayingForEggs then
            status = "eggs · staying"
        elseif idleTimeout > 0 then
            status = "idle needs Auto Steal"
        else
            status = "on"
        end

        local w = Widgets.AutoHop
        if w and w.status then
            pcall(function()
                w.status.Text = status .. " · " .. tostring(hopCount)
                    .. (hopCount ~= 1 and " hops this session"
                        or " hop this session")
            end)
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while engine.loaded do
            task.wait(0.4)
            pcall(tickReason)
        end
    end)

    --=====================================================================
    --  [1.10] Public API
    --=====================================================================
    return {
        now = function()
            local w = Widgets.HopNow
            if w and w.status then w.status.Text = "going…" end

            local ok = doHop("now")

            if w and w.status then
                w.status.Text = (not ok) and status or "going…"
            end
            return ok
        end,
        stop = function()
            hopPending = false
            teleporting = false
            pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportCancel() end)
        end,
        hops = function() return hopCount end,
    }
end)()

P1.hop = HOP

--=====================================================================
--  [2] WEBHOOK
--=====================================================================
local WEBHOOK = (function()
    local sessionStart = os.clock()
    local lastDigest = os.clock()
    local iconCache = {}
    local userIconCache = {}

    local RARITY_COLORS = {
        common = 9807270,
        uncommon = 3066993,
        rare = 3447003,
        epic = 10181046,
        legendary = 15844367,
        mythic = 15158332,
        cosmic = 5793266,
        secret = 2303786,
        eternal = 16766720,
        divine = 16738740,
        titan = 16777215,
    }

    local function formatMoney(v)
        local n = tonumber(v) or 0
        local a = math.abs(n)
        if a >= 1e12 then return string.format("%.2fT", n/1e12) end
        if a >= 1e9  then return string.format("%.2fB", n/1e9)  end
        if a >= 1e6  then return string.format("%.2fm", n/1e6)  end
        if a >= 1e3  then return string.format("%.1fk", n/1e3)  end
        return string.format("%.0f", n)
    end

    local function rarityPass(name)
        local floor = Flags.HookRarityFloor
        if not floor or floor == "Any" then return true end

        local want, have = 0, 0
        local w = string.lower(tostring(floor or ""))
        local h = string.lower(tostring(name or ""))

        for i, v in ipairs(P1.RARITIES) do
            local lv = string.lower(v)
            if lv == w then want = i end
            if lv == h then have = i end
        end
        return want <= have
    end

    local function formatSession()
        local s = math.max(0, math.floor(os.clock() - sessionStart))
        local h = math.floor(s / 3600)
        local m = math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)
        local sec = s % 60
        if h > 0 then return string.format("%d:%02d:%02d", h, m, sec) end
        return string.format("%d:%02d", m, sec)
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [2.1] Thumbnail resolving
    --=====================================================================
    local function normalizeImage(v)
        if v == nil then return nil end
        local tp = typeof(v)
        if tp == "number" then
            if v > 100 then return "rbxassetid://" .. math.floor(v) end
            return nil
        end
        if tp == "string" then
            if v == "" or v == "0" or v == "rbxassetid://0" then return nil end
            if v:find("http", 1, true) or v:find("rbxasset", 1, true)
                or v:find("rbxthumb", 1, true) then
                return v
            end
            local n = tonumber(v)
            if n and n > 100 then
                return "rbxassetid://" .. math.floor(n)
            end
            return nil
        end
        if tp == "Instance" then
            if v:IsA("ImageLabel") or v:IsA("ImageButton") then
                return normalizeImage(v.Image)
            end
            if v:IsA("Decal") or v:IsA("Texture") then
                return normalizeImage(v.Texture)
            end
            local child = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageLabel", true)
                or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageButton", true)
                or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal", true)
            if child then return normalizeImage(child) end
            return nil
        end
        if tp == "table" then
            return normalizeImage(v.Image) or normalizeImage(v.ImageId)
                or normalizeImage(v.Icon) or normalizeImage(v.Id)
        end
    end

    local ICON_FIELDS = {
        "IndexImage", "IndexIcon", "Icon", "Image", "ImageId",
        "IconImage", "Thumbnail", "AssetImage", "PetImage",
        "EggImage", "RenderImage", "Picture",
    }

    local function extractCfgIcon(cfg)
        if type(cfg) ~= "table" then return nil end
        for _, k in ipairs(ICON_FIELDS) do
            local v = normalizeImage(cfg[k])
            if v then return v end
        end
        if type(cfg.Egg) == "table" then
            for _, k in ipairs(ICON_FIELDS) do
                local v = normalizeImage(cfg.Egg[k])
                if v then return v end
            end
        end
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [2.2] HTTP request helper
    --=====================================================================
    local function httpRequest(url, method, headers, body)
        local req = syn and syn.request
            or http_request or request
            or (http and http.request)
            or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
        if not req then return false, "no http" end

        local ok, res = pcall(req, {
            Url = url,
            Method = method or "GET",
            Headers = headers or {},
            Body = body,
        })

        if not ok then return false, tostring(res) end
        if type(res) ~= "table" then return true end

        local status = tonumber(res.StatusCode or res.status_code or res.Status)
        if status and status >= 400 then
            return false, "http " .. tostring(status)
        end

        return true, res
    end

    local function httpJson(url)
        local req = syn and syn.request
            or http_request or request
            or (http and http.request)
            or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
        if not req then
            if type(game.HttpGet) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, url)
                if ok and type(res) == "string" then
                    local data
                    pcall(function() data = HttpService:JSONDecode(res) end)
                    return data, res
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local ok, res = pcall(req, {
            Url = url,
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = { Accept = "application/json" },
        })
        if not ok or type(res) ~= "table" then return nil end

        local body = res.Body or res.body
        if type(body) == "table" then return body end
        if type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then return nil end

        local data
        pcall(function() data = HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
        return data, body
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [2.3] Icon resolution for Discord thumbnails
    --=====================================================================
    local function isCdnUrl(url)
        if type(url) ~= "string" or url == "" then return false end
        url = url:gsub("^http://", "https://")
        if not url:find("^https://") then return false end
        return url:find("rbxcdn.com", 1, true) ~= nil and url or nil
    end

    local function parseAssetUrl(url)
        if type(url) ~= "string" or url == "" then return nil end
        url = url:gsub("^http://", "https://")

        local thumbType, thumbId = url:match("rbxthumb://type=([%w]+)&id=(%d+)")
        if not thumbId then
            thumbType, thumbId = url:match("rbxthumb://[^%s]*type=([%w]+)[^%s]*id=(%d+)")
        end

        if thumbId then
            local t = string.lower(tostring(thumbType or "asset"))
            if t == "avatarheadshot" or t == "avatarbust" or t == "avatar" then
                return { kind = "user", id = thumbId }
            end
            if t == "bundlethumbnail" or t == "bundle" then
                return { kind = "bundle", id = thumbId }
            end
            return { kind = "asset", id = thumbId }
        end

        local assetId = url:match("rbxassetid://(%d+)")
            or url:match("[?&]assetId=(%d+)")
            or url:match("[?&]assetid=(%d+)")
            or url:match("/asset/%?id=(%d+)")
            or url:match("[?&]id=(%d+)")

        if assetId then return { kind = "asset", id = assetId } end
    end

    local function detectImageType(body)
        if type(body) ~= "string" or #body < 12 then return nil end

        local b1, b2, b3, b4 = string.byte(body, 1, 4)
        if b1 == 137 and b2 == 80 and b3 == 78 and b4 == 71 then
            return "image/png", "png"
        end
        if b1 == 255 and b2 == 216 then
            return "image/jpeg", "jpg"
        end
        if b1 == 71 and b2 == 73 and b3 == 70 then
            return "image/gif", "gif"
        end
        if b1 == 82 and b2 == 73 and b3 == 70 and b4 == 70
            and string.sub(body, 9, 12) == "WEBP" then
            return "image/webp", "webp"
        end
    end

    local function isValidImage(body, ext)
        if type(body) ~= "string" or #body < 32 then return false end
        if ext == "png" then return body:find("IEND", 1, true) ~= nil end
        if ext == "jpg" then
            local n = #body
            return string.byte(body, n-1) == 255 and string.byte(body, n) == 217
        end
        if ext == "gif" then return #body > 64 end
        return false
    end

    local function readHeader(res, name)
        local h = res and (res.Headers or res.headers)
        if type(h) ~= "table" then return nil end
        local low = string.lower(name)
        for k, v in pairs(h) do
            if low == string.lower(tostring(k)) then return v end
        end
    end

    local function fetchImageBytes(url)
        if type(url) ~= "string" then return nil end
        url = url:gsub("^http://", "https://")
        if not url:find("^https://") then return nil end

        local function validate(body)
            local mime, ext = detectImageType(body)
            if mime and ext ~= "webp" and isValidImage(body, ext) then
                return body, mime, ext
            end
        end

        -- Try game.HttpGet for rbxcdn
        if url:find("rbxcdn.com", 1, true) and type(game.HttpGet) == "function" then
            local ok, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, url)
            if ok then
                local mime, ext = detectImageType(body)
                if mime and ext ~= "webp" and isValidImage(body, ext) then
                    return validate(body)
                end
            end
        end

        local req = syn and syn.request
            or http_request or request
            or (http and http.request)
            or (fluxus and fluxus.request)

        if not req then return nil end

        local currentUrl = url
        for _ = 1, 5 do
            local ok, res = pcall(req, {
                Url = currentUrl,
                Method = "GET",
                Headers = {
                    Accept = "image/png,image/jpeg,image/*;q=0.8,*/*;q=0.1",
                },
            })
            if not ok or type(res) ~= "table" then break end

            local body = res.Body or res.body
            local mime, ext = detectImageType(body)
            if mime and ext ~= "webp" and isValidImage(body, ext) then
                return validate(body)
            end

            local loc = readHeader(res, "Location")
            if type(loc) ~= "string" or loc == "" then break end

            if loc:find("^https?://") then
                currentUrl = loc:gsub("^http://", "https://")
            elseif loc:sub(1, 1) == "/" then
                local base = currentUrl:match("^(https://[^/]+)")
                if not base then break end
                currentUrl = base .. loc
            else
                break
            end
        end

        -- Fallback
        if type(game.HttpGet) == "function" then
            local ok, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, currentUrl)
            if ok then return validate(body) end
        end
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [2.4] Discord payloads
    --=====================================================================
    local function buildUsername()
        if Flags.HookUsername == true then
            local u = {
                name = tostring(LocalPlayer.DisplayName or LocalPlayer.Name),
                url = "https://www.roblox.com/users/"
                    .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId) .. "/profile",
            }
            if not userIconCache[LocalPlayer.UserId] then
                local data = httpJson(
                    "https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/users/avatar-headshot?userIds="
                    .. tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId)
                    .. "&size=150x150&format=Png&isCircular=false")
                local entry = data and data.data and data.data[1]
                if type(entry) == "table" then
                    local state = string.lower(tostring(entry.state or entry.State or ""))
                    if state == "" or state == "completed" then
                        local url = entry.imageUrl or entry.imageurl or entry.ImageUrl
                        if type(url) == "string" and url ~= "" then
                            userIconCache[LocalPlayer.UserId] =
                                url:gsub("^http://", "https://")
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if userIconCache[LocalPlayer.UserId] then
                u.icon_url = userIconCache[LocalPlayer.UserId]
            end
            return u
        end
        return { name = "Kira Hub" }
    end

    local function withPing(payload)
        local mode = Flags.HookPing
        local ping
        if mode == "Here" then ping = "@here"
        elseif mode == "User id" then
            local uid = tostring(Flags.HookUserId or "")
            if uid ~= "" and uid ~= "0" then
                ping = "<@" .. uid .. ">"
            end
        end
        if ping then payload.content = ping end
        return payload
    end

    local function resolveIcon(cfg, cat, name)
        -- Try direct
        local direct = extractCfgIcon(cfg)
        if direct then return direct end

        -- Try icon map
        if ESP and ESP.icon then
            local ok, v = pcall(ESP.icon, cfg, cat)
            if ok and v then return v end
        end

        -- Try Directory
        if type(cat) == "string" and type(AssetDirectory) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(AssetDirectory) do
                local kLower = string.lower(tostring(k))
                if kLower == string.lower(cat) then
                    local found = extractCfgIcon(v)
                    if found then return found end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function sendEmbed(payload)
        if Flags.HookEnabled ~= true then return false, "off" end
        local url = Flags.HookUrl
        if type(url) ~= "string" or not url:find("^https://") then
            return false, "no url"
        end

        local req = syn and syn.request
            or http_request or request
            or (http and http.request)
            or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
        if not req then return false, "no http" end

        local icon = resolveIcon(payload.cfg, payload.cat, payload.title)
        local title = tostring(payload.title or "Kira")
        if #title > 256 then title = string.sub(title, 1, 253) .. "..." end

        local embed = {
            author = buildUsername(),
            title = title,
            color = tonumber(payload.color) or 11393254,
            timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
            description = payload.description and tostring(payload.description) or "",
            fields = payload.fields,
            footer = { text = "Kira Hub · " .. (
                (type(META.Discord) == "string" and META.Discord:match("%S"))
                and (META.Discord:find("discord%.", 1)
                    or META.Discord:find("http", 1, true))
                    and META.Discord:gsub("^https://", "")
                or "Kira Hub"
            ) },
        }

        if embed.description == "" then embed.description = nil end

        -- Try CDN URL first (no upload)
        local cdnUrl = isCdnUrl(icon)
        if cdnUrl then
            embed.thumbnail = { url = cdnUrl }
            local body = HttpService:JSONEncode(withPing({
                username = "Kira Hub",
                embeds = { embed },
            }))

            local ok, err = httpRequest(url, "POST",
                { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }, body)
            return ok, err
        end

        -- Try uploading as attachment
        if icon then
            local parsed = parseAssetUrl(icon)
            if parsed then
                local urls
                if parsed.kind == "asset" then
                    urls = {
                        "https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/assets?assetIds="
                            .. parsed.id .. "&size=150x150&format=Png&isCircular=false",
                    }
                elseif parsed.kind == "user" then
                    urls = {
                        "https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/users/avatar-headshot?userIds="
                            .. parsed.id .. "&size=150x150&format=Png&isCircular=false",
                    }
                elseif parsed.kind == "bundle" then
                    urls = {
                        "https://thumbnails.roblox.com/v1/bundles/thumbnails?bundleIds="
                            .. parsed.id .. "&size=150x150&format=Png&isCircular=false",
                    }
                end

                if urls then
                    for _, u in ipairs(urls) do
                        local data = httpJson(u)
                        local entry = data and data.data and data.data[1]
                        if type(entry) == "table" then
                            local img = entry.imageUrl or entry.imageurl or entry.ImageUrl
                            img = img and img:gsub("^http://", "https://")
                            local bytes, mime, ext = fetchImageBytes(img)
                            if bytes and ext then
                                local boundary = "----Kira"
                                    .. tostring(math.floor(os.clock() * 1e6))
                                local body = "--" .. boundary .. "\r\n"
                                    .. "Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"payload_json\"\r\n\r\n"
                                    .. HttpService:JSONEncode(withPing({
                                        username = "Kira Hub",
                                        embeds = { setmetatable(
                                            { thumbnail = { url = "attachment://icon." .. ext } },
                                            { __index = embed }
                                        ) },
                                    }))
                                    .. "\r\n--" .. boundary .. "\r\n"
                                    .. "Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"files[0]\"; filename=\"icon."
                                    .. ext .. "\"\r\n"
                                    .. "Content-Type: " .. mime .. "\r\n"
                                    .. "Content-Transfer-Encoding: binary\r\n\r\n"
                                    .. bytes .. "\r\n--" .. boundary .. "--\r\n"

                                local sendUrl = url
                                if not sendUrl:find("wait=", 1, true) then
                                    sendUrl = sendUrl
                                        .. (sendUrl:find("?", 1, true) and "&" or "?")
                                        .. "wait=true"
                                end

                                local ok = pcall(req, {
                                    Url = sendUrl,
                                    Method = "POST",
                                    Headers = {
                                        ["Content-Type"] = "multipart/form-data; boundary=" .. boundary,
                                    },
                                    Body = body,
                                })
                                if ok then return true end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- Fallback: no icon
        embed.thumbnail = nil
        local body = HttpService:JSONEncode(withPing({
            username = "Kira Hub",
            embeds = { embed },
        }))
        return httpRequest(url, "POST",
            { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" }, body)
    end

    --=====================================================================
    --  [2.5] Event senders
    --=====================================================================
    local function sendStolen(snapshot)
        if Flags.HookStolen ~= true then return end

        local data = snapshot or (STEAL_STATE and
            (STEAL_STATE.hookSnap or STEAL_STATE.target))
        if not data then return end

        local cfg = data.cfg
        if type(cfg) ~= "table" and data.rec then
            local cat = data.rec.AssetCategory or data.rec.Category
            cfg = AssetDirectory and cat and AssetDirectory[cat]
        end

        local name = data.name
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == ""
            or name == "egg" or name == "?" then
            name = cfg and (cfg.DisplayName
                or (type(cfg.Egg) == "table" and cfg.Egg.DisplayName))
                or data.cat or "egg"
        end

        local earn = ratePerSecond(data.rec, cfg)
        if (not earn or earn == 0) and tonumber(data.earn) then
            earn = data.earn
        end

        local rar = P1.rarityName(cfg)
        if rar == "?" and data.rar then rar = tostring(data.rar) end

        local minGen = numberFromString(Flags.HookMinGen)
        if minGen > 0 and minGen > (earn or 0) then return end
        if not rarityPass(rar) then return end

        local fields = {}
        fields[#fields+1] = {
            name = "Earns",
            value = "**" .. formatMoney(earn) .. "/s**",
            inline = true,
        }
        if rar and rar ~= "?" then
            fields[#fields+1] = {
                name = "Rarity",
                value = tostring(rar),
                inline = true,
            }
        end
        if data.area and data.area ~= "" then
            fields[#fields+1] = {
                name = "Area",
                value = tostring(data.area),
                inline = true,
            }
        end
        fields[#fields+1] = {
            name = "This session",
            value = tostring(STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.banked or 0)
                .. " stolen · "
                .. tostring(STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.lost or 0)
                .. " lost",
        }

        sendEmbed({
            title = tostring(name),
            color = RARITY_COLORS[string.lower(tostring(rar or ""))] or 5814783,
            thumbnail = data.icon,
            cfg = cfg,
            cat = data.cat or (data.rec and data.rec.AssetCategory),
            fields = fields,
        })
    end

    local function sendHatched(info)
        if Flags.HookHatched ~= true then return end
        if type(info) ~= "table" then return end

        local name = info.name or "pet"
        local earn = ratePerSecond(info.rec, info.cfg)
        if earn == 0 and tonumber(info.earn) then earn = info.earn end

        local rar = info.rar or P1.rarityName(info.cfg)

        local minGen = numberFromString(Flags.HookMinGen)
        if minGen > 0 and minGen > (earn or 0) then return end
        if not rarityPass(rar) then return end

        local fields = {
            { name = "Earns", value = "**" .. formatMoney(earn) .. "/s**", inline = true },
        }
        if rar and rar ~= "?" then
            fields[#fields+1] = { name = "Rarity", value = tostring(rar), inline = true }
        end

        sendEmbed({
            title = tostring(name),
            color = RARITY_COLORS[string.lower(tostring(rar or ""))] or 3908956,
            thumbnail = extractCfgIcon(info.cfg),
            cfg = info.cfg,
            cat = info.cat,
            fields = fields,
        })
    end

    local function sendSold(info)
        if Flags.HookSold ~= true then return end
        if type(info) ~= "table" then return end

        local kind = info.kind ~= "egg" and "Sold a pet" or "Sold an egg"
        local name = info.name
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then
            name = info.cfg and info.cfg.DisplayName or (info.cat or "?")
        end

        local earn = ratePerSecond(info.rec, info.cfg)
        if earn == 0 and tonumber(info.earn) then earn = info.earn end

        local fields = {
            { name = "Earns", value = "**" .. formatMoney(earn) .. "/s**", inline = true },
        }

        sendEmbed({
            title = tostring(name),
            color = RARITY_COLORS[string.lower(tostring(info.rar or ""))] or 15105570,
            thumbnail = extractCfgIcon(info.cfg),
            cfg = info.cfg,
            cat = info.cat,
            fields = fields,
            kind = kind,
        })
    end

    local function sendRewards(count)
        if Flags.HookRewards ~= true then return end

        sendEmbed({
            title = "Index rewards",
            description = "Claimed **" .. tostring(count) .. "** "
                .. (tonumber(count) ~= 1 and "entries" or "entry"),
            color = 3447003,
        })
    end

    local function sendDigest()
        if Flags.HookEnabled ~= true or Flags.SessionDigest ~= true then return end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastDigest < 600 then return end
        lastDigest = now

        local plotStats = PLOT and PLOT.stats and PLOT.stats() or {}
        local soldCount = (tonumber(plotStats.soldPets) or 0)
            + (tonumber(plotStats.soldEggs) or 0)

        sendEmbed({
            title = formatSession() .. " running",
            description = "Totals since this execute — not just the last 10 minutes.",
            color = 9807270,
            fields = {
                { name = "Stolen", value = tostring(STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.banked or 0), inline = true },
                { name = "Lost", value = tostring(STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.lost or 0), inline = true },
                { name = "Re-grabs", value = tostring(STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.regrabs or 0), inline = true },
                { name = "Hatched", value = tostring(plotStats.hatched or 0), inline = true },
                { name = "Sold", value = tostring(soldCount) .. " items", inline = true },
                { name = "Index claims", value = tostring(plotStats.claimIndex or 0), inline = true },
                { name = "Session", value = formatSession(), inline = true },
            },
        })
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while engine.loaded do
            task.wait(30)
            pcall(sendDigest)
        end
    end)

    --=====================================================================
    --  [2.6] Public API
    --=====================================================================
    return {
        send = function(payload)
            if type(payload) ~= "table" then return false, "bad embed" end
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, err = sendEmbed(payload)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[Kira/hook] send fail:", tostring(err),
                        tostring(payload.title))
                end
            end)
            return true
        end,
        test = function()
            Flags.HookEnabled = true
            if Widgets.HookEnabled and Widgets.HookEnabled.set then
                pcall(Widgets.HookEnabled.set, true)
            end

            local sessionText = formatSession()
            return sendEmbed({
                title = "Connected",
                description = "Stolen and hatched eggs will post as embeds with the pet icon, $/s, and rarity.",
                color = 11393254,
                fields = {
                    { name = "Player", value = tostring(LocalPlayer.DisplayName or LocalPlayer.Name), inline = true },
                    { name = "Game", value = tostring(META.Game or "Roblox"), inline = true },
                    { name = "Session", value = sessionText, inline = true },
                },
            })
        end,
        stolen = sendStolen,
        hatched = sendHatched,
        sold = sendSold,
        rewards = sendRewards,
    }
end)()

P1.webhook = WEBHOOK

--=====================================================================
--  [3] Widget wire for hop + webhook
--=====================================================================
local function wire(flag, fn)
    local w = Widgets[flag]
    if w then w.on = fn
    else warn("[Kira/wire/final] missing:", flag) end
end

wire("AutoHop", function() end)
wire("HopNow", function()
    if HOP and HOP.now then pcall(HOP.now) end
end)

wire("HookTest", function()
    local w = Widgets.HookTest
    if not w or not w.status then return end

    if not WEBHOOK or not WEBHOOK.test then
        w.status.Text = "missing"
        return
    end

    w.status.Text = "sending…"

    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, err = WEBHOOK.test()
        if w and w.status then
            w.status.Text = ok and "sent" or ("fail · " .. tostring(err))
        end
        warn("[Kira/hook]", ok and "test ok" or "test fail", tostring(err))
    end)
end)

--=====================================================================
--  [4] Keybind: flight toggle (top-level)
--=====================================================================
trackConn(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed then return end
    if Flags.FlightBind and input.KeyCode == Flags.FlightBind then
        local newState = not Flags.Flight
        if Widgets.Flight and Widgets.Flight.set then
            Widgets.Flight.set(newState)
        else
            Flags.Flight = newState
        end

        if Widgets.Flight and Widgets.Flight.on then
            Widgets.Flight.on(newState)
        else
            if P1.setEngineOn then
                P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
            end
        end

        if not P1.applyingConfig then
            P1.scheduleConfigSave()
        end
    end
end))

--=====================================================================
--  [5] ProximityPrompt bus
--=====================================================================
do
    pcall(function()
        P1.findCarryPrompt()
        for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                P1.preparePrompt(d)
            end
        end
    end)

    trackConn(workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            task.defer(P1.preparePrompt, d)
        end
    end))

    trackConn(game:GetService("ProximityPromptService").PromptButtonHoldBegan:Connect(function(prompt)
        P1.preparePrompt(prompt)

        if P1.promptLooksLikeSteal(prompt)
            and STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running
            and Flags.AutoSteal then
            if (STEAL_STATE.state == "GoEgg" or STEAL_STATE.state == "Grab")
                and STEAL_STATE.promptIsTarget
                and STEAL_STATE.promptIsTarget(prompt, STEAL_STATE.target, 5) then
                P1.firePromptNow(prompt)
            end
            return
        end

        if fireproximityprompt then
            pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt, 0)
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            prompt:InputHoldBegin()
            prompt:InputHoldEnd()
        end)
    end))

    pcall(function()
        trackConn(game:GetService("ProximityPromptService").PromptShown:Connect(function(prompt)
            P1.lastShownPrompt = prompt
            P1.preparePrompt(prompt)

            if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.running and Flags.AutoSteal
                and P1.promptLooksLikeSteal(prompt)
                and STEAL_STATE.state == "Grab"
                and STEAL_STATE.promptIsTarget
                and STEAL_STATE.promptIsTarget(prompt, STEAL_STATE.target, 5) then
                P1.firePromptNow(prompt)
            end
        end))
    end)

    pcall(function()
        trackConn(game:GetService("ProximityPromptService").PromptHidden:Connect(function(prompt)
            if prompt == P1.lastShownPrompt then
                P1.lastShownPrompt = nil
            end
        end))
    end)

    warn("[Kira/prompts] instant HoldDuration=0 armed")
end

if P1.setEngineOn then
    P1.setEngineOn(P1.engineShouldRun and P1.engineShouldRun())
end

pcall(function()
    if P1.autoLoad then P1.autoLoad(true) end
end)

if not P1.phoneLayoutLocked then
    P1.isMobile = Flags.PhoneUI == true
end
if P1.relayout then

end

    STEAL_STATE.bat.setLive(Flags.BatAura == true)
end

if P1.plot and P1.plot.sync then
    pcall(P1.plot.sync)
end

if P1.refreshModules then
    local ok, err = pcall(P1.refreshModules)
    if not ok then
        warn("[Kira/modules] sync fail:", tostring(err))
    end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while engine.loaded do
        task.wait(20)
        if engine.loaded and P1.refreshModules then
            pcall(P1.refreshModules)
        end
    end
end)

--=====================================================================
--  [11] Reset landing after engine turned off
--=====================================================================
if not P1.isFlying() then
    internals.cleanupSupport()
    local humanoid = select(1, getChar())
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function() humanoid.PlatformStand = false end)
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [12] FPS cap + optimizer
--=====================================================================
if ESP and ESP.fps then ESP.fps() end
if Flags.Optimizer and ESP and ESP.opt then ESP.opt(true) end

--=====================================================================
--  [13] Save original unload, define final shutdown
--=====================================================================
local originalUnload = P1.Destroy or function() end

local function shutdown()
    engine.loaded = false

    -- 1. Stop steal
    pcall(function()
        if P1.stopSteal then P1.stopSteal() end
    end)

    -- 2. Restore hazards + belt
    pcall(function()
        if STEAL_STATE then
            if STEAL_STATE.muteHazards then STEAL_STATE.muteHazards(false) end
            if STEAL_STATE.muteBelt then STEAL_STATE.muteBelt(false) end
        end
    end)

    -- 3. Bat aura off
    pcall(function()
        if STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.bat and STEAL_STATE.bat.stop then
            STEAL_STATE.bat.stop()
        end
    end)

    -- 4. Hop off
    pcall(function()
        if HOP and HOP.stop then HOP.stop() end
    end)

    -- 5. Plot off
    pcall(function()
        if P1.plot and P1.plot.stop then P1.plot.stop() end
    end)

    -- 6. Engine off
    pcall(function()
        if P1.setEngineOn then P1.setEngineOn(false) end
        if internals and internals.disconnectAll then internals.disconnectAll() end
    end)

    -- 7. Anti-mob cleanup
    pcall(function()
        local antiMob = internals and internals.antiMob
        if antiMob then
            for _, k in ipairs({ "attrConn", "stConn", "hpConn" }) do
                local c = antiMob[k]
                antiMob[k] = nil
                if c then pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 8. Disconnect all tracked connections
    pcall(function()
        if internals and internals.disconnectAll then internals.disconnectAll() end
    end)

    -- 9. Clear ESP
    pcall(function()
        if ESP and ESP.clear then ESP.clear() end
    end)

    -- 10. Destroy world guis
    pcall(function()
        local parent = ScreenGui and ScreenGui.Parent
        if parent then
            local wg = parent:FindFirstChild(WORLD_NAME)
            if wg then wg:Destroy() end
        end
        local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            local wg = pg:FindFirstChild(WORLD_NAME)
            if wg then wg:Destroy() end
            local legacy = pg:FindFirstChild("KiraWorldGui")
            if legacy then legacy:Destroy() end
        end
        if gethui then
            local ok, hui = pcall(gethui)
            if ok and hui then
                local wg = hui:FindFirstChild(WORLD_NAME)
                if wg then wg:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- 11. Ghost support part
    pcall(function()
        if internals and internals.cleanupSupport then
            internals.cleanupSupport()
        end
    end)

    -- 12. Optimizer off
    pcall(function()
        if ESP and ESP.opt then ESP.opt(false) end
    end)

    -- 13. Save config
    pcall(function()
        if P1.saveConfig then P1.saveConfig(true) end
    end)

    -- 14. Clean global state
    pcall(function()
        local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
        local slot = P1.getSlot and P1.getSlot()
        if slot then
            slot.unload = nil
            slot.alive = false
            slot.dump = nil
        end

        if type(env.KiraUI) == "table" then
            env.KiraUI[P1.UserIdStr] = nil
        end
        if type(env.KiraDumpByUser) == "table" then
            env.KiraDumpByUser[P1.UserIdStr] = nil
        end
        if env.UI == P1.UI then env.UI = nil end

        if env.KiraUnloadUid == P1.UserIdStr then
            env.KiraUnloadUid = nil
            env.KiraUnload = nil
        end
    end)

    -- 15. Original cleanup (screen gui)
    pcall(originalUnload)
end

P1.Destroy = shutdown

--=====================================================================
--  [14] Public interface object
--=====================================================================
local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
local slot = P1.getSlot()

local UI_API = {
    Flags = Flags,
    Widgets = Widgets,
    SetPage = function(name)
        if P1.setPage then pcall(P1.setPage, name) end
    end,
    SetVisible = function(on)
        if P1.setVisible then pcall(P1.setVisible, on) end
    end,
    SetTheme = function(name)
        if P1.setTheme then pcall(P1.setTheme, name) end
    end,
    Destroy = shutdown,
    SetStatus = function(flag, text)
        local w = Widgets[flag]
        if w and w.status then w.status.Text = text end
    end,
    On = function(flag, fn)
        local w = Widgets[flag]
        if w then w.on = fn end
    end,
}

slot.UI = UI_API
env.KiraUI = type(env.KiraUI) == "table" and env.KiraUI or {}
env.KiraUI[P1.UserIdStr] = UI_API

-- Global UI (first instance only)
local hasOther = (function()
    local KiraHub = env.KiraHub
    local slots = type(KiraHub) == "table" and KiraHub.slots
    if type(slots) ~= "table" then return false end
    for k, v in pairs(slots) do
        if tostring(k) ~= P1.UserIdStr and type(v) == "table"
            and v.alive == true then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end)()

if type(env.UI) ~= "table" or not hasOther then
    env.UI = UI_API
end

-- Attach slot alive + unload
slot.alive = true
slot.unload = shutdown

env.KiraUnloadUid = P1.UserIdStr
env.KiraUnload = function()
    local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local uid = tostring(lp and lp.UserId or 0)
    local hub = env.KiraHub
    local s = type(hub) == "table" and type(hub.slots) == "table"
        and hub.slots[uid]
    if type(s) == "table" and type(s.unload) == "function" then
        s.unload()
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [15] KiraDump — for console diagnostics
--=====================================================================
local function dumpState()
    local _, root = getChar()
    local target = STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.target

    local nightWindow = workspace:GetAttribute("AreaEggCycleNightSeconds")
    if type(nightWindow) ~= "number" then nightWindow = 10 end

    return {
        state = STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.state,
        travel = Flags.StealTravel,
        stealSpeed = Flags.StealSpeed,
        bypass = Flags.BypassSpeed,
        cap = Flags.BypassCap,
        engine = engine.on,
        carrying = STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.carrying,
        untrapArmed = engine.untrapArmed,
        untrapCount = engine.untrapCount,
        stillFor = STEAL_STATE and STEAL_STATE.stillFor,
        target = target and ((target.area or "") .. " " .. (target.name or "")) or nil,
        pos = root and {
            math.floor(root.Position.X),
            math.floor(root.Position.Y),
            math.floor(root.Position.Z),
        },
        wraps = engine.wraps and next(engine.wraps) ~= nil,
        areas = Flags.Areas,
        eggTypes = Flags.Rarities,
        stealMode = Flags.StealMode,
        eggEsp = Flags.EggESP,
        plotEsp = Flags.PlotESP,
        night = P1.secondsToNight() <= math.clamp(nightWindow, 1, 300),
    }
end

P1.dump = dumpState

local dumpRegistry = env.KiraDumpByUser
dumpRegistry = type(dumpRegistry) == "table" and dumpRegistry or {}
env.KiraDumpByUser = dumpRegistry
dumpRegistry[P1.UserIdStr] = dumpState

if slot then slot.dump = dumpState end

env.KiraDump = function(...)
    local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local uid = tostring(lp and lp.UserId or 0)
    local fn = env.KiraDumpByUser and env.KiraDumpByUser[uid]
    if type(fn) == "function" then
        return fn(...)
    end
end

--=====================================================================
--  [16] Boot message
--=====================================================================
print("[UI] Kira Hub ready · RightShift toggles · Dark / Light in Settings")
warn("[Kira/boot] game logic attached · KiraDump() in F9 console")
